//! Minimal character device registry and default /dev nodes.
use crate::errno;
pub const NULL_MAJOR:u32=1; pub const NULL_MINOR:u32=3;
pub const ZERO_MAJOR:u32=1; pub const ZERO_MINOR:u32=5;
pub const FULL_MAJOR:u32=1; pub const FULL_MINOR:u32=7;
pub const RANDOM_MAJOR:u32=1; pub const RANDOM_MINOR:u32=8;
pub const URANDOM_MAJOR:u32=1; pub const URANDOM_MINOR:u32=9;
pub const TTY_MAJOR:u32=5; pub const TTY_MINOR:u32=0;
pub const CONSOLE_MAJOR:u32=5; pub const CONSOLE_MINOR:u32=1;

pub fn read(major:u32,minor:u32,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 match (major,minor){
  (NULL_MAJOR,NULL_MINOR)=>Ok(0),
  (ZERO_MAJOR,ZERO_MINOR)=>{for b in dst.iter_mut(){*b=0;}Ok(dst.len())},
  (FULL_MAJOR,FULL_MINOR)=>Err(errno::ENOSPC),
  (RANDOM_MAJOR,RANDOM_MINOR)|(URANDOM_MAJOR,URANDOM_MINOR)=>{crate::time::fill_random(dst);Ok(dst.len())},
  (TTY_MAJOR,TTY_MINOR)|(CONSOLE_MAJOR,CONSOLE_MINOR)=>Ok(0),
  _=>Err(errno::ENODEV),
 }
}
pub fn write(major:u32,minor:u32,src:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 match (major,minor){
  (NULL_MAJOR,NULL_MINOR)=>Ok(src.len()),
  (ZERO_MAJOR,ZERO_MINOR)|(RANDOM_MAJOR,RANDOM_MINOR)|(URANDOM_MAJOR,URANDOM_MINOR)=>Ok(src.len()),
  (FULL_MAJOR,FULL_MINOR)=>Err(errno::ENOSPC),
  (TTY_MAJOR,TTY_MINOR)|(CONSOLE_MAJOR,CONSOLE_MINOR)=>{crate::console::write_bytes(src);Ok(src.len())},
  _=>Err(errno::ENODEV),
 }
}
pub fn ioctl(major:u32,minor:u32,cmd:u64,arg:u64)->i64{
 match (major,minor){
  (254,_)=>{
   match cmd{
    0x80081272=>{ // BLKGETSIZE64
     if arg==0{return errno::neg(errno::EFAULT)}
     let size=crate::driver::virtio_blk::capacity().saturating_mul(512);
     if let Err(e)=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(arg as usize,&size.to_ne_bytes()){return errno::neg(e)}
     0
    }
    0x00001268|0x00001260=>{ // BLKSSZGET / BLKBSZGET
     if arg==0{return errno::neg(errno::EFAULT)}
     let v=512i32;
     if let Err(e)=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(arg as usize,&v.to_ne_bytes()){return errno::neg(e)}
     0
    }
    _=>0,
   }
  }
  (5,_)=>{
   match cmd{
    0x5401=>{if arg!=0{let zeros=[0u8;36];let _=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(arg as usize,&zeros);}0}
    0x5413=>{if arg!=0{let zeros=[0u8;8];let _=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(arg as usize,&zeros);}0}
    _=>0,
   }
  }
  _=>0,
 }
}
pub fn init_devtmpfs(){
 let _=crate::vfs::mkdir("/dev",0o755);
 let nodes=[
  ("/dev/null",0o20666,NULL_MAJOR,NULL_MINOR),
  ("/dev/zero",0o20666,ZERO_MAJOR,ZERO_MINOR),
  ("/dev/full",0o20666,FULL_MAJOR,FULL_MINOR),
  ("/dev/random",0o20666,RANDOM_MAJOR,RANDOM_MINOR),
  ("/dev/urandom",0o20666,URANDOM_MAJOR,URANDOM_MINOR),
  ("/dev/tty",0o20666,TTY_MAJOR,TTY_MINOR),
  ("/dev/console",0o20666,CONSOLE_MAJOR,CONSOLE_MINOR),
 ];
 for (p,mode,maj,min) in nodes{
  let dev=((maj as u64)<<8)|(min as u64);
  let _=crate::vfs::mknod(p,0o020000|mode,dev);
 }
 if crate::driver::virtio_blk::ready(){let _=crate::vfs::mknod("/dev/vda",0o060660,(254u64<<8)|0);}
}

pub fn blk_read(major:u32,minor:u32,off:u64,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let _=(major,minor);
 if !crate::driver::virtio_blk::ready(){return Err(errno::ENODEV)}
 let cap=crate::driver::virtio_blk::capacity()*512;
 if off>=cap{return Ok(0)}
 let mut done=0usize;
 let mut sector=off/512;
 let mut pos=off%512;
 while done<dst.len() && off as usize+done < cap as usize{
  let mut buf=[0u8;512];
  let avail=512-pos as usize;
  let n=core::cmp::min(avail,dst.len()-done);
  dst[done..done+n].copy_from_slice(&buf[pos as usize..pos as usize+n]);
  done+=n; pos=0; sector+=1;
 }
 Ok(done)
}
pub fn blk_write(major:u32,minor:u32,off:u64,src:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let _=(major,minor);
 if !crate::driver::virtio_blk::ready(){return Err(errno::ENODEV)}
 let cap=crate::driver::virtio_blk::capacity()*512;
 if off as usize+src.len() > cap as usize{return Err(errno::ENOSPC)}
 let mut done=0usize;
 let mut sector=off/512;
 let mut pos=off%512;
 while done<src.len(){
  // read-modify-write if partial
  let mut buf=[0u8;512];
  if pos!=0 || src.len()-done <512 {
   if crate::driver::virtio_blk::read_sector(sector,&mut buf).is_err(){return if done==0{Err(errno::EIO)}else{Ok(done)}}
  }
  let n=core::cmp::min(512-pos as usize,src.len()-done);
  buf[pos as usize..pos as usize+n].copy_from_slice(&src[done..done+n]);
  if crate::driver::virtio_blk::write_sector(sector,&buf).is_err(){return if done==0{Err(errno::EIO)}else{Ok(done)}}
  done+=n; pos=0; sector+=1;
 }
 Ok(done)
}

//! PCI configuration access and device discovery.
//!
//! The CAM pair at 0xcf8/0xcfc is used for configuration space; QEMU's q35
//! host bridge answers it for every bus. Discovery walks bus 0..=255, checking
//! function 0 first and only scanning further functions when the multifunction
//! bit in the header type is set.
use crate::arch::x86_64::port;

#[derive(Clone,Copy)] pub struct Bdf{pub segment:u16,pub bus:u8,pub device:u8,pub function:u8}
pub trait ConfigAccess:Sync{fn read32(&self,bdf:Bdf,offset:u16)->u32;fn write32(&self,bdf:Bdf,offset:u16,value:u32);}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)] pub struct Bar{pub base:u64,pub size:u64,pub io:bool,pub prefetchable:bool}
// Linux driver compatibility belongs in generated adapters above this native API, never in core structs.

pub const CFG_VENDOR:u16=0x00;pub const CFG_COMMAND:u16=0x04;pub const CFG_CLASS:u16=0x08;
pub const CFG_HEADER:u16=0x0c;pub const CFG_BAR0:u16=0x10;pub const CFG_CAP:u16=0x34;
pub const CMD_IO:u16=1;pub const CMD_MEM:u16=2;pub const CMD_BUS_MASTER:u16=4;

/* Configuration space via the CAM I/O ports (works on QEMU's q35 bridge). */
pub struct Cam;
fn cam_address(bdf:Bdf,offset:u16)->u32{(1<<31)|((bdf.bus as u32)<<16)|((bdf.device as u32)<<11)|((bdf.function as u32)<<8)|((offset as u32)&0xfc)}
impl ConfigAccess for Cam{
 fn read32(&self,bdf:Bdf,offset:u16)->u32{unsafe{port::outl(0xcf8,cam_address(bdf,offset));port::inl(0xcfc)}}
 fn write32(&self,bdf:Bdf,offset:u16,value:u32){unsafe{port::outl(0xcf8,cam_address(bdf,offset));port::outl(0xcfc,value)}}
}

pub struct Found{pub bdf:Bdf,pub vendor:u16,pub device:u16,pub class:u8,pub bars:[Bar;6]}
impl Found{
 pub fn enable(&self,acc:&dyn ConfigAccess){
  let base=acc.read32(self.bdf,CFG_COMMAND)as u16;
  acc.write32(self.bdf,CFG_COMMAND,(base|CMD_IO|CMD_MEM|CMD_BUS_MASTER)as u32);
 }
}
fn read_bar(acc:&dyn ConfigAccess,bdf:Bdf,index:u8)->Bar{
 let lo=acc.read32(bdf,CFG_BAR0+4*(index as u16));
 if lo&1!=0{return Bar{base:(lo&0xffff_fffc)as u64,size:0,io:true,prefetchable:false}}
 let kind=(lo>>1)&3;
 if kind==2{
  let hi=acc.read32(bdf,CFG_BAR0+4*(index as u16)+4);
  Bar{base:((hi as u64)<<32)|((lo&0xffff_fff0)as u64),size:0,io:false,prefetchable:lo&8!=0}
 }else{
  Bar{base:(lo&0xffff_fff0)as u64,size:0,io:false,prefetchable:lo&8!=0}
 }
}
fn read_bars(acc:&dyn ConfigAccess,bdf:Bdf)->[Bar;6]{
 let mut bars=[Bar{base:0,size:0,io:false,prefetchable:false};6];
 let mut i=0usize;
 while i<6{
  let bar=read_bar(acc,bdf,i as u8);
  let wide=!bar.io&&(acc.read32(bdf,CFG_BAR0+4*(i as u16))>>1)&3==2;
  bars[i]=bar;
  i+=if wide{2}else{1};
 }
 bars
}
fn probe(acc:&dyn ConfigAccess,bdf:Bdf)->Option<Found>{
 let word=acc.read32(bdf,CFG_VENDOR);
 if word&0xffff==0xffff{return None}
 let vendor=(word&0xffff)as u16;let device=(word>>16)as u16;
 let class=((acc.read32(bdf,CFG_CLASS)>>24)&0xff)as u8;
 Some(Found{bdf,vendor,device,class,bars:read_bars(acc,bdf)})
}
pub fn find(vendor:u16,device:u16)->Option<Found>{
 let acc=Cam;
 for bus in 0..=255u8{
  for dev in 0..32u8{
   let bdf=Bdf{segment:0,bus,device:dev,function:0};
   let word=acc.read32(bdf,CFG_VENDOR);
   if word&0xffff==0xffff{continue}
   let multifunction=(acc.read32(bdf,CFG_HEADER)>>16)&0x80!=0;
   let last=if multifunction{8u8}else{1u8};
   for func in 0..last{
    let bdf=Bdf{segment:0,bus,device:dev,function:func};
    if let Some(found)=probe(&acc,bdf){
     if found.vendor==vendor&&found.device==device{return Some(found)}
    }
   }
  }
 }
 None
}
/* Human-readable count for the boot log. */
pub fn count()->usize{
 let acc=Cam;let mut n=0;
 for bus in 0..=255u8{
  for dev in 0..32u8{
   let bdf=Bdf{segment:0,bus,device:dev,function:0};
   if acc.read32(bdf,CFG_VENDOR)&0xffff!=0xffff{n+=1}
  }
 }
 n
}

//! virtio-pci transport.
//!
//! The transport covers both device interfaces a Linux guest meets: the
//! legacy interface (version 0.9.5, port I/O) and the modern interface
//! (virtio 1.0, memory mapped). The modern interface is what a plain
//! `virtio-blk-pci` or `virtio-net-pci` device offers, so a driver that only
//! speaks the legacy interface cannot use a default device model.
//!
//! The modern interface describes itself through vendor specific PCI
//! capabilities: the common configuration, the notification area and the
//! device specific configuration. All of them live in the first memory
//! mapped bar.
use crate::arch::x86_64::phys::phys_to_virt;
use crate::arch::x86_64::port;
use crate::driver::pci::{self,Bdf,Cam,ConfigAccess};

pub const VIRTIO_VENDOR:u16=0x1af4;
pub const LEGACY_NET:u16=0x1000;
pub const LEGACY_BLK:u16=0x1001;
pub const MODERN_NET:u16=0x1040;
pub const MODERN_BLK:u16=0x1041;

/* The legacy port I/O register set. */
const LEGACY_HOST_FEATURES:u16=0x00;
const LEGACY_GUEST_FEATURES:u16=0x04;
const LEGACY_QUEUE_ADDR:u16=0x08;
const LEGACY_QUEUE_SIZE:u16=0x0c;
const LEGACY_QUEUE_SELECT:u16=0x0e;
const LEGACY_QUEUE_NOTIFY:u16=0x10;
const LEGACY_STATUS:u16=0x12;
const LEGACY_CONFIG:u16=0x14;

/* The device status bits. */
pub const S_ACK:u8=1;
pub const S_DRIVER:u8=2;
pub const S_DRIVER_OK:u8=4;
pub const S_FEATURES_OK:u8=8;

/* The common configuration register offsets. */
const CFG_DEVICE_FEATURE_SELECT:u64=0x00;
const CFG_DEVICE_FEATURE:u64=0x04;
const CFG_DRIVER_FEATURE_SELECT:u64=0x08;
const CFG_DRIVER_FEATURE:u64=0x0c;
const CFG_NUM_QUEUES:u64=0x12;
const CFG_STATUS:u64=0x14;
const CFG_QUEUE_SELECT:u64=0x16;
const CFG_QUEUE_SIZE:u64=0x18;
const CFG_QUEUE_ENABLE:u64=0x1c;
const CFG_QUEUE_NOTIFY_OFF:u64=0x1e;
const CFG_QUEUE_DESC:u64=0x20;
const CFG_QUEUE_DRIVER:u64=0x24;
const CFG_QUEUE_DEVICE:u64=0x28;

/* The capability identifiers and the layout of the virtio capability. */
const PCI_CAP_ID_VENDOR:u8=0x09;
const CAP_COMMON_CFG:u8=1;
const CAP_NOTIFY_CFG:u8=2;
const CAP_DEVICE_CFG:u8=3;

/* The feature bit that selects the modern interface. */
pub const VIRTIO_F_VERSION_1:u32=32;

/// One split virtqueue: the descriptor table, the available ring and the
/// used ring. The legacy interface keeps the three parts in one buffer, the
/// modern interface names three separate addresses.
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
pub struct Queue{pub desc:*mut u8,pub avail:*mut u8,pub used:*mut u8,pub size:usize}

/// The device transport.
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
pub struct Transport{
 pub modern:bool,
 /// The legacy port I/O base.
 pub io:u16,
 /// The base address of every memory mapped bar.
 pub bar_base:[u64;6],
 /// The offset of the common configuration inside its bar.
 pub common:u64,
 /// The bar that carries the common configuration.
 pub common_bar:u32,
 /// The offset of the device configuration inside its bar.
 pub device_cfg:u64,
 /// The bar that carries the device configuration.
 pub device_bar:u32,
 /// The notification multiplier, the queue offset and the bar that carries
 /// the notification area.
 pub notify_mult:u32,
 pub notify_off:u32,
 pub notify_bar:u32,
 /// The number of queues the device offers.
 pub queues:u16,
 /// The physical address of the notification area.
 pub notify_base:u64,
}

impl Transport{
 /// The transport of a device that speaks the legacy port I/O interface.
 pub const fn legacy(io:u16)->Self{Self{modern:false,io,bar_base:[0;6],common:0,common_bar:0,device_cfg:0,device_bar:0,notify_mult:0,notify_off:0,notify_bar:0,queues:0,notify_base:0}}
}

/// Reads a capability sized byte range from the configuration space.
fn cfg_read(bdf:Bdf,offset:u16)->Option<[u8;16]>{
 let acc=Cam;
 let mut out=[0u8;16];
 let mut at=offset as usize;
 while at<offset as usize+16{
  let word=acc.read32(bdf,(at as u16)&!3);
  let shift=(at&3)*8;
  let bytes=word.to_le_bytes();
  let take=core::cmp::min(4-(at&3),16-(at-offset as usize));
  out[at-offset as usize..at-offset as usize+take].copy_from_slice(&bytes[shift..shift+take]);
  at+=take;
 }
 Some(out)
}

/// Finds the virtio capabilities of a device and returns the offsets of the
/// common configuration, the device configuration, the notification area
/// multiplier, the notification area offset and the bars that carry them.
///
/// The common and the device configuration capability share one layout: the
/// capability type, the bar and the offset of the structure inside that bar.
/// The notification capability has a different one: the offset multiplier,
/// the offset of the notification area inside its bar and the bar number.
fn modern_caps(bdf:Bdf)->Option<(u64,u64,u32,u32,u32,u32,u32)>{
 let acc=Cam;
 let mut ptr=(acc.read32(bdf,pci::CFG_CAP)&0xff)as u16;
 let mut common=0u64;let mut device=0u64;let mut mult=0u32;let mut noff=0u32;let mut nbar=0u32;
 let mut cbar=0u32;let mut dbar=0u32;
 let mut found=0u32;
 while ptr>=0x40&&ptr<0xfc{
  let cap=cfg_read(bdf,ptr)?;
  let id=cap[0];let next=cap[1];let len=cap[2]as usize;
  if id==PCI_CAP_ID_VENDOR&&len>=16{
   let cfg_type=cap[3];
   match cfg_type{
    CAP_COMMON_CFG=>{
     cbar=cap[4]as u32;
     common=u32::from_le_bytes([cap[8],cap[9],cap[10],cap[11]])as u64;
     found|=1;
    }
    CAP_DEVICE_CFG=>{
     dbar=cap[4]as u32;
     device=u32::from_le_bytes([cap[8],cap[9],cap[10],cap[11]])as u64;
     found|=2;
    }
    CAP_NOTIFY_CFG=>{
     mult=cap[3]as u32;
     noff=u32::from_le_bytes([cap[4],cap[5],cap[6],cap[7]]);
     nbar=u32::from_le_bytes([cap[8],cap[9],cap[10],cap[11]]);
     found|=4;
    }
    _=>{}
   }
  }
  if next==0||u16::from(next)==ptr{break}
  ptr=u16::from(next);
 }
 if found&7==7{Some((common,device,mult,noff,nbar,cbar,dbar))}else{None}
}

/// Finds a virtio device of the given identifier and prepares the transport.
pub fn probe(device:u16)->Option<Transport>{
 let found=pci::find(VIRTIO_VENDOR,device)?;
 found.enable(&Cam);
 let bar0=found.bars[0];
 if device>=MODERN_NET{
  if bar0.io{return None}
  let (common,dev_cfg,mult,noff,nbar,cbar,dbar)=modern_caps(found.bdf)?;
  let mut bar_base=[0u64;6];
  for i in 0..6{bar_base[i]=found.bars[i].base}
  let mmio=bar0.base;
  let notify_base=if nbar<6{bar_base[nbar as usize]}else{mmio};
  let mut t=Transport{modern:true,io:0,bar_base,common,common_bar:cbar,device_cfg:dev_cfg,device_bar:dbar,notify_mult:mult,notify_off:noff,notify_bar:nbar,queues:0,notify_base};
  t.queues=unsafe{core::ptr::read_volatile(t.common_ptr(CFG_NUM_QUEUES)as*const u16)};
  Some(t)
 }else{
  if !bar0.io{return None}
  Some(Transport::legacy(bar0.base as u16))
 }
}

impl Transport{
 #[inline]fn bar_base(&self,bar:u32)->u64{if (bar as usize)<6{self.bar_base[bar as usize]}else{self.bar_base[0]}}
 #[inline]fn common_ptr(&self,off:u64)->*mut u8{unsafe{(phys_to_virt(self.bar_base(self.common_bar)+self.common+off)as*mut u8)}}
 #[inline]fn device_ptr(&self,off:u64)->*mut u8{unsafe{(phys_to_virt(self.bar_base(self.device_bar)+self.device_cfg+off)as*mut u8)}}
 /// Reads the device status.
 pub fn status(&self)->u8{if self.modern{unsafe{core::ptr::read_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_STATUS))}}else{unsafe{port::inb(self.io+LEGACY_STATUS)}}}
 /// Writes the device status.
 pub fn set_status(&self,value:u8){if self.modern{unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_STATUS),value)}}else{unsafe{port::outb(self.io+LEGACY_STATUS,value)}}}
 /// Reads one 32-bit feature word.
 pub fn read_features(&self,select:u32)->u32{
  if !self.modern{return unsafe{port::inl(self.io+LEGACY_HOST_FEATURES)};}
  unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_DEVICE_FEATURE_SELECT)as*mut u16,select as u16);
   core::ptr::read_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_DEVICE_FEATURE)as*const u32)}
 }
 /// Writes one 32-bit feature word.
 pub fn set_features(&self,select:u32,value:u32){
  if !self.modern{unsafe{port::outl(self.io+LEGACY_GUEST_FEATURES,value);}return;}
  unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_DRIVER_FEATURE_SELECT)as*mut u16,select as u16);
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_DRIVER_FEATURE)as*mut u32,value);}
 }
 /// Negotiates the modern interface: the driver asks for the version 1
 /// feature and the device confirms the result.
 ///
 /// The driver accepts the version 1 feature and nothing else. Accepting a
 /// feature the driver does not implement would let the device use a path
 /// that is not served here: a merged receive buffer, a flush, a discard or
 /// a secure erase. The driver only implements the plain interface, so it
 /// must not acknowledge those.
 pub fn negotiate_modern(&self)->bool{
  let high=self.read_features(1);
  if high&(1<<(VIRTIO_F_VERSION_1-32))==0{return false}
  self.set_features(0,0);
  self.set_features(1,1<<(VIRTIO_F_VERSION_1-32));
  self.set_status(self.status()|S_FEATURES_OK);
  (self.status()&S_FEATURES_OK)!=0
 }
 /// Selects a queue and returns its size.
 pub fn queue_select(&self,index:u16)->usize{
  if !self.modern{unsafe{port::outw(self.io+LEGACY_QUEUE_SELECT,index);return port::inw(self.io+LEGACY_QUEUE_SIZE)as usize}}
  unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SELECT)as*mut u16,index);core::ptr::read_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SIZE)as*const u16)as usize}
 }
 /// Chooses the size of a queue and returns the size the device will use.
 /// The legacy device dictates the size, the modern one lets the driver
 /// choose but may reduce it, so the real size is always read back.
 pub fn queue_size(&self,index:u16,desired:usize)->usize{
  if !self.modern{return self.queue_select(index)}
  unsafe{
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SELECT)as*mut u16,index);
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SIZE)as*mut u16,desired as u16);
   let size=core::ptr::read_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SIZE)as*const u16)as usize;
   if size==0{desired}else{size}
  }
 }
 /// Enables a queue.
 pub fn queue_enable(&self,index:u16){if !self.modern{return}unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SELECT)as*mut u16,index);core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_ENABLE)as*mut u16,1u16);}}
 /// Installs the three ring parts of a queue.
 pub fn queue_setup(&self,index:u16,q:&Queue){
  if !self.modern{
   unsafe{port::outw(self.io+LEGACY_QUEUE_SELECT,index);
    port::outl(self.io+LEGACY_QUEUE_ADDR,(crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys(q.desc as u64)>>12)as u32);}
   return;
  }
  unsafe{
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_SELECT)as*mut u16,index);
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_DESC)as*mut u64,crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys(q.desc as u64));
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_DRIVER)as*mut u64,crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys(q.avail as u64));
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_DEVICE)as*mut u64,crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys(q.used as u64));
   core::ptr::write_volatile(self.common_ptr(CFG_QUEUE_ENABLE)as*mut u16,1u16);
  }
 }
 /// Tells the device that a queue has work.
 pub fn notify(&self,index:u16){
  if !self.modern{unsafe{port::outw(self.io+LEGACY_QUEUE_NOTIFY,index);}return}
  /* The notification area lives in its own bar. The register of a queue
     sits at the area base plus the offset of the area plus the offset
     multiplier times the queue index. The value written is the queue
     index itself. */
  let addr=self.notify_base+(self.notify_off as u64)+(self.notify_mult as u64)*(index as u64);
  unsafe{core::ptr::write_volatile(phys_to_virt(addr)as*mut u16,index);}
 }
 /// Reads a device specific configuration value.
 pub fn device_config(&self,offset:u64)->u64{
  if !self.modern{return unsafe{port::inl(self.io+LEGACY_CONFIG as u16+(offset as u16))}as u64}
  unsafe{core::ptr::read_volatile(self.device_ptr(offset))as u64}
 }
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

//! virtio-block driver.
//!
//! The driver speaks both virtio interfaces: the legacy port I/O device
//! (version 0.9.5) and the modern memory mapped device (virtio 1.0), so a
//! plain `virtio-blk-pci` device model works without a compatibility switch.
use crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys;
use crate::arch::x86_64::port;
use crate::driver::virtio::{self,Transport};
use crate::sync::IrqSpinLock;
use core::sync::atomic::Ordering;

const VIRTIO_BLK_T_IN:u32=0;
const VIRTIO_BLK_T_OUT:u32=1;

struct Blk{ready:bool,transport:Transport,capacity:u64,qsize:usize}
static BLK:IrqSpinLock<Blk>=IrqSpinLock::new(Blk{ready:false,transport:Transport::legacy(0),capacity:0,qsize:0});

const MAX_Q:usize=128;
const RING_BYTES:usize=12288;
#[repr(C,align(4096))]struct Rings([u8;RING_BYTES]);
static mut RING:Rings=Rings([0;RING_BYTES]);
/* The offset of the used ring inside the ring buffer. The descriptor table
   and the available ring follow the split virtqueue layout, the used ring
   starts at the next page boundary. */
#[repr(align(16))]struct ReqBuf([u8;16]);
static mut REQ:ReqBuf=ReqBuf([0;16]);
#[repr(align(512))]struct StatusBuf([u8;1]);
static mut BLK_STATUS:StatusBuf=StatusBuf([0;1]);
#[repr(align(512))]struct DataBuf([u8;512]);
static mut DATA:DataBuf=DataBuf([0;512]);

fn ring()->*mut u8{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RING.0)as*mut u8}}
/* The split virtqueue layout inside the ring buffer: the descriptor table
   comes first, the available ring follows it and the used ring starts at
   the next page boundary. */
fn avail_off(size:usize)->usize{16*size}
fn used_off(size:usize)->usize{((avail_off(size)+6+2*size)+4095)&!4095}
fn avail_idx(r:*mut u8,size:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{r.add(avail_off(size)+2).cast()}}
fn avail_slot(r:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{r.add(avail_off(size)+4+2*(i%size)).cast()}}
fn used_idx(r:*mut u8,size:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{r.add(used_off(size)+2).cast()}}
fn used_entry(r:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize)->(u32,u32){unsafe{let p=r.add(used_off(size)+4+8*(i%size));((p as*const u32).read_volatile(),(p.add(4)as*const u32).read_volatile())}}
fn desc_set(r:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize,addr:u64,len:u32,flags:u16,next:u16){
 unsafe{
  let p=r.add(16*(i%size));
  (p as*mut u64).write_volatile(addr);
  (p.add(8)as*mut u32).write_volatile(len);
  (p.add(12)as*mut u16).write_volatile(flags);
  (p.add(14)as*mut u16).write_volatile(next);
 }
}

/// Builds the queue description and installs it in the device.
fn setup_queue(t:&Transport,size:usize)->Result<(),&'static str>{
 let r=ring();
 let desc=r;
 let avail=unsafe{r.add(avail_off(size))};
 let used=unsafe{r.add(used_off(size))};
 let q=virtio::Queue{desc,avail,used,size};
 t.queue_setup(0,&q);
 Ok(())
}

fn do_request(typ:u32,sector:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 let (t,size)={
  let blk=BLK.lock();
  if !blk.ready{return Err(crate::errno::ENODEV)}
  (blk.transport,blk.qsize)
 };
 let r=ring();
 unsafe{
  // The request header: type, reserved, sector.
  let hdr=core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(REQ.0)as*mut u8;
  (hdr as*mut u32).write_volatile(typ);
  (hdr.add(4)as*mut u32).write_volatile(0);
  (hdr.add(8)as*mut u64).write_volatile(sector);
  BLK_STATUS.0[0]=0xff;
  // The descriptor chain: header, data, status.
  desc_set(r,size,0,virt_to_phys(core::ptr::addr_of!(REQ.0)as u64),16,1,1);
  desc_set(r,size,1,virt_to_phys(core::ptr::addr_of!(DATA.0)as u64),512,if typ==VIRTIO_BLK_T_IN{3}else{1},2);
  desc_set(r,size,2,virt_to_phys(core::ptr::addr_of!(BLK_STATUS.0)as u64),1,2,0);
  // The available ring.
  let ai=avail_idx(r,size).read_volatile();
  avail_slot(r,size,ai as usize).write_volatile(0);
  core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::SeqCst);
  avail_idx(r,size).write_volatile(ai.wrapping_add(1));
  core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::SeqCst);
  t.notify(0);
  // Wait for the device to consume the request.
  let start=used_idx(r,size).read_volatile();
  let mut used=start;
  for _ in 0..100000{
   used=used_idx(r,size).read_volatile();
   if used!=start{break;}
  }
  if used==start{return Err(crate::errno::EIO)}
  let _=used_entry(r,size,used.wrapping_sub(1)as usize);
  if (BLK_STATUS.0[0]as*const u8).read_volatile()!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EIO)}
 }
 Ok(())
}

/// Brings up the block device and returns its capacity in sectors.
pub fn init()->Result<u64,&'static str>{
 let t=match virtio::probe(virtio::MODERN_BLK).or_else(||virtio::probe(virtio::LEGACY_BLK)){Some(t)=>t,None=>return Err("virtio-blk not found")};
 t.set_status(0);
 t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK);
 t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK|virtio::S_DRIVER);
 if t.modern{
  if !t.negotiate_modern(){return Err("virtio-blk feature negotiation failed")}
 }else{
  let _=t.read_features(0);
  t.set_features(0,0);
 }
 let size=t.queue_size(0,MAX_Q);
 if size<1{return Err("virtio-blk queue too small")}
 if size>MAX_Q{return Err("virtio-blk queue larger than the ring buffer")}
 setup_queue(&t,size)?;
 t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK|virtio::S_DRIVER|virtio::S_DRIVER_OK);
 let mut cap=t.device_config(0);
 if cap==0{
  // The legacy device reports the capacity in two words.
  unsafe{cap=port::inl(t.io+0x14)as u64|((port::inl(t.io+0x18)as u64)<<32);}
 }
 let mut blk=BLK.lock();
 blk.ready=true;blk.transport=t;blk.capacity=cap;blk.qsize=size;if cap==0{blk.capacity=1024;}
 Ok(blk.capacity)
}
pub fn ready()->bool{BLK.lock().ready}
pub fn capacity()->u64{BLK.lock().capacity}
pub fn read_sector(sector:u64,dst:&mut [u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 if dst.len()<512{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 do_request(VIRTIO_BLK_T_IN,sector)?;
 unsafe{dst[..512].copy_from_slice(&DATA.0[..512]);}
 Ok(())
}
pub fn write_sector(sector:u64,src:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 if src.len()<512{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 unsafe{DATA.0[..512].copy_from_slice(&src[..512]);}
 do_request(VIRTIO_BLK_T_OUT,sector)?;
 Ok(())
}

//! virtio-net driver.
//!
//! The driver speaks both virtio interfaces: the legacy port I/O device
//! (version 0.9.5) and the modern memory mapped device (virtio 1.0), so the
//! default `virtio-net-pci` device model works without a compatibility
//! switch. The device is polled: the receive queue is drained whenever the
//! network stack asks for a frame.
//!
//! The driver state sits behind an interrupt safe lock. The timer interrupt
//! polls the link, so a plain spin lock would deadlock: an interrupt that
//! arrives while the interrupted code holds the lock spins forever on a lock
//! the interrupted code cannot release.
use crate::arch::x86_64::phys::virt_to_phys;
use crate::driver::virtio::{self,Transport};
use crate::sync::IrqSpinLock;
use core::sync::atomic::Ordering;

const RXQ:u16=0;
const TXQ:u16=1;
/* The queue size a modern device is configured with. */
const QSIZE:usize=256;
const DESC_WRITE:u16=2;

struct Net{ready:bool,transport:Transport,mac:[u8;6],rx_used:u16,tx_used:u16,tx_next:u8,rx_size:usize,tx_size:usize}
static NET:IrqSpinLock<Net>=IrqSpinLock::new(Net{ready:false,transport:Transport::legacy(0),mac:[0;6],rx_used:0,tx_used:0,tx_next:0,rx_size:0,tx_size:0});

const RING_BYTES:usize=12288;
const RX_BUFS:usize=32;
const TX_BUFS:usize=8;
pub const BUF_SZ:usize=2048;

#[repr(C,align(4096))]struct Rings([u8;RING_BYTES]);
static mut RX_RING:Rings=Rings([0;RING_BYTES]);
static mut TX_RING:Rings=Rings([0;RING_BYTES]);
#[repr(align(64))]struct Buf([u8;BUF_SZ]);
static mut RX_MEM:[Buf;RX_BUFS]=[const{Buf([0;BUF_SZ])};RX_BUFS];
static mut TX_MEM:[Buf;TX_BUFS]=[const{Buf([0;BUF_SZ])};TX_BUFS];

fn rx_ring()->*mut u8{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RX_RING.0)as*mut u8}}
fn tx_ring()->*mut u8{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(TX_RING.0)as*mut u8}}
fn rx_buf(i:usize)->*mut u8{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(RX_MEM[i].0)as*mut u8}}
fn tx_buf(i:usize)->*mut u8{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of_mut!(TX_MEM[i].0)as*mut u8}}

/* The split virtqueue layout inside one ring buffer: the descriptor table
   comes first, the available ring follows it and the used ring starts at
   the next page boundary. The legacy interface demands exactly this layout,
   the modern one names the three parts separately but accepts any address. */
fn avail_off(size:usize)->usize{16*size}
fn used_off(size:usize)->usize{((avail_off(size)+6+2*size)+4095)&!4095}
/* The available ring of a split virtqueue follows the descriptor table. */
fn avail_idx(ring:*mut u8,size:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{ring.add(avail_off(size)+2).cast()}}
fn avail_slot(ring:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{ring.add(avail_off(size)+4+2*(i%size)).cast()}}
fn used_idx(ring:*mut u8,size:usize)->*mut u16{unsafe{ring.add(used_off(size)+2).cast()}}
fn used_entry(ring:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize)->(u32,u32){unsafe{let p=ring.add(used_off(size)+4+8*(i%size));((p as*const u32).read_volatile(),(p.add(4)as*const u32).read_volatile())}}
fn desc_set(ring:*mut u8,size:usize,i:usize,addr:u64,len:u32,flags:u16){
 unsafe{
  let p=ring.add(16*(i%size));
  (p as*mut u64).write_volatile(addr);
  (p.add(8)as*mut u32).write_volatile(len);
  (p.add(12)as*mut u16).write_volatile(flags);
  (p.add(14)as*mut u16).write_volatile(0);
 }
}
/* Publishes one descriptor on a queue's available ring and notifies the device. */
unsafe fn post(t:&Transport,q:u16,ring:*mut u8,size:usize,id:usize){
 unsafe{
  let ai=avail_idx(ring,size).read_volatile();
  avail_slot(ring,size,ai as usize).write_volatile(id as u16);
  core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::SeqCst);
  avail_idx(ring,size).write_volatile(ai.wrapping_add(1));
  core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::SeqCst);
  t.notify(q);
 }
}

/// Installs one queue of the device.
fn setup_queue(t:&Transport,q:u16,ring:*mut u8,min:usize)->Result<usize,&'static str>{
 let size=t.queue_size(q,QSIZE);
 if size<min{return Err("virtio queue smaller than requested")}
 if size>QSIZE{return Err("virtio queue larger than the ring buffer")}
 if used_off(size)+6+8*size>RING_BYTES{return Err("virtio queue does not fit the ring buffer")}
 let qd=virtio::Queue{desc:ring,avail:unsafe{ring.add(avail_off(size))},used:unsafe{ring.add(used_off(size))},size};
 t.queue_setup(q,&qd);
 Ok(size)
}
pub fn init()->Result<[u8;6],&'static str>{
 let t=match virtio::probe(virtio::MODERN_NET).or_else(||virtio::probe(virtio::LEGACY_NET)){Some(t)=>t,None=>return Err("virtio-net device not found")};
 t.set_status(0);
 t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK);
 t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK|virtio::S_DRIVER);
 if t.modern{
  if !t.negotiate_modern(){return Err("virtio-net feature negotiation failed")}
 }else{
  let _=t.read_features(0);
  t.set_features(0,0);
 }
 let rx_size=setup_queue(&t,RXQ,rx_ring(),RX_BUFS)?;
 let tx_size=setup_queue(&t,TXQ,tx_ring(),TX_BUFS)?;
 let mut net=NET.lock();
 unsafe{
  for i in 0..6{net.mac[i]=t.device_config(i as u64)as u8}
  for i in 0..RX_BUFS{desc_set(rx_ring(),rx_size,i,virt_to_phys(rx_buf(i)as u64),BUF_SZ as u32,DESC_WRITE)}
  let ai=avail_idx(rx_ring(),rx_size);
  for i in 0..RX_BUFS{avail_slot(rx_ring(),rx_size,i).write_volatile(i as u16)}
  ai.write_volatile(RX_BUFS as u16);
  core::sync::atomic::compiler_fence(Ordering::SeqCst);
  t.set_status(virtio::S_ACK|virtio::S_DRIVER|virtio::S_DRIVER_OK);
  t.notify(RXQ);
 }
 net.transport=t;
 net.rx_used=0;net.tx_used=0;net.tx_next=0;
 net.rx_size=rx_size;net.tx_size=tx_size;
 net.ready=true;
 let mac=net.mac;
 Ok(mac)
}
pub fn ready()->bool{NET.lock().ready}
pub fn mac()->[u8;6]{NET.lock().mac}
/* Moves one received frame out of the RX ring into `out`, recycling the
   buffer immediately so the device can keep filling it. */
/* Legacy virtio-net always prefixes each buffer with a 10-byte
   virtio_net_hdr (flags, gso_type, hdr_len, gso_size, csum_start,
   csum_offset). We negotiate no offloads, so it is all zeroes and the
   frame data starts after it. */
const VNET_HDR:usize=10;
pub fn poll_frame(out:&mut[u8])->Option<usize>{
 let mut net=NET.lock();
 if !net.ready{return None}
 let size=net.rx_size;
 unsafe{
  let ring=rx_ring();
  let used=used_idx(ring,size).read_volatile();
  if used==net.rx_used{return None}
  let(id,len)=used_entry(ring,size,net.rx_used as usize);
  net.rx_used=net.rx_used.wrapping_add(1);
  let id=id as usize;
  if id>=RX_BUFS{return None}
  if (len as usize)<VNET_HDR{post(&net.transport,RXQ,ring,size,id);return None}
  let n=core::cmp::min(len as usize-VNET_HDR,out.len());
  core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(rx_buf(id).add(VNET_HDR),out.as_mut_ptr(),n);
  post(&net.transport,RXQ,ring,size,id);
  Some(n)
 }
}
/* Copies one frame into a TX buffer and transmits it, draining the used ring
   entry before returning. */
pub fn send_frame(frame:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 if frame.len()+VNET_HDR>BUF_SZ{return Err(crate::errno::EMSGSIZE)}
 let (i,t,size)={
  let mut net=NET.lock();
  if !net.ready{return Err(crate::errno::ENETDOWN)}
  let i=net.tx_next as usize;
  net.tx_next=((i+1)%TX_BUFS)as u8;
  (i,net.transport,net.tx_size)
 };
 unsafe{
  for b in 0..VNET_HDR{tx_buf(i).add(b).write_volatile(0)}
  core::ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(frame.as_ptr(),tx_buf(i).add(VNET_HDR),frame.len());
  desc_set(tx_ring(),size,i,virt_to_phys(tx_buf(i)as u64),(frame.len()+VNET_HDR)as u32,0);
  post(&t,TXQ,tx_ring(),size,i);
  /* Wait for the device to consume the buffer. The lock is not held while
     waiting: the timer interrupt polls the link and would deadlock on it. */
  let mut spins=0u32;
  loop{
   let used=used_idx(tx_ring(),size).read_volatile();
   let done={let mut net=NET.lock();if used.wrapping_sub(net.tx_used)>=1{net.tx_used=used;true}else{false}};
   if done{return Ok(())}
   spins+=1;
   if spins>4_000_000{return Err(crate::errno::EIO)}
   core::hint::spin_loop();
  }
 }
}

//! Bounds-checked ELF64 parser and PT_LOAD planning. No borrowed packed structs.
pub const ELF_MAGIC:[u8;4]=*b"\x7fELF";
pub const ET_EXEC:u16=2; pub const ET_DYN:u16=3; pub const EM_X86_64:u16=62;
pub const PT_LOAD:u32=1; pub const PT_INTERP:u32=3; pub const PF_X:u32=1; pub const PF_W:u32=2; pub const PF_R:u32=4;
pub const MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS:usize=16;

#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct Header { pub kind:u16,pub machine:u16,pub entry:u64,pub phoff:u64,pub phentsize:u16,pub phnum:u16 }
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct ProgramHeader { pub kind:u32,pub flags:u32,pub offset:u64,pub vaddr:u64,pub filesz:u64,pub memsz:u64,pub align:u64 }
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct LoadSegment { pub file_offset:u64,pub vaddr:u64,pub file_size:u64,pub memory_size:u64,pub flags:u32 }
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
pub struct LoadPlan { pub header:Header,pub segments:[LoadSegment;MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS],pub segment_count:usize,pub has_interpreter:bool,pub interp_offset:u64,pub interp_len:u64,pub image_start:u64,pub image_end:u64 }
impl Default for LoadPlan { fn default()->Self{Self{header:Header::default(),segments:[LoadSegment::default();MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS],segment_count:0,has_interpreter:false,interp_offset:0,interp_len:0,image_start:u64::MAX,image_end:0}} }

pub fn plan(image:&[u8])->Result<LoadPlan,&'static str>{
    if image.len()<64{return Err("ELF header truncated")}
    if image[0..4]!=ELF_MAGIC{return Err("bad ELF magic")}
    if image[4]!=2 || image[5]!=1 || image[6]!=1{return Err("not ELF64 little-endian v1")}
    let h=Header{kind:u16at(image,16)?,machine:u16at(image,18)?,entry:u64at(image,24)?,phoff:u64at(image,32)?,phentsize:u16at(image,54)?,phnum:u16at(image,56)?};
    if h.machine!=EM_X86_64{return Err("wrong machine")}
    if h.kind!=ET_EXEC && h.kind!=ET_DYN{return Err("unsupported ELF type")}
    if (h.phentsize as usize)<56{return Err("program header too small")}
    let table_size=(h.phentsize as u64).checked_mul(h.phnum as u64).ok_or("program table overflow")?;
    let table_end=h.phoff.checked_add(table_size).ok_or("program table overflow")?;
    if table_end>image.len() as u64{return Err("program table truncated")}
    let mut out=LoadPlan{header:h,..LoadPlan::default()};
    for i in 0..h.phnum as u64 {
        let off=h.phoff.checked_add(i*(h.phentsize as u64)).ok_or("program header overflow")? as usize;
        let p=ProgramHeader{kind:u32at(image,off)?,flags:u32at(image,off+4)?,offset:u64at(image,off+8)?,vaddr:u64at(image,off+16)?,filesz:u64at(image,off+32)?,memsz:u64at(image,off+40)?,align:u64at(image,off+48)?};
        if p.kind==PT_INTERP{out.has_interpreter=true;out.interp_offset=p.offset;out.interp_len=p.filesz;}
        if p.kind!=PT_LOAD{continue}
        if out.segment_count==MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS{return Err("too many load segments")}
        if p.filesz>p.memsz{return Err("segment filesz exceeds memsz")}
        if p.offset.checked_add(p.filesz).ok_or("segment file overflow")?>image.len() as u64{return Err("segment truncated")}
        if p.vaddr.checked_add(p.memsz).ok_or("segment address overflow")?>crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return Err("segment outside user range")}
        if p.align!=0 && !p.align.is_power_of_two(){return Err("bad segment alignment")}
        if p.align>1 && (p.vaddr%p.align)!=(p.offset%p.align){return Err("incongruent segment alignment")}
        let seg=LoadSegment{file_offset:p.offset,vaddr:p.vaddr,file_size:p.filesz,memory_size:p.memsz,flags:p.flags};
        out.image_start=core::cmp::min(out.image_start,p.vaddr); out.image_end=core::cmp::max(out.image_end,p.vaddr+p.memsz);
        out.segments[out.segment_count]=seg; out.segment_count+=1;
    }
    if out.segment_count==0{return Err("ELF has no load segments")}
    if h.entry<out.image_start || h.entry>=out.image_end{return Err("entry outside image")}
    Ok(out)
}
/* Reads the NUL-terminated PT_INTERP path recorded by plan(). */
pub fn interp_path<'a>(image:&'a [u8],plan:&LoadPlan)->Result<&'a str,&'static str>{
 if plan.interp_len==0{return Err("no PT_INTERP segment")}
 let seg=image.get(plan.interp_offset as usize..(plan.interp_offset+plan.interp_len)as usize).ok_or("interp truncated")?;
 let end=seg.iter().position(|&b|b==0).ok_or("interp not NUL-terminated")?;
 core::str::from_utf8(&seg[..end]).map_err(|_|"interp not utf8")
}
fn get<const N:usize>(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<[u8;N],&'static str>{b.get(o..o+N).ok_or("field truncated")?.try_into().map_err(|_|"field truncated")}
fn u16at(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<u16,&'static str>{Ok(u16::from_le_bytes(get(b,o)?))}
fn u32at(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<u32,&'static str>{Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(get(b,o)?))}
fn u64at(b:&[u8],o:usize)->Result<u64,&'static str>{Ok(u64::from_le_bytes(get(b,o)?))}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: kernel/elf.rs

//! Transaction-friendly Linux ELF execution plan. It does not mutate an address space.
use crate::{elf,linux_abi::stack,vm::VmFlags};
pub const USER_STACK_TOP:u64=0x0000_7fff_ffff_f000; pub const USER_STACK_SIZE:usize=1024*1024;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)] pub struct Mapping { pub start:u64,pub length:u64,pub file_offset:u64,pub file_length:u64,pub flags:VmFlags }
pub struct Plan { pub entry:u64,pub phdr:u64,pub phent:u64,pub phnum:u64,pub mappings:[Mapping;elf::MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS],pub count:usize,pub stack_start:u64,pub stack_end:u64,pub needs_interpreter:bool,pub interp_base:u64 }
pub fn plan(image:&[u8])->Result<Plan,&'static str>{
 let e=elf::plan(image)?; let mut out=Plan{entry:e.header.entry,phdr:0,phent:e.header.phentsize as u64,phnum:e.header.phnum as u64,mappings:[Mapping::default();elf::MAX_LOAD_SEGMENTS],count:0,stack_start:USER_STACK_TOP-USER_STACK_SIZE as u64,stack_end:USER_STACK_TOP,needs_interpreter:e.has_interpreter,interp_base:0};
 for s in &e.segments[..e.segment_count]{let start=s.vaddr&!4095;let delta=s.vaddr-start;let end=align_up(s.vaddr.checked_add(s.memory_size).ok_or("segment overflow")?)?;let mut f=VmFlags::USER;if s.flags&elf::PF_R!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::READ.0}if s.flags&elf::PF_W!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::WRITE.0}if s.flags&elf::PF_X!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::EXEC.0}out.mappings[out.count]=Mapping{start,length:end-start,file_offset:s.file_offset.checked_sub(delta).ok_or("segment page prefix outside file")?,file_length:s.file_size.checked_add(delta).ok_or("segment file length overflow")?,flags:f};out.count+=1;}
 // AT_PHDR is valid when the program-header table resides in a loadable file range.
 for s in &e.segments[..e.segment_count]{if e.header.phoff>=s.file_offset && e.header.phoff<s.file_offset+s.file_size{out.phdr=s.vaddr+(e.header.phoff-s.file_offset);break}}
 if out.phdr==0{return Err("program headers are not mapped")}
 Ok(out)
}
pub fn stack_info(p:&Plan)->stack::ImageInfo{stack::ImageInfo{phdr:p.phdr,phent:p.phent,phnum:p.phnum,entry:p.entry,base:p.interp_base}}
fn align_up(v:u64)->Result<u64,&'static str>{v.checked_add(4095).map(|x|x&!4095).ok_or("alignment overflow")}

//! Commits an ELF64 image (static or dynamic) to a private bootstrap CR3 with
//! rollback on failure. Dynamic images load their PT_INTERP interpreter first
//! and enter through its entry point with AT_BASE set.
use crate::{arch::x86_64::{paging::{self,PageTableRoot},phys::Identity},elf,linux_abi::stack,memory};
const PAGE:u64=4096;const MAX_COMMITTED:usize=512;const USER_STACK_PAGES:u64=8;
/* Load bias for the dynamic linker and for the PIE main image. Every user
   region sits alone in a page table slot: the linker in slot 128, the image
   in slot 129, the anonymous mappings in slot 130 and the program break in
   slot 131. Regions that share a slot also share the lower level indices,
   so a mapping in one of them would overwrite the page table entry of the
   other. */
const LD_BASE:u64=0x0000_4000_0000_0000;
const EXE_BASE:u64=0x0000_4080_0000_0000;
/* One committed PT_LOAD range, kept for /proc/<pid>/maps. */
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]pub struct MapSeg{pub start:u64,pub end:u64,pub flags:u32}
pub const MAX_MAP_SEGS:usize=8;
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub struct Loaded{pub entry:u64,pub rsp:u64,pub cr3:u64,pub bootstrap_cr3:u64,pub image_slot:usize,pub stack_slot:usize,pub interp_slot:usize,
 pub cmdline:[u8;512],pub cmdline_len:usize,pub environ:[u8;512],pub environ_len:usize,
 pub segs:[MapSeg;MAX_MAP_SEGS],pub nsegs:usize,pub stack_base:u64,pub stack_top:u64}
fn pack_vec(out:&mut [u8],out_len:&mut usize,items:&[&str]){
 for s in items{let b=s.as_bytes();if *out_len+b.len()+1>out.len(){return}out[*out_len..*out_len+b.len()].copy_from_slice(b);*out_len+=b.len();out[*out_len]=0;*out_len+=1;}
}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]struct Owned{va:u64,pa:u64}const EMPTY:Owned=Owned{va:0,pa:0};
struct Transaction<'a>{root:&'a PageTableRoot,phys:&'a Identity,pages:[Owned;MAX_COMMITTED],len:usize,committed:bool}
impl<'a>Transaction<'a>{fn new(root:&'a PageTableRoot,phys:&'a Identity)->Self{Self{root,phys,pages:[EMPTY;MAX_COMMITTED],len:0,committed:false}}fn page(&mut self,va:u64,flags:u64)->Result<u64,&'static str>{/* Always map a fresh frame: the address may still hold the old image's
           page, and the exec teardown releases those frames afterwards. */
 if self.len==MAX_COMMITTED{return Err("ELF transaction page limit")}let pa=memory::alloc_frame().ok_or("out of physical frames")?;unsafe{self.phys.bytes(pa,PAGE as usize).fill(0)};if self.root.map_page(self.phys,va,pa,flags).is_err(){let _=memory::free_frame(pa);return Err("map_page failed")}self.pages[self.len]=Owned{va,pa};self.len+=1;Ok(pa)}fn commit(mut self){self.committed=true;}}
impl Drop for Transaction<'_>{fn drop(&mut self){if self.committed{return}for p in self.pages[..self.len].iter().rev(){let _=self.root.unmap_page(self.phys,p.va);let _=memory::free_frame(p.pa);}}}
pub unsafe fn load_static(image:&[u8])->Result<Loaded,&'static str>{unsafe{load_static_args(image,&["/init"],&[],"/init",PageTableRoot::current().pml4)}}
pub unsafe fn load_static_args(image:&[u8],argv:&[&str],envp:&[&str],execfn:&str,bootstrap_cr3:u64)->Result<Loaded,&'static str>{
 let parsed=elf::plan(image)?;let access=Identity;let firmware=PageTableRoot::current();
 /* A PT_INTERP segment means dynamic linking: load the interpreter from the
    initramfs, then the main image above it, and enter via the interpreter. */
 let interp_bytes=if parsed.has_interpreter{
  let path=elf::interp_path(image,&parsed)?;
  Some(crate::vfs::image(path).map_err(|_|"interpreter not in initramfs")?)
 }else{None};
 let interp=match interp_bytes{Some(b)=>Some(elf::plan(b).map_err(|_|"bad interpreter ELF")?),None=>None};
 let is_pie=parsed.header.kind==elf::ET_DYN;
 let exe_base=if is_pie{EXE_BASE}else{0};
 let entry=match interp.as_ref(){Some(ip)=>LD_BASE+ip.header.entry,None=>exe_base+parsed.header.entry};
 /* The interpreter and the main image live in page table slots of their own,
    so a mapping in one of them never overwrites the page table entry of the
    other. A static ET_EXEC keeps its absolute entry-derived slot. */
 let image_slot=if is_pie{((exe_base>>39)&511)as usize}else{((parsed.header.entry>>39)&511)as usize};
 let interp_slot=((LD_BASE>>39)&511)as usize;
 let stack_slot=((crate::exec::USER_STACK_TOP>>39)&511)as usize;
 let mmap_slot=((crate::vm::MMAP_BASE>>39)&511)as usize;
 let brk_slot=((crate::process::BRK_BASE>>39)&511)as usize;
 let mut private=[image_slot,stack_slot,mmap_slot,interp_slot,brk_slot];
 let root=firmware.clone_bootstrap(&access,&private).map_err(|_|"private CR3 allocation failed")?;
 let mut tx=Transaction::new(&root,&access);
 if let Some(ip)=interp.as_ref(){
  let bytes=interp_bytes.ok_or("interpreter image lost")?;
  for seg in &ip.segments[..ip.segment_count]{
   if ((LD_BASE+seg.vaddr>>39)&511)as usize!=interp_slot{return Err("interpreter crosses private PML4 slot")}
   map_segment(&mut tx,&root,&access,bytes,LD_BASE,seg)?;
  }
 }
 for seg in &parsed.segments[..parsed.segment_count]{
  if ((exe_base+seg.vaddr>>39)&511)as usize!=image_slot{return Err("ELF crosses private PML4 slot")}
  map_segment(&mut tx,&root,&access,image,exe_base,seg)?;
 }
 let stack_top=crate::exec::USER_STACK_TOP;let stack_base=stack_top-USER_STACK_PAGES*PAGE;
 for i in 0..USER_STACK_PAGES{let pa=tx.page(stack_base+i*PAGE,paging::USER|paging::WRITABLE|paging::NX)?;unsafe{access.bytes(pa,PAGE as usize).fill(0)};}
 let stack_pa=root.translate(&access,stack_top-PAGE).ok_or("stack mapping disappeared")?;
 let page=unsafe{access.bytes(stack_pa,PAGE as usize)};
 /* AT_PHDR and AT_ENTRY describe the main image; AT_BASE is the interpreter. */
 let phoff=parsed.header.phoff;
 let phdr_vaddr=parsed.segments[..parsed.segment_count].iter().find_map(|s|{
  if phoff>=s.file_offset&&phoff<s.file_offset+s.file_size{Some(exe_base+s.vaddr+(phoff-s.file_offset))}else{None}
 }).ok_or("program headers not mapped")?;
 let info=stack::ImageInfo{phdr:phdr_vaddr,phent:parsed.header.phentsize as u64,phnum:parsed.header.phnum as u64,entry:exe_base+parsed.header.entry,base:if interp.is_some(){LD_BASE}else{0}};
  let built=stack::build(page,stack_top-PAGE,argv,envp,execfn,[0x4e;16],info).map_err(|_|"stack build failed")?;
  let mut segs=[MapSeg::default();MAX_MAP_SEGS];let mut nsegs=0usize;
  if let Some(ip)=interp.as_ref(){for seg in &ip.segments[..ip.segment_count]{if nsegs==MAX_MAP_SEGS{break}segs[nsegs]=MapSeg{start:LD_BASE+seg.vaddr&!(PAGE-1),end:align(LD_BASE+seg.vaddr+seg.memory_size).map_err(|_|"segment overflow")?,flags:seg.flags};nsegs+=1;}}
  for seg in &parsed.segments[..parsed.segment_count]{if nsegs==MAX_MAP_SEGS{break}segs[nsegs]=MapSeg{start:exe_base+seg.vaddr&!(PAGE-1),end:align(exe_base+seg.vaddr+seg.memory_size).map_err(|_|"segment overflow")?,flags:seg.flags};nsegs+=1;}
  let mut cmdline=[0u8;512];let mut cmdline_len=0usize;pack_vec(&mut cmdline,&mut cmdline_len,argv);
  let mut environ=[0u8;512];let mut environ_len=0usize;pack_vec(&mut environ,&mut environ_len,envp);
  let loaded=Loaded{entry,rsp:built.rsp,cr3:root.pml4,bootstrap_cr3,image_slot,stack_slot,interp_slot,cmdline,cmdline_len,environ,environ_len,segs,nsegs,stack_base,stack_top};tx.commit();Ok(loaded)
}
/* Maps one PT_LOAD at base+seg.vaddr, copying file bytes and zeroing the rest. */
fn map_segment(tx:&mut Transaction,root:&PageTableRoot,access:&Identity,image:&[u8],base:u64,seg:&elf::LoadSegment)->Result<(),&'static str>{
 let first=base+seg.vaddr&!(PAGE-1);
 let last=align(base+seg.vaddr.checked_add(seg.memory_size).ok_or("segment overflow")?)?;
 let mut va=first;
 while va<last{tx.page(va,flags(seg.flags))?;va+=PAGE;}
 for i in 0..seg.file_size{let pa=root.translate(access,base+seg.vaddr+i).ok_or("ELF mapping disappeared")?;unsafe{access.bytes(pa,1)[0]=image[(seg.file_offset+i)as usize];}}
 for i in seg.file_size..seg.memory_size{let pa=root.translate(access,base+seg.vaddr+i).ok_or("BSS mapping disappeared")?;unsafe{access.bytes(pa,1)[0]=0;}}
 Ok(())
}
fn flags(pf:u32)->u64{let mut f=paging::USER;if pf&elf::PF_W!=0{f|=paging::WRITABLE}if pf&elf::PF_X==0{f|=paging::NX}f}
fn align(v:u64)->Result<u64,&'static str>{v.checked_add(PAGE-1).map(|x|x&!(PAGE-1)).ok_or("alignment overflow")}

//! Minimal read-only ext2 filesystem on the first virtio-blk device.
use crate::{errno, vfs};
const EXT2_MAGIC:u16=0xEF53;
pub fn ready()->bool{ crate::driver::virtio_blk::ready() }
fn read_bytes(off:u64,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 let cap=crate::driver::virtio_blk::capacity()*512;
 if off as usize+dst.len() > cap as usize {return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 let mut done=0usize;
 while done<dst.len(){
  let sec=(off as usize+done)/512;
  let soff=(off as usize+done)%512;
  let n=core::cmp::min(dst.len()-done,512-soff);
  let mut buf=[0u8;512];
  // read sector
  let mut frame=[0u8;512];
  // virtio_blk read_sector needs 512 dst
  if crate::driver::virtio_blk::read_sector(sec as u64,&mut frame).is_err(){return Err(errno::EIO)}
  dst[done..done+n].copy_from_slice(&frame[soff..soff+n]);
  done+=n;
 }
 Ok(())
}
fn read_block(block:u64,blksize:usize,buf:&mut [u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 if buf.len()<blksize{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 let off=block*blksize as u64;
 read_bytes(off,&mut buf[..blksize])
}
#[repr(C)] struct Super{ s_inodes_count:u32, s_blocks_count:u32, _r0:u32, _r1:u32, _r2:u32, s_first_data_block:u32, s_log_block_size:u32, _r3:u32, s_blocks_per_group:u32, _r4:u32, s_inodes_per_group:u32, _r5:u32, _r6:u32, _r7:u16, _r8:u16, s_magic:u16, _r9:[u8;30], s_inode_size:u16, }
#[repr(C)] struct GroupDesc{ bg_block_bitmap:u32, bg_inode_bitmap:u32, bg_inode_table:u32, bg_free_blocks_count:u16, bg_free_inodes_count:u16, bg_used_dirs_count:u16, bg_pad:u16, _r:[u8;12], }
#[repr(C)] struct Inode{ i_mode:u16, i_uid:u16, i_size_lo:u32, i_atime:u32, i_ctime:u32, i_mtime:u32, i_dtime:u32, i_gid:u16, i_links_count:u16, i_blocks:u32, i_flags:u32, i_osd1:u32, i_block:[u32;15], i_generation:u32, i_file_acl:u32, i_dir_acl:u32, i_faddr:u32, i_osd2:[u8;12], }
pub fn superblock()->Result<Super,i64>{
 let mut buf=[0u8;1024];
 read_bytes(1024,&mut buf)?;
 let sb:Super=unsafe{core::ptr::read(buf.as_ptr() as *const Super)};
 if sb.s_magic != EXT2_MAGIC {return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 Ok(sb)
}
fn block_size(sb:&Super)->usize{1024usize<<(sb.s_log_block_size as usize)}
fn groups_count(sb:&Super)->usize{ (sb.s_blocks_count as usize + sb.s_blocks_per_group as usize -1)/sb.s_blocks_per_group as usize }
fn group_desc_block(sb:&Super)->u64{ if block_size(sb)==1024 {2} else {1} }
fn read_group_desc(sb:&Super,g:usize)->Result<GroupDesc,i64>{
 let bs=block_size(sb);
 let mut buf=[0u8;4096];
 read_block(group_desc_block(sb),bs,&mut buf)?;
 let off=g*32;
 let gd:GroupDesc=unsafe{core::ptr::read(buf[off..].as_ptr() as *const GroupDesc)};
 Ok(gd)
}
fn read_inode(sb:&Super,inum:u32)->Result<Inode,i64>{
 let g=((inum-1) as usize)/sb.s_inodes_per_group as usize;
 let idx=(inum-1) as usize % sb.s_inodes_per_group as usize;
 let gd=read_group_desc(sb,g)?;
 let bs=block_size(sb);
 let inode_size=sb.s_inode_size as usize;
 let table_block=gd.bg_inode_table as u64;
 let byte_off=idx*inode_size;
 let block=table_block + (byte_off/bs) as u64;
 let off=byte_off%bs;
 let mut buf=[0u8;4096];
 read_block(block,bs,&mut buf)?;
 let inode:Inode=unsafe{core::ptr::read(buf[off..].as_ptr() as *const Inode)};
 Ok(inode)
}
fn read_file_block(sb:&Super,inode:&Inode,block_index:usize)->Result<u64,i64>{
 let bs=block_size(sb);
 if block_index < 12 { return Ok(inode.i_block[block_index] as u64); }
 // single indirect
 let mut idx=block_index-12;
 if idx < (bs/4) {
  let ib=inode.i_block[12] as u64;
  if ib==0 {return Ok(0)}
  let mut buf=[0u8;4096];
  read_block(ib,bs,&mut buf)?;
  let b=u32::from_le_bytes(buf[idx*4..idx*4+4].try_into().unwrap());
  return Ok(b as u64);
 }
 idx -= bs/4;
 if idx < (bs/4)*(bs/4) {
  let dib=inode.i_block[13] as u64;
  if dib==0 {return Ok(0)}
  let mut buf=[0u8;4096];
  read_block(dib,bs,&mut buf)?;
  let i1=(idx/(bs/4)) as usize;
  let ib=u32::from_le_bytes(buf[i1*4..i1*4+4].try_into().unwrap()) as u64;
  if ib==0 {return Ok(0)}
  let mut buf2=[0u8;4096];
  read_block(ib,bs,&mut buf2)?;
  let i2=idx%(bs/4);
  let b=u32::from_le_bytes(buf2[i2*4..i2*4+4].try_into().unwrap());
  return Ok(b as u64);
 }
 Err(errno::EINVAL)
}
pub fn lookup(path:&str)->Result<vfs::Inode,i64>{
 if !ready(){return Err(errno::ENODEV)}
 let sb=superblock()?;
 let mut inum=2u32;
 let mut inode=read_inode(&sb,inum)?;
 let trimmed=path.trim_start_matches('/').trim_end_matches('/');
 if trimmed.is_empty(){return Ok(make_vfs_inode("/mnt",inum,&inode,&sb))}
 for comp in trimmed.split('/') {
  if comp.is_empty() || comp=="." {continue}
  if comp==".." { // not fully supported
   inum=2; inode=read_inode(&sb,inum)?; continue
  }
  // read directory
  let size=inode.i_size_lo as usize;
  let mut off=0usize;
  let mut found=None;
   while off < size {
   let mut buf=[0u8;256];
   // read at least 8 bytes
   let blk=read_file_block(&sb,&inode,off/ (block_size(&sb)))?;
   if blk==0 {break}
   let bs=block_size(&sb);
   let mut block=[0u8;4096];
   read_block(blk,bs,&mut block)?;
   let boff=off%bs;
   let ino=u32::from_le_bytes(block[boff..boff+4].try_into().unwrap());
   let rec_len=u16::from_le_bytes(block[boff+4..boff+6].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
   let name_len=block[boff+6] as usize;
   if rec_len==0 || boff+rec_len>bs {break}
   if ino!=0 && name_len==comp.len() && &block[boff+8..boff+8+name_len]==comp.as_bytes(){
    found=Some(ino); break;
   }
   off += rec_len;
  }
  let ino=found.ok_or(errno::ENOENT)?;
   inode=read_inode(&sb,ino)?;
   // skip re-read below
  inum=ino;
  inode=read_inode(&sb,inum)?;
  }
let n=make_vfs_inode(path,inum,&inode,&sb); Ok(n)
}
fn make_vfs_inode(path:&str,inum:u32,inode:&Inode,sb:&Super)->vfs::Inode{
 let mode=inode.i_mode as u32;
 let size=inode.i_size_lo as u64;
 let mut name=[0u8;127+1];
 let b=path.as_bytes(); let nl=b.len().min(127); name[..nl].copy_from_slice(&b[..nl]);
 vfs::Inode{ino: (inum as u64)+0x100000, mode: if mode & 0o170000==0 {vfs::S_IFREG| (mode&0o777)} else {mode}, size, capacity:size, data:core::ptr::null_mut(), rdev:inum as u64, pages:[0;8], npages:0, name, name_len:nl}
}
pub fn read(inode_rdev:u32,off:u64,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let sb=superblock()?;
 let inode=read_inode(&sb,inode_rdev)?;
 let size=inode.i_size_lo as usize;
 if off as usize >= size {return Ok(0)}
 let mut done=0usize;
 while done<dst.len() && off as usize+done < size {
  let poff=off as usize+done;
  let blk=read_file_block(&sb,&inode,poff/ (block_size(&sb) as usize))?;
  if blk==0 {break}
  let bs=block_size(&sb);
  let mut block=[0u8;4096];
  read_block(blk,bs,&mut block)?;
  let po=poff % bs;
  let n=core::cmp::min(dst.len()-done, core::cmp::min(bs-po, size-poff));
  if n==0{break}
  dst[done..done+n].copy_from_slice(&block[po..po+n]);
  done+=n;
 }
 Ok(done)
}
pub fn is_dir(path:&str)->bool{
 if path=="/mnt" {return true}
 if let Ok(inode)=lookup(path){ inode.mode & 0o040000 !=0 } else {false}
}
pub fn readdir(path:&str,mut cb:impl FnMut(u64,u32,&str))->Result<usize,i64>{
 // path is ext2 path without /mnt
 let inum=if path=="/" || path.is_empty() {2} else {
  // lookup parent? For simplicity only root listing supported
  2
 };
 let sb=superblock()?;
 let inode=read_inode(&sb,inum)?;
 let size=inode.i_size_lo as usize;
 let mut off=0usize; let mut count=0;
 let bs=block_size(&sb);
 while off < size {
  let blk=read_file_block(&sb,&inode,off/bs)?;
  if blk==0 {break}
  let mut block=[0u8;4096];
  read_block(blk,bs,&mut block)?;
  let boff=off%bs;
  let ino=u32::from_le_bytes(block[boff..boff+4].try_into().unwrap());
  let rec_len=u16::from_le_bytes(block[boff+4..boff+6].try_into().unwrap()) as usize;
  let name_len=block[boff+6] as usize;
  let ftype=block[boff+7];
  if rec_len==0 || boff+rec_len>bs {break}
  if ino!=0 && name_len>0 {
   let name=core::str::from_utf8(&block[boff+8..boff+8+name_len]).unwrap_or("");
   if name!="." && name!=".." {
    let mode=if ftype==2 {vfs::S_IFDIR} else {vfs::S_IFREG};
    cb((ino as u64)+0x100000, mode, name);
    count+=1;
   }
  }
  off += rec_len;
 }
 Ok(count)
}

//! Open-file descriptions and per-process descriptor tables.
//! dup and fork share descriptions, mirroring the Unix file-description model.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,vfs};
/* Linux allows 1024 descriptors per process and a far larger system-wide
   table; the old 32/64 limits made ordinary shells fail with EMFILE. */
pub const MAX_FDS:usize=1024;const MAX_OPEN:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Debug,Default,PartialEq,Eq)]
pub struct ResourceStats{pub descriptors:usize,pub open_descriptions:usize,pub open_references:usize}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub enum OpenKind{Console,Inode(vfs::Inode),PipeRead(u8),PipeWrite(u8),Socket(u8),EventFd(u64,u8),Epoll,TimerFd(u64,u64),CharDevice(u64),BlockDevice(u64),Inotify,SignalFd(u64),Netlink,IoUring,PidFd(u32)}
pub const O_APPEND:u32=0o2000;
pub const O_NONBLOCK:u32=0o4000;
const STATUS_MASK:u32=O_APPEND|O_NONBLOCK;
const FD_CLOEXEC:u8=1;
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]struct OpenFile{refs:u32,offset:u64,flags:u32,kind:OpenKind}
static OPEN:SpinLock<[Option<OpenFile>;MAX_OPEN]>=SpinLock::new([const{None};MAX_OPEN]);
static mut IOURING_SQ_RING:[u64;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_CQ_RING:[u64;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_SQES:[u64;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_ENTRIES:[u32;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_SQ_HEAD:[u32;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_CQ_TAIL:[u32;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_MAP_REFS:[u32;MAX_OPEN]=[0;MAX_OPEN];
static mut IOURING_CLOSING:[bool;MAX_OPEN]=[false;MAX_OPEN];

const FREE_FD:i32=-1;
/* The close-on-exec flags are a bitmap: a descriptor table with a thousand
   entries must still fit in a kernel stack. */
const CLOSE_WORDS:usize=MAX_FDS/64;
pub struct FdTable{slots:[i32;MAX_FDS],close_exec:[u64;CLOSE_WORDS]}
impl FdTable{
 pub fn stdio()->Result<Self,i64>{let mut t=Self{slots:[FREE_FD;MAX_FDS],close_exec:[0;CLOSE_WORDS]};for fd in 0..3{let id=alloc_file(OpenKind::Console)?;t.slots[fd]=id as i32;}Ok(t)}
 pub fn get(&self,fd:usize)->Option<u32>{if fd>=MAX_FDS{return None}if self.slots[fd]<0{None}else{Some(self.slots[fd]as u32)}}
 fn clear_close(&mut self,fd:usize){self.close_exec[fd/64]&=!(1u64<<(fd%64));}
 fn set_close_bit(&mut self,fd:usize){self.close_exec[fd/64]|=1u64<<(fd%64);}
 fn close_bit(&self,fd:usize)->bool{self.close_exec[fd/64]&(1u64<<(fd%64))!=0}
 pub fn install(&mut self,id:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{self.install_from(0,id)}
 pub fn install_from(&mut self,min:usize,id:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{let fd=(min..MAX_FDS).find(|&i|self.slots[i]<0).ok_or(errno::EMFILE)?;self.slots[fd]=id as i32;self.clear_close(fd);Ok(fd as i32)}
 pub fn install_pair(&mut self,first:u32,second:u32)->Result<(i32,i32),i64>{let a=(0..MAX_FDS).find(|&i|self.slots[i]<0).ok_or(errno::EMFILE)?;self.slots[a]=first as i32;self.clear_close(a);let b=match (a+1..MAX_FDS).find(|&i|self.slots[i]<0){Some(b)=>b,None=>{self.slots[a]=FREE_FD;return Err(errno::EMFILE)}};self.slots[b]=second as i32;self.clear_close(b);Ok((a as i32,b as i32))}
 pub fn replace(&mut self,fd:usize,id:u32)->Result<(),i64>{if fd>=MAX_FDS{return Err(errno::EBADF)}if self.slots[fd]>=0{release_closed(self.slots[fd]as u32)}self.slots[fd]=id as i32;self.clear_close(fd);Ok(())}
   pub fn close(&mut self,fd:usize)->Result<(),i64>{if fd>=MAX_FDS{return Err(errno::EBADF)}let s=self.slots[fd];if s<0{return Err(errno::EBADF)}self.slots[fd]=FREE_FD;self.clear_close(fd);release_closed(s as u32);Ok(())}
   pub fn get_close_exec(&self,fd:usize)->Option<bool>{if fd>=MAX_FDS||self.slots[fd]<0{return None}Some(self.close_bit(fd))}
   pub fn set_close_exec(&mut self,fd:usize,v:bool)->Result<(),i64>{if fd>=MAX_FDS||self.slots[fd]<0{return Err(errno::EBADF)}if v{self.set_close_bit(fd)}else{self.clear_close(fd)}Ok(())}
   /* Closes every descriptor marked FD_CLOEXEC, as execve must. Sockets need
      the extra peer teardown that close(2) performs. */
   pub fn close_on_exec(&mut self)->usize{
    let mut closed=0;
    for i in 0..MAX_FDS{
     if !self.close_bit(i)||self.slots[i]<0{continue}
     let id=self.slots[i]as u32;
     let kind=kind(id);
     self.slots[i]=FREE_FD;self.clear_close(i);
     release_closed(id);
     if let Some(OpenKind::Socket(s))=kind{crate::socket::close(s);}
     closed+=1;
    }
    closed
   }
   pub fn clone_incref(&self)->Self{let mut t=Self{slots:self.slots,close_exec:self.close_exec};for &s in t.slots.iter(){if s>=0{incref(s as u32)}}t}
  pub fn descriptor_count(&self)->usize{self.slots.iter().filter(|&&s|s>=0).count()}
  pub fn open_fds(&self,mut cb:impl FnMut(usize)){for i in 0..MAX_FDS{if self.slots[i]>=0{cb(i)}}}
  pub fn open_description_count(&self)->usize{
   let mut ids=[FREE_FD;MAX_FDS];let mut count=0;
   for &slot in &self.slots{if slot<0||ids[..count].contains(&slot){continue}ids[count]=slot;count+=1;}
   count
  }
  pub fn close_all(&mut self)->usize{let mut closed=0;for i in 0..MAX_FDS{let s=self.slots[i];if s>=0{release(s as u32);self.slots[i]=FREE_FD;self.clear_close(i);closed+=1}}closed}
}
impl Drop for FdTable{fn drop(&mut self){let _=self.close_all();}}

pub fn resource_stats()->ResourceStats{
 let t=OPEN.lock();
 let mut stats=ResourceStats::default();
 for file in t.iter().flatten(){stats.open_descriptions+=1;stats.open_references+=file.refs as usize;}
 stats
}

pub fn resource_self_test()->bool{
 let before=resource_stats();
 let table=match FdTable::stdio(){Ok(t)=>t,Err(_)=>return false};
 if table.descriptor_count()!=3||table.open_description_count()!=3{return false}
 let after_create=resource_stats();
 if after_create.open_descriptions!=before.open_descriptions+3||after_create.open_references!=before.open_references+3{return false}
 let clone=table.clone_incref();
 if clone.descriptor_count()!=3||clone.open_description_count()!=3{return false}
 let after_clone=resource_stats();
 if after_clone.open_descriptions!=after_create.open_descriptions||after_clone.open_references!=after_create.open_references+3{return false}
 drop(clone);
 if resource_stats()!=after_create{return false}
 drop(table);
 resource_stats()==before
}

pub fn alloc_file(kind:OpenKind)->Result<u32,i64>{alloc_file_flags(kind,0)}
pub fn alloc_file_flags(kind:OpenKind,flags:u32)->Result<u32,i64>{
 let mut t=OPEN.lock();let id=t.iter().enumerate().position(|(i,s)|s.is_none()&&!unsafe{IOURING_CLOSING[i]}).ok_or(errno::EMFILE)?;
 t[id]=Some(OpenFile{refs:1,offset:0,flags:flags&STATUS_MASK,kind});drop(t);
 match kind{OpenKind::PipeRead(p)=>crate::pipe::add_reader(p),OpenKind::PipeWrite(p)=>crate::pipe::add_writer(p),_=>{}}
 Ok(id as u32)
}
pub fn incref(id:u32){if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.refs+=1}}
pub fn release(id:u32){release_inner(id,false)}
/* A real close(2): drops the process's POSIX record locks on the inode even
   while other descriptors keep the description alive (Linux semantics). */
pub fn release_closed(id:u32){release_inner(id,true)}
fn release_inner(id:u32,closed:bool){
 let (kind,last)={let mut t=OPEN.lock();match t.get_mut(id as usize).and_then(Option::as_mut){None=>return,Some(f)=>{f.refs-=1;let last=f.refs==0;let k=f.kind;if last{t[id as usize]=None;}(k,last)}}};
 if closed{if let OpenKind::Inode(n)=kind{crate::record_lock::on_close(crate::task::current_pid(),n.ino);}}
 if !last{return}
 crate::flock::release(id);crate::record_lock::release_ofd(id);
 match kind{OpenKind::PipeRead(p)=>crate::pipe::close_reader(p),OpenKind::PipeWrite(p)=>crate::pipe::close_writer(p),OpenKind::Inotify=>crate::inotify::close(id),OpenKind::IoUring=>iouring_close(id),_=>{}}
}
pub fn kind(id:u32)->Option<OpenKind>{OPEN.lock()[id as usize].map(|f|f.kind)}
/* Per-open-file status flags (O_APPEND/O_NONBLOCK), shared by dup and fork. */
pub fn flags(id:u32)->u32{OPEN.lock()[id as usize].map(|f|f.flags).unwrap_or(0)}
pub fn flags_set(id:u32,set:u32,clear:u32){if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.flags=(f.flags|(set&STATUS_MASK))&!(clear&STATUS_MASK)}}
pub fn is_nonblock(id:u32)->bool{flags(id)&O_NONBLOCK!=0}
/* Directory-walk cursor used by getdents64. */
pub fn offset_get(id:u32)->u64{OPEN.lock()[id as usize].map(|f|f.offset).unwrap_or(0)}
/* Positional variants used by pread64/pwrite64; they ignore the file offset. */
pub fn read_at(id:u32,off:u64,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let f=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;
 match f.kind{
  OpenKind::Inode(n)=>vfs::read_off(n,off,dst),
  OpenKind::PipeRead(p)=>crate::pipe::read(p,dst,f.flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0),
  OpenKind::Socket(s)=>crate::socket::recv(s,dst,f.flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0),
  _=>Err(errno::EBADF),
 }
}
pub fn write_at(id:u32,off:u64,src:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let f=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;
 match f.kind{
  OpenKind::Inode(n)=>vfs::write_off(n,off,src),
  OpenKind::PipeWrite(p)=>crate::pipe::write(p,src,f.flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0),
  OpenKind::Socket(s)=>{crate::socket::send(s,src,f.flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0)},
  _=>Err(errno::EBADF),
 }
}
pub fn offset_set(id:u32,v:u64){if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset=v}}

pub fn open(path:&str)->Result<i32,i64>{open_flags(path,0)}
pub fn open_flags(path:&str,status:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{let inode=vfs::lookup(path)?;let kind=if inode.mode&0o170000==0o020000{OpenKind::CharDevice(inode.rdev)}else if inode.mode&0o170000==0o060000{OpenKind::BlockDevice(inode.rdev)}else{OpenKind::Inode(inode)};let id=alloc_file_flags(kind,status)?;match crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.fds.install(id)){Some(Ok(fd))=>Ok(fd),Some(Err(e))=>{release(id);Err(e)},None=>{release(id);Err(errno::ESRCH)}}}
/* O_CREAT path: creates the node when missing, then opens it. */
pub fn create_open(path:&str,mode:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{create_open_flags(path,mode,0)}
pub fn create_open_flags(path:&str,mode:u32,status:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{let inode=match vfs::lookup(path){Ok(n)=>n,Err(_)=>vfs::create(path,mode)?};let id=alloc_file_flags(if inode.mode&0o170000==0o020000{OpenKind::CharDevice(inode.rdev)}else if inode.mode&0o170000==0o060000{OpenKind::BlockDevice(inode.rdev)}else{OpenKind::Inode(inode)},status)?;match crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.fds.install(id)){Some(Ok(fd))=>Ok(fd),Some(Err(e))=>{release(id);Err(e)},None=>{release(id);Err(errno::ESRCH)}}}
pub fn close(fd:usize)->Result<(),i64>{
 let kind=crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.fds.get(fd)).flatten().map(|id|crate::fd::kind(id));
 crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.fds.close(fd)).unwrap_or(Err(errno::ESRCH))?;
 if let Some(Some(crate::fd::OpenKind::Socket(s)))=kind{crate::socket::close(s);}
 Ok(())
}
pub fn read(id:u32,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let(kind,offset,flags)={let t=OPEN.lock();let f=t[id as usize].ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;(f.kind,f.offset,f.flags)};
 let nonblock=flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0;
 match kind{
  OpenKind::Console=>Ok(0),
  OpenKind::Inode(n)=>{let got=vfs::read(n,offset,dst)?;if got>0{if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset+=got as u64}}Ok(got)},
  OpenKind::CharDevice(dev)=>crate::device::read((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,dst),
  OpenKind::BlockDevice(dev)=>{let got=crate::device::blk_read((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,offset,dst)?; if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset+=got as u64;} Ok(got)},
  OpenKind::PipeRead(p)=>crate::pipe::read(p,dst,nonblock),
  OpenKind::Socket(s)=>crate::socket::recv(s,dst,nonblock),
  OpenKind::EventFd(c,sem)=>{
    if dst.len()<8{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
    let mut t=OPEN.lock();let f=t[id as usize].as_mut().ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;
    if let OpenKind::EventFd(counter,_)=&mut f.kind{
      if *counter==0{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
      let val=if sem!=0{1}else{*counter};
      let bytes=val.to_ne_bytes();dst[..8].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
      *counter=counter.saturating_sub(val);
      Ok(8)
    }else{Err(errno::EBADF)}
  },
  OpenKind::Epoll=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::Inotify=>crate::inotify::read(id,dst),
  OpenKind::SignalFd(mask)=>{
    let pending=crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.pending).unwrap_or(0);
    let m=pending & mask;
    if m==0{return Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}
    let sig=(m.trailing_zeros() as i32)+1;
    let _=crate::task::with_current_process(|p|{p.pending &= !crate::signal::bit(sig);});
    let mut buf=[0u8;128];
    buf[0..4].copy_from_slice(&sig.to_ne_bytes());
    let pid=crate::task::current_pid() as i32;
    buf[12..16].copy_from_slice(&pid.to_ne_bytes());
    if dst.len()<128{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
    dst[..128].copy_from_slice(&buf);
    Ok(128)
  },
  OpenKind::Netlink=>Ok(0),
  OpenKind::IoUring=>Err(crate::errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::PidFd(_)=>Err(crate::errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::TimerFd(exp,ivl)=>{
    let mut exp=exp; let mut ivl=ivl;
    let mut now=crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks();
    if now<exp{
      crate::task::sleep_until(exp);
      now=crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks();
    }
    let mut expirations=1u64;
    if ivl>0 && now>exp{
      expirations=1+(now-exp)/ivl.max(1);
      exp += expirations*ivl.max(1);
    } else if ivl>0 {
      exp = exp.saturating_add(ivl);
    } else {
      exp = u64::MAX;
    }
    // update kind in place
    let mut t=OPEN.lock();
    if let Some(f)=t[id as usize].as_mut(){
      if let OpenKind::TimerFd(e,i)=&mut f.kind{*e=exp;*i=ivl;}
    }
    drop(t);
    let bytes=expirations.to_ne_bytes();
    if dst.len()<8{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
    dst[..8].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
    Ok(8)
  },
  OpenKind::PipeWrite(_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::CharDevice(dev)=>crate::device::read((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,dst),
  OpenKind::BlockDevice(dev)=>{let got=crate::device::blk_read((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,offset,dst)?; if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset+=got as u64;} Ok(got)},
  OpenKind::Inotify=>crate::inotify::read(id,dst),
  OpenKind::SignalFd(mask)=>{
    let pending=crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.pending).unwrap_or(0);
    let m=pending & mask;
    if m==0{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
    let sig=(m.trailing_zeros() as i32)+1;
    let _=crate::task::with_current_process(|p|{p.pending &= !crate::signal::bit(sig);});
    let mut buf=[0u8;128];
    buf[0..4].copy_from_slice(&sig.to_ne_bytes());
    let pid=crate::task::current_pid() as i32;
    buf[12..16].copy_from_slice(&pid.to_ne_bytes());
    if dst.len()<128{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
    dst[..128].copy_from_slice(&buf);
    Ok(128)
  },
  OpenKind::Netlink=>Ok(0),
  OpenKind::IoUring=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::PidFd(_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
 }
}
pub fn write(id:u32,src:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let(kind,offset,flags)={let t=OPEN.lock();let f=t[id as usize].ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;(f.kind,f.offset,f.flags)};
 let nonblock=flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0;
 match kind{
  OpenKind::Console=>{crate::console::write_bytes(src);Ok(src.len())},
  OpenKind::PipeWrite(p)=>crate::pipe::write(p,src,nonblock),
  OpenKind::Socket(s)=>{crate::socket::send(s,src,nonblock)},
  OpenKind::EventFd(c,_)=>{
    if src.len()<8{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
    let val=u64::from_ne_bytes(src[..8].try_into().unwrap());
    let mut t=OPEN.lock();let f=t[id as usize].as_mut().ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;
    if let OpenKind::EventFd(counter,_)=&mut f.kind{*counter=counter.saturating_add(val);Ok(8)}else{Err(errno::EBADF)}
  },
  OpenKind::Epoll=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::TimerFd(_,_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::Inode(n)=>{
   let start=if flags&O_APPEND!=0{crate::vfs::inode_size(n.ino).unwrap_or(n.size)}else{offset};
   let got=vfs::write(n,start,src)?;
   if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset=start+got as u64}
   Ok(got)
  },
  OpenKind::PipeRead(_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::CharDevice(dev)=>crate::device::write((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,src),
  OpenKind::BlockDevice(dev)=>{let got=crate::device::blk_write((dev>>8)as u32,(dev&0xff)as u32,offset,src)?; if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset+=got as u64;} Ok(got)},
  OpenKind::Netlink=>Ok(src.len()),
  OpenKind::IoUring=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::PidFd(_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::Inotify=>Err(errno::EBADF),
  OpenKind::SignalFd(_)=>Err(errno::EBADF),
 }
}
pub fn seek(id:u32,off:i64,whence:u64)->Result<u64,i64>{
 let(kind,offset)={let t=OPEN.lock();let f=t[id as usize].ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;(f.kind,f.offset)};
 let size=match kind{OpenKind::Inode(n)=>crate::vfs::inode_size(n.ino).unwrap_or(n.size),OpenKind::Console=>return Err(errno::ESPIPE),OpenKind::PipeRead(_)|OpenKind::PipeWrite(_)|OpenKind::Socket(_)|OpenKind::EventFd(_,_)|OpenKind::Epoll|OpenKind::TimerFd(_,_)|OpenKind::CharDevice(_)|OpenKind::BlockDevice(_)|OpenKind::Inotify|OpenKind::SignalFd(_)|OpenKind::Netlink|OpenKind::IoUring|OpenKind::PidFd(_)=>return Err(errno::ESPIPE)};
 let size=match kind{OpenKind::BlockDevice(_)=>crate::driver::virtio_blk::capacity(),_=>0};
 let base=match whence{0=>0,1=>offset,2=>size,_=>return Err(errno::EINVAL)};let next=if off>=0{base.checked_add(off as u64)}else{base.checked_sub(off.unsigned_abs())}.ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;
 if let Some(f)=OPEN.lock()[id as usize].as_mut(){f.offset=next}Ok(next)
}
pub fn dup2(oldfd:usize,newfd:usize)->Result<i32,i64>{
 if newfd>=MAX_FDS{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 crate::task::with_current_process(|p|{let id=p.fds.get(oldfd).ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;if oldfd==newfd{return Ok(newfd as i32)}incref(id);p.fds.replace(newfd,id)?;Ok(newfd as i32)}).unwrap_or(Err(errno::ESRCH))
}
pub fn dup_from(oldfd:usize,min:usize)->Result<i32,i64>{
 if min>=MAX_FDS{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 crate::task::with_current_process(|p|{let id=p.fds.get(oldfd).ok_or(errno::EBADF)?;incref(id);match p.fds.install_from(min,id){Ok(fd)=>Ok(fd),Err(e)=>{release(id);Err(e)}}}).unwrap_or(Err(errno::ESRCH))
}
pub fn pipe2(flags:u64)->Result<(i32,i32),i64>{
 const O_CLOEXEC:u64=0x80000;const O_NONBLOCK:u64=0o4000;
 if flags&!(O_CLOEXEC|O_NONBLOCK)!=0{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 let status=if flags&O_NONBLOCK!=0{self::O_NONBLOCK}else{0};
 let p=crate::pipe::alloc()?;
 let r=alloc_file_flags(OpenKind::PipeRead(p),status)?;
 let w=match alloc_file_flags(OpenKind::PipeWrite(p),status){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{release(r);return Err(e)}};
 let installed=crate::task::with_current_process(|proc|{let pair=proc.fds.install_pair(r,w)?;if flags&O_CLOEXEC!=0{let _=proc.fds.set_close_exec(pair.0 as usize,true);let _=proc.fds.set_close_exec(pair.1 as usize,true);}Ok(pair)});
 match installed{Some(Ok(pair))=>Ok(pair),Some(Err(e))=>{release(r);release(w);Err(e)},None=>{release(r);release(w);Err(errno::ESRCH)}}
}

//! flock(2) advisory locks keyed by the open-file description.
//! Locks live on the inode but are owned by the description, so dup and fork
//! share a lock while an independent open(2) contends. Locks are dropped when
//! the last descriptor referencing the description closes.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,wait_queue::WaitQueue};
const MAX_LOCKS:usize=32;
pub const LOCK_SH:u64=1;
pub const LOCK_EX:u64=2;
pub const LOCK_NB:u64=4;
pub const LOCK_UN:u64=8;
const OP_MASK:u64=LOCK_SH|LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB|LOCK_UN;
struct Lock{used:bool,ino:u64,id:u32,exclusive:bool,q:WaitQueue}
const EMPTY:Lock=Lock{used:false,ino:0,id:0,exclusive:false,q:WaitQueue::new()};
static LOCKS:[SpinLock<Lock>;MAX_LOCKS]=[const{SpinLock::new(EMPTY)};MAX_LOCKS];

/* Finds the first entry from another description that blocks this request. */
fn conflict(ino:u64,id:u32,exclusive:bool)->Option<usize>{
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let l=LOCKS[i].lock();
  if l.used&&l.id!=id&&l.ino==ino&&(exclusive||l.exclusive){return Some(i)}
 }
 None
}
fn place(ino:u64,id:u32,exclusive:bool)->Result<(),i64>{
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let mut l=LOCKS[i].lock();
  if !l.used{l.used=true;l.ino=ino;l.id=id;l.exclusive=exclusive;return Ok(())}
 }
 Err(errno::ENOMEM)
}
pub fn flock(id:u32,ino:u64,op:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 if op&!OP_MASK!=0{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 if op&LOCK_UN!=0{release(id);return Ok(())}
 if op&(LOCK_SH|LOCK_EX)==0{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 let exclusive=op&LOCK_EX!=0;
 let nonblock=op&LOCK_NB!=0;
 loop{
  /* Re-requesting replaces any lock this description already holds. */
  release(id);
  match conflict(ino,id,exclusive){
   None=>return place(ino,id,exclusive),
   Some(idx)=>{
    if nonblock{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
    let registered={let l=LOCKS[idx].lock();l.q.register().is_ok()};
    if !registered{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
    crate::task::block_current();
   }
  }
 }
}
pub fn release(id:u32){
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let mut l=LOCKS[i].lock();
  if l.used&&l.id==id{l.used=false;l.id=0;l.q.wake_all();}
 }
}

//! Minimal futex: FUTEX_WAIT, FUTEX_WAKE and the PRIVATE/CROSS_PROCESS,
//! REQUEUE and PI opcodes are rejected. A fixed waiter table keyed by user
//! address backs the two supported operations; Linux wake/wait semantics for
//! those ops are preserved, including the EAGAIN value race check.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,task};
pub const FUTEX_WAIT:u32=0;pub const FUTEX_WAKE:u32=1;
pub const FUTEX_PRIVATE_FLAG:u32=0x80;
const MAX_WAITERS:usize=64;
struct Waiter{used:bool,uaddr:u64,pid:u32}
const EMPTY:Waiter=Waiter{used:false,uaddr:0,pid:0};
static WAITERS:SpinLock<[Waiter;MAX_WAITERS]>=SpinLock::new([EMPTY;MAX_WAITERS]);
/* Reads the u32 the kernel compares against the caller-supplied value. */
fn peek(addr:u64)->Result<u32,i64>{
 let mut b=[0u8;4];
 if let Err(e)=unsafe{crate::usercopy::copy_from_user(&mut b,addr as usize)}{return Err(e)}
 Ok(u32::from_ne_bytes(b))
}
/* Blocks until woken or the deadline elapses; deadline_ns==u64::MAX waits forever. */
fn block_until(deadline_ns:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 if deadline_ns==u64::MAX{
  task::block_current();
  return Ok(())
 }
 let now=crate::time::monotonic_ns();
 if now>=deadline_ns{return Err(errno::ETIMEDOUT)}
 task::sleep_until(crate::time::ticks_after_ns(deadline_ns-now));
 Ok(())
}
pub fn dispatch(uaddr:u64,op:u32,val:u32,timeout:u64)->Result<i64,i64>{
 if uaddr==0||uaddr%4!=0{return Err(errno::EFAULT)}
 match op&!FUTEX_PRIVATE_FLAG{
  FUTEX_WAIT=>wait(uaddr,val,timeout,op),
  FUTEX_WAKE=>wake(uaddr,val),
  _=>Err(errno::ENOSYS),
 }
}
fn wait(uaddr:u64,val:u32,timeout:u64,op:u32)->Result<i64,i64>{
 if peek(uaddr)?!=val{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
 let deadline=if timeout==0{u64::MAX}else{
  let mut ts=[0u8;16];
  if let Err(e)=unsafe{crate::usercopy::copy_from_user(&mut ts,timeout as usize)}{return Err(e)}
  let sec=u64::from_ne_bytes(ts[0..8].try_into().unwrap());
  let nsec=u64::from_ne_bytes(ts[8..16].try_into().unwrap());
  if sec>u32::MAX as u64{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
  sec.saturating_mul(1_000_000_000).saturating_add(nsec)
 };
 let pid=task::current_pid();
 loop{
  {let mut w=WAITERS.lock();
   if w.iter().any(|e|e.used&&e.uaddr==uaddr&&e.pid==pid){return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
   match w.iter().position(|e|!e.used){Some(i)=>{w[i]=Waiter{used:true,uaddr,pid}},None=>return Err(errno::ENOMEM)}};
  let res=block_until(deadline);
  /* wake() removes our entry; find it by identity rather than slot index,
     since the slot may have been reused by another waiter meanwhile. */
  let still_registered={let mut w=WAITERS.lock();
   match w.iter().position(|e|e.used&&e.uaddr==uaddr&&e.pid==pid){Some(i)=>{w[i]=EMPTY;true},None=>false}};
  if !still_registered{return Ok(0)}
  match res{Ok(())=>continue,Err(e)=>return Err(e)}
 }
}
pub fn wake(uaddr:u64,count:u32)->Result<i64,i64>{
 let mut woken=0i64;
 let targets:([u32;MAX_WAITERS],usize)={
  let mut w=WAITERS.lock();
  let mut t=[0u32;MAX_WAITERS];let mut n=0;
  for e in w.iter_mut(){
   if e.used&&e.uaddr==uaddr&&n<count as usize{t[n]=e.pid;e.used=false;n+=1}
  }
  (t,n)
 };
 for &pid in targets.0.iter().take(targets.1){
  task::wake_pid(pid);
  woken+=1;
 }
 Ok(woken)
}
