//! Ethernet, ARP, IPv4, ICMP, UDP and a minimal TCP client over virtio-net.
//!
//! This is the native side of networking. kernel/socket.rs dispatches AF_INET
//! endpoints here by id range; the Linux ABI layer above it only parses
//! sockaddr structures. Delivery is poll based: the timer tick calls `poll`,
//! and every blocking wait loops `poll` plus a `sti;hlt;cli` nap so the tick
//! (which polls too) provides the wakeup without needing a device IRQ.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,wait_queue::WaitQueue};
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32,Ordering};

pub const OUR_IP:u32=0x0A00_020F;  /* 10.0.2.15, QEMU user networking */
pub const GATEWAY:u32=0x0A00_0202; /* 10.0.2.2 */
const NETMASK:u32=0xFFFF_FF00;
const ETH_ARP:u16=0x0806;const ETH_IPV4:u16=0x0800;
const IP_ICMP:u8=1;const IP_TCP:u8=6;const IP_UDP:u8=17;
const MSS:usize=1400;
const BROADCAST:[u8;6]=[0xff;6];
/* Retransmission timeout in timer ticks (NS_PER_TICK is 1ms nominal). */
const RTO_TICKS:u64=50;

static IP_ID:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(1);
static EPHEMERAL:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(0xC350);

#[inline]fn be16(b:&[u8],o:usize)->u16{u16::from_be_bytes([b[o],b[o+1]])}
#[inline]fn be32(b:&[u8],o:usize)->u32{u32::from_be_bytes([b[o],b[o+1],b[o+2],b[o+3]])}
fn csum_fold(mut sum:u32)->u16{while sum>>16!=0{sum=(sum&0xffff)+(sum>>16)}!(sum as u16)}
fn csum_bytes(init:u32,data:&[u8])->u16{
 let mut sum=init;let mut i=0;
 while i+1<data.len(){sum+=be16(data,i)as u32;i+=2}
 if i<data.len(){sum+=(data[i]as u32)<<8}
 csum_fold(sum)
}
fn csum_pseudo(src:u32,dst:u32,proto:u8,len:usize,data:&[u8])->u16{
 let init=(src>>16)+(src&0xffff)+(dst>>16)+(dst&0xffff)+(proto as u32)+(len as u32);
 csum_bytes(init,data)
}
fn seq_gt(a:u32,b:u32)->bool{(a.wrapping_sub(b)as i32)>0}
fn seq_ge(a:u32,b:u32)->bool{a==b||seq_gt(a,b)}
#[inline]fn ticks()->u64{crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks()}
/* Puts the CPU to sleep for one tick; the timer handler polls the link, so
   data that arrives while napping is dispatched by the time we resume. */
fn idle_hlt(){unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti","hlt","cli",options(nomem,nostack,preserves_flags));}}
fn nap(round:u32){
 for _ in 0..20_000{core::hint::spin_loop();}
 if round%8==7{idle_hlt();}
}

/* ---------- link layer ---------- */

fn eth_send(ethertype:u16,dst:&[u8;6],payload:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 let mut frame=[0u8;1600];
 if payload.len()+14>frame.len(){return Err(errno::EMSGSIZE)}
 frame[..6].copy_from_slice(dst);
 frame[6..12].copy_from_slice(&crate::driver::virtio_net::mac());
 frame[12..14].copy_from_slice(&ethertype.to_be_bytes());
 frame[14..14+payload.len()].copy_from_slice(payload);
 crate::driver::virtio_net::send_frame(&frame[..14+payload.len()])
}
fn ip_emit(proto:u8,dst_ip:u32,dst_mac:&[u8;6],payload:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 let total=20+payload.len();
 if total>1500{return Err(errno::EMSGSIZE)}
 let mut pkt=[0u8;1500];
 pkt[0]=0x45;
 pkt[2..4].copy_from_slice(&(total as u16).to_be_bytes());
 let id=(IP_ID.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed)&0xffff)as u16;
 pkt[4..6].copy_from_slice(&id.to_be_bytes());
 pkt[6..8].copy_from_slice(&0x4000u16.to_be_bytes());
 pkt[8]=64;
 pkt[9]=proto;
 pkt[12..16].copy_from_slice(&OUR_IP.to_be_bytes());
 pkt[16..20].copy_from_slice(&dst_ip.to_be_bytes());
 pkt[20..total].copy_from_slice(payload);
 let h=csum_bytes(0,&pkt[..20]);
 pkt[10..12].copy_from_slice(&h.to_be_bytes());
 eth_send(ETH_IPV4,dst_mac,&pkt[..total])
}

/* ---------- ARP ---------- */

static ARP:SpinLock<[(u32,[u8;6]);4]>=SpinLock::new([(0,[0;6]);4]);
fn arp_lookup(ip:u32)->Option<[u8;6]>{
 if ip==0xFFFF_FFFF{return Some(BROADCAST)}
 ARP.lock().iter().find(|e|e.0==ip).map(|e|e.1)
}
fn arp_insert(ip:u32,mac:[u8;6]){
 if ip==0{return}
 let mut t=ARP.lock();
 if let Some(e)=t.iter_mut().find(|e|e.0==ip){e.1=mac;return}
 if let Some(e)=t.iter_mut().find(|e|e.0==0){e.0=ip;e.1=mac;return}
 t[0]=(ip,mac);
}
fn arp_emit(op:u16,target_ip:u32,dst_mac:&[u8;6],target_mac:&[u8;6]){
 let mut p=[0u8;28];
 p[0..2].copy_from_slice(&1u16.to_be_bytes());
 p[2..4].copy_from_slice(&ETH_IPV4.to_be_bytes());
 p[4]=6;p[5]=4;
 p[6..8].copy_from_slice(&op.to_be_bytes());
 p[8..14].copy_from_slice(&crate::driver::virtio_net::mac());
 p[14..18].copy_from_slice(&OUR_IP.to_be_bytes());
 p[18..24].copy_from_slice(target_mac);
 p[24..28].copy_from_slice(&target_ip.to_be_bytes());
 let _=eth_send(ETH_ARP,dst_mac,&p);
}
fn arp_request(ip:u32){arp_emit(1,ip,&BROADCAST,&[0;6])}
fn arp_reply(ip:u32,mac:&[u8;6]){arp_emit(2,ip,mac,mac)}
fn resolve(hop:u32)->Result<[u8;6],i64>{
 if let Some(m)=arp_lookup(hop){return Ok(m)}
 arp_request(hop);
 let mut round=0u32;
 loop{
  poll();
  if let Some(m)=arp_lookup(hop){return Ok(m)}
  round+=1;
  if round>2_000{return Err(errno::EHOSTUNREACH)}
  nap(round);
 }
}
pub fn mac_for(ip:u32)->Result<[u8;6],i64>{
 if ip==0xFFFF_FFFF{return Ok(BROADCAST)}
 let hop=if ip&NETMASK==OUR_IP&NETMASK{ip}else{GATEWAY};
 resolve(hop)
}

/* ---------- ICMP ---------- */

fn icmp_input(src_ip:u32,src_mac:&[u8;6],icmp:&[u8]){
 if icmp.len()<8||icmp[0]!=8{return}
 let n=core::cmp::min(icmp.len(),1500);
 let mut seg=[0u8;1500];
 seg[..n].copy_from_slice(&icmp[..n]);
 seg[0]=0;
 seg[2..4].copy_from_slice(&0u16.to_be_bytes());
 let h=csum_bytes(0,&seg[..n]);
 seg[2..4].copy_from_slice(&h.to_be_bytes());
 let _=ip_emit(IP_ICMP,src_ip,src_mac,&seg[..n]);
}

/* ---------- UDP ---------- */

pub const MAX_UDP:usize=8;
const UDPQ:usize=4;const DGRAM_MAX:usize=1472;
struct UdpSocket{
 used:bool,laddr:u32,lport:u16,connected:bool,raddr:u32,rport:u16,
 rx:[[u8;DGRAM_MAX];UDPQ],rxlen:[usize;UDPQ],rxsrc:[(u32,u16);UDPQ],rxhead:usize,rxn:usize,
 readq:WaitQueue,
}
impl UdpSocket{
 const fn new()->Self{Self{used:false,laddr:0,lport:0,connected:false,raddr:0,rport:0,rx:[[0;DGRAM_MAX];UDPQ],rxlen:[0;UDPQ],rxsrc:[(0,0);UDPQ],rxhead:0,rxn:0,readq:WaitQueue::new()}}
}
static UDPS:[SpinLock<UdpSocket>;MAX_UDP]=[const{SpinLock::new(UdpSocket::new())};MAX_UDP];
fn port_in_use(skip:usize,port:u16)->bool{
 for i in 0..MAX_UDP{
  if i==skip{continue}
  let s=UDPS[i].lock();
  if s.used&&s.lport==port{return true}
 }
 false
}
fn autobind(s:&mut UdpSocket,id:usize){
 while s.lport==0{
  let p=((EPHEMERAL.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed)&0x3fff)as u16).wrapping_add(0xC000);
  if p>=0xC000&&!port_in_use(id,p){s.lport=p;}
 }
}
pub fn udp_open()->Result<u8,i64>{
 for i in 0..MAX_UDP{
  let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
  if !s.used{*s=UdpSocket::new();s.used=true;return Ok(i as u8)}
 }
 Err(errno::EMFILE)
}
pub fn udp_close(id:u8){if(id as usize)<MAX_UDP{*UDPS[id as usize].lock()=UdpSocket::new()}}
pub fn udp_bind(id:u8,ip:u32,port:u16)->Result<(),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_UDP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if port!=0{
  for j in 0..MAX_UDP{
   if j==i{continue}
   let s=UDPS[j].lock();
   if s.used&&s.lport==port&&(s.laddr==0||ip==0||s.laddr==ip){return Err(errno::EADDRINUSE)}
  }
 }
 let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
 if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 s.laddr=ip;
 s.lport=port;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn udp_connect(id:u8,ip:u32,port:u16)->Result<(),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_UDP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
 if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if s.lport==0{autobind(&mut s,i)}
 s.connected=true;
 s.raddr=ip;
 s.rport=port;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn udp_send(id:u8,to:Option<(u32,u16)>,data:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_UDP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if data.len()>DGRAM_MAX{return Err(errno::EMSGSIZE)}
 let(dst_ip,dst_port,src_port)={
  let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
  if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
  let(ip,port)=match to{
   Some(t)=>t,
   None=>if s.connected{(s.raddr,s.rport)}else{return Err(errno::EDESTADDRREQ)},
  };
  if s.lport==0{autobind(&mut s,i)}
  (ip,port,s.lport)
 };
 let mac=resolve_peer(dst_ip)?;
 udp_emit(src_port,dst_ip,dst_port,&mac,data)?;
 Ok(data.len())
}
fn resolve_peer(ip:u32)->Result<[u8;6],i64>{
 if let Some(m)=arp_lookup(ip){return Ok(m)}
 mac_for(ip)
}
fn udp_emit(sp:u16,di:u32,dp:u16,dm:&[u8;6],data:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{
 let len=8+data.len();
 let mut seg=[0u8;1500];
 seg[0..2].copy_from_slice(&sp.to_be_bytes());
 seg[2..4].copy_from_slice(&dp.to_be_bytes());
 seg[4..6].copy_from_slice(&(len as u16).to_be_bytes());
 seg[8..len].copy_from_slice(data);
 let mut c=csum_pseudo(OUR_IP,di,IP_UDP,len,&seg[..len]);
 if c==0{c=0xffff}
 seg[6..8].copy_from_slice(&c.to_be_bytes());
 ip_emit(IP_UDP,di,dm,&seg[..len])
}
fn udp_input(src_ip:u32,seg:&[u8]){
 if seg.len()<8{return}
 let sp=be16(seg,0);let dp=be16(seg,2);
 let ulen=be16(seg,4)as usize;
 if ulen<8||seg.len()<ulen{return}
 let sum=be16(seg,6);
 if sum!=0&&csum_pseudo(src_ip,OUR_IP,IP_UDP,ulen,&seg[..ulen])!=0{return}
 udp_deliver(src_ip,sp,dp,&seg[8..ulen]);
}
fn udp_deliver(src_ip:u32,src_port:u16,dst_port:u16,data:&[u8]){
 for i in 0..MAX_UDP{
  let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
  if !s.used||s.lport!=dst_port||(s.laddr!=0&&s.laddr!=OUR_IP){continue}
  if s.connected&&!(s.raddr==src_ip&&s.rport==src_port){continue}
  if s.rxn==UDPQ{continue}
  let slot=(s.rxhead+s.rxn)%UDPQ;
  let n=core::cmp::min(data.len(),DGRAM_MAX);
  s.rx[slot][..n].copy_from_slice(&data[..n]);
  s.rxlen[slot]=n;
  s.rxsrc[slot]=(src_ip,src_port);
  s.rxn+=1;
  s.readq.wake_all();
  return;
 }
}
pub fn udp_recv(id:u8,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<(usize,u32,u16),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_UDP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut round=0u32;
 loop{
  poll();
  {
   let mut s=UDPS[i].lock();
   if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
   if s.rxn>0{
    let slot=s.rxhead;
    let n=core::cmp::min(s.rxlen[slot],dst.len());
    dst[..n].copy_from_slice(&s.rx[slot][..n]);
    let src=s.rxsrc[slot];
    s.rxhead=(s.rxhead+1)%UDPQ;
    s.rxn-=1;
    return Ok((n,src.0,src.1));
   }
  }
  round+=1;
  if round>4_000{return Err(errno::ETIMEDOUT)}
  nap(round);
 }
}
pub fn udp_local(id:u8)->Result<(u32,u16),i64>{
 let s=UDPS[id as usize].lock();
 if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 Ok((s.laddr,s.lport))
}
pub fn udp_peername(id:u8)->Result<(u32,u16),i64>{
 let s=UDPS[id as usize].lock();
 if !s.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if !s.connected{return Err(errno::ENOTCONN)}
 Ok((s.raddr,s.rport))
}

/* ---------- TCP (client) ---------- */

pub const MAX_TCP:usize=8;
const TCP_RCV_CAP:usize=8192;const TCP_SND_CAP:usize=4096;
const SYN:u8=0x02;const ACK:u8=0x10;const FIN:u8=0x01;const RST:u8=0x04;const PSH:u8=0x08;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq)]enum TcpState{Closed,SynSent,Established,FinWait1,FinWait2,CloseWait,LastAck,TimeWait}
struct TcpConn{
 used:bool,state:TcpState,
 laddr:u32,lport:u16,rip:u32,rport:u16,
 dmac:[u8;6],
 snd_una:u32,snd_nxt:u32,rlen:usize,
 rmem:[u8;TCP_SND_CAP],
 wmem:[u8;TCP_RCV_CAP],wlen:usize,rcv_nxt:u32,adv_window:u16,
 peer_window:u16,
 fin_pending:bool,fin_sent:bool,fin_seq:u32,peer_fin:bool,reset:bool,
 retrans_at:u64,retrans_budget:u32,
}
impl TcpConn{
 const fn new()->Self{Self{used:false,state:TcpState::Closed,laddr:0,lport:0,rip:0,rport:0,dmac:[0;6],snd_una:0,snd_nxt:0,rlen:0,rmem:[0;TCP_SND_CAP],wmem:[0;TCP_RCV_CAP],wlen:0,rcv_nxt:0,adv_window:0,peer_window:0,fin_pending:false,fin_sent:false,fin_seq:0,peer_fin:false,reset:false,retrans_at:0,retrans_budget:0}}
 fn window(&self)->u16{(TCP_RCV_CAP-self.wlen)as u16}
}
static TCPS:[SpinLock<TcpConn>;MAX_TCP]=[const{SpinLock::new(TcpConn::new())};MAX_TCP];
fn tcp_emit(t:&TcpConn,seq:u32,ack:u32,flags:u8,payload:&[u8]){
 let len=20+payload.len();
 let mut seg=[0u8;1500];
 seg[0..2].copy_from_slice(&t.lport.to_be_bytes());
 seg[2..4].copy_from_slice(&t.rport.to_be_bytes());
 seg[4..8].copy_from_slice(&seq.to_be_bytes());
 seg[8..12].copy_from_slice(&ack.to_be_bytes());
 seg[12]=0x50;
 seg[13]=flags;
 seg[14..16].copy_from_slice(&t.window().to_be_bytes());
 seg[20..len].copy_from_slice(payload);
 let c=csum_pseudo(OUR_IP,t.rip,IP_TCP,len,&seg[..len]);
 seg[16..18].copy_from_slice(&c.to_be_bytes());
 let _=ip_emit(IP_TCP,t.rip,&t.dmac,&seg[..len]);
}
fn tcp_ack(t:&TcpConn){tcp_emit(t,t.snd_nxt,t.rcv_nxt,ACK,&[])}
/* Sends as much buffered data as the peer window allows, then a pending FIN.
   Returns true when something was transmitted. */
fn tcp_flush(t:&mut TcpConn)->bool{
 let mut sent=false;
 if t.reset{return false}
 if t.state==TcpState::SynSent||t.state==TcpState::Closed{return false}
 let win=if t.peer_window==0{0usize}else{t.peer_window as usize};
 while t.rlen>0&&(t.snd_nxt.wrapping_sub(t.snd_una)as usize)<t.rlen{
  let inflight=t.snd_nxt.wrapping_sub(t.snd_una)as usize;
  if inflight>=win{break}
  let avail=core::cmp::min(t.rlen-inflight,win-inflight);
  let n=core::cmp::min(MSS,avail);
  if n==0{break}
  let mut tmp=[0u8;MSS];
  tmp[..n].copy_from_slice(&t.rmem[inflight..inflight+n]);
  tcp_emit(t,t.snd_nxt,t.rcv_nxt,ACK|PSH,&tmp[..n]);
  t.snd_nxt=t.snd_nxt.wrapping_add(n as u32);
  t.retrans_at=ticks()+RTO_TICKS;
  t.retrans_budget=0;
  sent=true;
 }
 if t.fin_pending&&!t.fin_sent&&(t.snd_nxt.wrapping_sub(t.snd_una)as usize)==t.rlen&&win>0{
  t.fin_seq=t.snd_nxt;
  tcp_emit(t,t.fin_seq,t.rcv_nxt,ACK|FIN,&[]);
  t.snd_nxt=t.snd_nxt.wrapping_add(1);
  t.fin_sent=true;
  t.fin_pending=false;
  t.retrans_at=ticks()+RTO_TICKS;
  t.retrans_budget=0;
  sent=true;
 }
 sent
}
pub fn tcp_open()->Result<u8,i64>{
 for i in 0..MAX_TCP{
  let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
  if !t.used{*t=TcpConn::new();t.used=true;return Ok(i as u8)}
 }
 Err(errno::EMFILE)
}
pub fn tcp_close(id:u8){
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return}
 {
  let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
  if !t.used{return}
  if !t.fin_sent&&!t.reset&&matches!(t.state,TcpState::Established|TcpState::CloseWait){
   t.fin_pending=true;
   tcp_flush(&mut t);
  }
 }
 let mut round=0u32;
 loop{
  poll();
  {
   let t=TCPS[i].lock();
   if !t.used||t.reset||t.state==TcpState::Closed||t.state==TcpState::TimeWait||round>2_000{break}
  }
  round+=1;
  nap(round);
 }
 *TCPS[i].lock()=TcpConn::new();
}
pub fn tcp_bind(id:u8,ip:u32,port:u16)->Result<(),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
 if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 t.laddr=ip;
 t.lport=port;
 Ok(())
}
fn tcp_autobind(t:&mut TcpConn,id:usize){
 while t.lport==0{
  let p=((EPHEMERAL.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed)&0x3fff)as u16).wrapping_add(0xC000);
  if p>=0xC000&&!port_in_use(id,p){t.lport=p;}
 }
}
pub fn tcp_connect(id:u8,ip:u32,port:u16)->Result<(),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mac=mac_for(ip)?;
 {
  let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
  if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
  if t.state!=TcpState::Closed{return Err(errno::EISCONN)}
  if t.lport==0{tcp_autobind(&mut t,i)}
  if t.laddr==0{t.laddr=OUR_IP}
  t.rip=ip;
  t.rport=port;
  t.dmac=mac;
  let isn=0x4000_0000u32^(ticks()as u32).wrapping_mul(0x0100_0101);
  t.snd_una=isn;
  t.snd_nxt=isn;
  t.rlen=0;
  t.wlen=0;
  t.rcv_nxt=0;
  t.peer_window=TCP_RCV_CAP as u16;
  t.state=TcpState::SynSent;
  tcp_emit(&t,isn,0,SYN,&[]);
  t.snd_nxt=isn.wrapping_add(1);
  t.retrans_at=ticks()+RTO_TICKS;
  t.retrans_budget=0;
 }
 let mut round=0u32;
 loop{
  poll();
  {
   let t=TCPS[i].lock();
   if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
   if t.reset{return Err(errno::ECONNREFUSED)}
   if t.state==TcpState::Established{return Ok(())}
  }
  round+=1;
  if round>4_000{return Err(errno::ETIMEDOUT)}
  nap(round);
 }
}
pub fn tcp_send(id:u8,data:&[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut off=0usize;let mut round=0u32;
 while off<data.len(){
  let mut progressed=false;
  {
   let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
   if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
   if t.reset{return Err(errno::ECONNRESET)}
   if t.fin_sent||t.fin_pending||t.state==TcpState::Closed{return Err(errno::EPIPE)}
   if t.rlen<TCP_SND_CAP{
    let n=core::cmp::min(TCP_SND_CAP-t.rlen,data.len()-off);
    {let rlen=t.rlen;t.rmem[rlen..rlen+n].copy_from_slice(&data[off..off+n]);}
    t.rlen+=n;
    off+=n;
    tcp_flush(&mut t);
    progressed=true;
   }
  }
  if off==data.len(){break}
  if !progressed{
   poll();
   round+=1;
   if round>4_000{return Err(errno::ETIMEDOUT)}
   nap(round);
  }
 }
 Ok(off)
}
pub fn tcp_recv(id:u8,dst:&mut[u8])->Result<usize,i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut round=0u32;
 loop{
  poll();
  {
   let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
   if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
   if t.reset{return Err(errno::ECONNRESET)}
   if t.wlen>0{
    let n=core::cmp::min(t.wlen,dst.len());
    dst[..n].copy_from_slice(&t.wmem[..n]);
    let wlen=t.wlen;
    t.wmem.copy_within(n..wlen,0);
    t.wlen-=n;
    let win=t.window();
    if win as usize>=MSS&&win.saturating_sub(t.adv_window)>=MSS as u16/2{
     t.adv_window=win;
     tcp_ack(&*t);
    }
    return Ok(n);
   }
   if t.peer_fin{return Ok(0)}
  }
  round+=1;
  if round>4_000{return Err(errno::ETIMEDOUT)}
  nap(round);
 }
}
pub fn tcp_shutdown(id:u8,how:i32)->Result<(),i64>{
 let i=id as usize;
 if i>=MAX_TCP{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if how==0{return Ok(())}
 let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
 if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if !t.fin_sent&&!t.reset&&matches!(t.state,TcpState::Established|TcpState::CloseWait){
  t.fin_pending=true;
  tcp_flush(&mut t);
 }
 Ok(())
}
pub fn tcp_local(id:u8)->Result<(u32,u16),i64>{
 let t=TCPS[id as usize].lock();
 if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 Ok((t.laddr,t.lport))
}
pub fn tcp_peername(id:u8)->Result<(u32,u16),i64>{
 let t=TCPS[id as usize].lock();
 if !t.used{return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if t.state==TcpState::Closed{return Err(errno::ENOTCONN)}
 Ok((t.rip,t.rport))
}
fn tcp_input(src_ip:u32,seg:&[u8],src_mac:[u8;6]){
 if seg.len()<20{return}
 let sp=be16(seg,0);let dp=be16(seg,2);
 let seq=be32(seg,4);let ack=be32(seg,8);
 let doff=((seg[12]>>4)as usize)*4;
 if doff<20||seg.len()<doff{return}
 let flags=seg[13];
 let window=be16(seg,14)as usize;
 let payload=&seg[doff..];
 for i in 0..MAX_TCP{
  let mut guard=TCPS[i].lock();
  if !guard.used||guard.lport!=dp||guard.rip!=src_ip||guard.rport!=sp{continue}
  let t=&mut *guard;
  t.dmac=src_mac;
  if flags&RST!=0{
   t.reset=true;
   t.state=TcpState::Closed;
   return;
  }
  let mut ack_needed=false;
  if flags&SYN!=0{
   if t.state==TcpState::SynSent&&flags&ACK!=0&&ack==t.snd_nxt{
    t.snd_una=ack;
    t.snd_nxt=ack;
    t.rcv_nxt=seq.wrapping_add(1);
    t.peer_window=window as u16;
    t.adv_window=t.window();
    t.state=TcpState::Established;
    t.retrans_at=0;
    tcp_ack(&t);
   }
   return;
  }
  if t.state==TcpState::SynSent{return}
  /* Payload acceptance: strictly in order; anything else is ACKed as-is. */
  if !payload.is_empty(){
   if seq==t.rcv_nxt{
    let space=TCP_RCV_CAP-t.wlen;
    if payload.len()<=space{
     t.wmem[t.wlen..t.wlen+payload.len()].copy_from_slice(payload);
     t.wlen+=payload.len();
     t.rcv_nxt=seq.wrapping_add(payload.len()as u32);
    }
   }
   ack_needed=true;
  }
  if flags&FIN!=0&&seq.wrapping_add(payload.len()as u32)==t.rcv_nxt{
   t.rcv_nxt=t.rcv_nxt.wrapping_add(1);
   t.peer_fin=true;
   ack_needed=true;
   if t.state==TcpState::Established{t.state=TcpState::CloseWait}
   else if t.state==TcpState::FinWait2{t.state=TcpState::TimeWait}
   else if t.state==TcpState::FinWait1{t.state=TcpState::TimeWait}
  }
  if flags&ACK!=0{
   let una=t.snd_una;
   t.peer_window=window as u16;
   if seq_gt(ack,una)&&!seq_gt(ack,una.wrapping_add(t.rlen as u32)){
    let delta=(ack.wrapping_sub(una))as usize;
    let rlen=t.rlen;
    t.rmem.copy_within(delta..rlen,0);
    t.rlen-=delta;
    /* The acknowledged bytes leave the send window, so the base of the
       window moves forward. Without this the window never shrinks and the
       same bytes are retransmitted for ever. */
    t.snd_una=ack;
    if seq_gt(t.snd_nxt,ack){t.snd_nxt=ack}
    t.retrans_at=ticks()+RTO_TICKS;
    t.retrans_budget=0;
    tcp_flush(t);
   }
   if t.fin_sent&&ack==t.fin_seq.wrapping_add(1){
    match t.state{
     TcpState::FinWait1=>t.state=if t.peer_fin{TcpState::TimeWait}else{TcpState::FinWait2},
     TcpState::LastAck|TcpState::TimeWait=>t.state=TcpState::Closed,
     _=>{}
    }
   }
  }
  if ack_needed{
   t.adv_window=t.window();
   tcp_ack(t);
  }
  return;
 }
}
fn tcp_retransmit(t:&mut TcpConn){
 let now=ticks();
 if t.retrans_at==0{t.retrans_at=now+RTO_TICKS;return}
 if now<t.retrans_at{return}
 t.retrans_budget+=1;
 if t.retrans_budget>16{
  t.reset=true;
  t.state=TcpState::Closed;
  return;
 }
 t.retrans_at=now+RTO_TICKS;
 match t.state{
  TcpState::SynSent=>{
   tcp_emit(t,t.snd_una,0,SYN,&[]);
  }
  TcpState::Established|TcpState::CloseWait|TcpState::FinWait1|TcpState::FinWait2|TcpState::LastAck=>{
   let inflight=t.snd_nxt.wrapping_sub(t.snd_una)as usize;
   if inflight>0{
    let n=core::cmp::min(MSS,inflight);
    let mut tmp=[0u8;MSS];
    tmp[..n].copy_from_slice(&t.rmem[..n]);
    tcp_emit(t,t.snd_una,t.rcv_nxt,ACK|PSH,&tmp[..n]);
    t.snd_nxt=t.snd_una.wrapping_add(n as u32);
   }else if t.fin_sent{
    tcp_emit(t,t.fin_seq,t.rcv_nxt,ACK|FIN,&[]);
   }
  }
  _=>{}
 }
}
fn tcp_tick(){
 let now=ticks();
 for i in 0..MAX_TCP{
  let mut t=TCPS[i].lock();
  if !t.used||t.reset{continue}
  let active=matches!(t.state,TcpState::SynSent|TcpState::Established|TcpState::FinWait1|TcpState::FinWait2|TcpState::CloseWait|TcpState::LastAck);
  if active&&now>=t.retrans_at{tcp_retransmit(&mut t);}
 }
}

/* ---------- receive path ---------- */

fn dispatch_arp(frame:&[u8]){
 let p=&frame[14..];
 if p.len()<28{return}
 let op=be16(p,6);
 let sha=[p[8],p[9],p[10],p[11],p[12],p[13]];
 let spa=be32(p,14);
 let tpa=be32(p,24);
 arp_insert(spa,sha);
 if op==1&&tpa==OUR_IP{arp_reply(spa,&sha)}
}
fn dispatch_ip(frame:&[u8]){
 let ip=&frame[14..];
 if ip.len()<20{return}
 let ihl=((ip[0]&0x0f)as usize)*4;
 if ihl<20||ip.len()<ihl{return}
 let proto=ip[9];
 let src=be32(ip,12);
 let dst=be32(ip,16);
 if dst!=OUR_IP&&dst!=0xFFFF_FFFF{return}
 let payload=&ip[ihl..];
 match proto{
  IP_ICMP=>icmp_input(src,&[frame[6],frame[7],frame[8],frame[9],frame[10],frame[11]],payload),
  IP_UDP=>udp_input(src,payload),
  IP_TCP=>tcp_input(src,payload,[frame[6],frame[7],frame[8],frame[9],frame[10],frame[11]]),
  _=>{}
 }
}
fn dispatch(frame:&[u8]){
 if frame.len()<14{return}
 match be16(frame,12){
  ETH_ARP=>dispatch_arp(frame),
  ETH_IPV4=>dispatch_ip(frame),
  _=>{}
 }
}
/* Drains the RX ring and services TCP timers. Safe to call from the timer
   interrupt, syscall context and idle loops; all state is behind spin locks
   and never held across nested polls. */
pub fn poll(){
 if !crate::driver::virtio_net::ready(){return}
 let mut frame=[0u8;2048];
 while let Some(len)=crate::driver::virtio_net::poll_frame(&mut frame){
  dispatch(&frame[..len]);
 }
 tcp_tick();
}

//! Anonymous pipes: a fixed pool of ring buffers with blocking ends.
//! Readers and writers count open descriptions, giving Linux EOF/EPIPE behavior.
use crate::{sync::SpinLock,wait_queue::WaitQueue};
pub const CAPACITY:usize=4096;const MAX_PIPES:usize=16;
struct Pipe{used:bool,buf:[u8;CAPACITY],head:usize,len:usize,readers:u32,writers:u32,readq:WaitQueue,writeq:WaitQueue}
impl Pipe{
 const fn new()->Self{Self{used:false,buf:[0;CAPACITY],head:0,len:0,readers:0,writers:0,readq:WaitQueue::new(),writeq:WaitQueue::new()}}
 fn reset(&mut self){self.head=0;self.len=0;self.readers=0;self.writers=0;self.used=true;}
}
static PIPES:[SpinLock<Pipe>;MAX_PIPES]=[const{SpinLock::new(Pipe::new())};MAX_PIPES];
pub fn alloc()->Result<u8,i64>{for i in 0..MAX_PIPES{let mut p=PIPES[i].lock();if !p.used{p.reset();return Ok(i as u8)}}Err(crate::errno::EMFILE)}
pub fn add_reader(id:u8){PIPES[id as usize].lock().readers+=1}
pub fn add_writer(id:u8){PIPES[id as usize].lock().writers+=1}
pub fn close_reader(id:u8){let mut p=PIPES[id as usize].lock();p.readers=p.readers.saturating_sub(1);let empty=p.readers==0&&p.writers==0;if p.readers==0{p.writeq.wake_all();}if empty{p.used=false;}}
pub fn has_data(id:u8)->bool{PIPES[id as usize].lock().len>0}
pub fn has_space(id:u8)->bool{PIPES[id as usize].lock().len<CAPACITY}
pub fn close_writer(id:u8){let mut p=PIPES[id as usize].lock();p.writers=p.writers.saturating_sub(1);let empty=p.readers==0&&p.writers==0;if p.writers==0{p.readq.wake_all();}if empty{p.used=false;}}
fn copy_into(p:&mut Pipe,src:&[u8],n:usize){for i in 0..n{p.buf[(p.head+p.len+i)%CAPACITY]=src[i];}}
fn copy_out(p:&mut Pipe,dst:&mut[u8],n:usize){for i in 0..n{dst[i]=p.buf[p.head];p.head=(p.head+1)%CAPACITY;}p.len-=n;}
/* Blocking reads: EOF once every writer has closed, otherwise sleep on readq.
   The waiter is registered while the pipe lock is held, so a concurrent wake
   can never slip between the readiness check and the sleep. */
pub fn read(id:u8,dst:&mut[u8],nonblock:bool)->Result<usize,i64>{
 if dst.is_empty(){return Ok(0)}
 loop{
  let registered={let mut p=PIPES[id as usize].lock();
   if p.len>0{let n=core::cmp::min(p.len,dst.len());copy_out(&mut p,dst,n);p.writeq.wake_all();return Ok(n)}
   if p.writers==0{return Ok(0)}
   if nonblock{return Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}
   p.readq.register().is_ok()};
  if !registered{return Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}
  if crate::task::has_deliverable_signal(){return Err(crate::errno::EINTR)}
  crate::task::block_current();
 }
}
/* Blocking writes: all bytes or an error; EPIPE when every reader has closed. */
pub fn write(id:u8,src:&[u8],nonblock:bool)->Result<usize,i64>{
 let mut done=0usize;
 while done<src.len(){
  let registered={let mut p=PIPES[id as usize].lock();
   if p.readers==0{let _=crate::signal::send_self(crate::signal::SIGPIPE);return if done>0{Ok(done)}else{Err(crate::errno::EPIPE)}}
   if p.len<CAPACITY{let n=core::cmp::min(CAPACITY-p.len,src.len()-done);copy_into(&mut p,&src[done..],n);p.len+=n;done+=n;p.readq.wake_all();false}else if nonblock{return if done>0{Ok(done)}else{Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}}else{p.writeq.register().is_ok()}};
  if done==src.len(){break}
  if !registered{return Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)}
  if crate::task::has_deliverable_signal(){return Err(crate::errno::EINTR)}
  crate::task::block_current();
 }
 Ok(done)
}

//! POSIX interval timers (timer_create family) and legacy alarm/setitimer.
//! Signals follow the process-wide bitmask model: expiry marks the signal
//! pending on the owner and wakes it; si_value/si_overrun are stored on the
//! owning process for rt_sigtimedwait to report.
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32,Ordering};
use crate::sync::SpinLock;
use crate::errno;

const MAX_TIMERS:usize=32;
const MAX_ALARMS:usize=32;

#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
pub enum Notify{None,Signal{sig:i32,value:u64}}

#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
struct Timer{used:bool,id:u32,owner:u32,clock:i32,notify:Notify,active:bool,next:u64,interval:u64,overrun:u32}
const EMPTY_T:Timer=Timer{used:false,id:0,owner:0,clock:0,notify:Notify::None,active:false,next:0,interval:0,overrun:0};
static TIMERS:SpinLock<[Timer;MAX_TIMERS]>=SpinLock::new([EMPTY_T;MAX_TIMERS]);
static NEXT_ID:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(1);

#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
/* which: 0 ITIMER_REAL (next is an absolute tick), 1 ITIMER_VIRTUAL and
   2 ITIMER_PROF count down user (and kernel) ticks attributed to the pid. */
struct Alarm{used:bool,pid:u32,which:u8,active:bool,next:u64,interval:u64}
const EMPTY_A:Alarm=Alarm{used:false,pid:0,which:0,active:false,next:0,interval:0};
static ALARMS:SpinLock<[Alarm;MAX_ALARMS]>=SpinLock::new([EMPTY_A;MAX_ALARMS]);

/* CLOCK_REALTIME, CLOCK_MONOTONIC and CLOCK_BOOTTIME are distinguishable in
   Slop/MNU; the coarse/CPU clocks are rejected where Linux would reject them. */
pub fn valid_clock(clock:i32)->bool{matches!(clock,0|1|7)}

pub fn create(owner:u32,clock:i32,notify:Notify)->Result<u32,i64>{
 if !valid_clock(clock){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 let slot=t.iter().position(|e|!e.used).ok_or(errno::EAGAIN)?;
 let id=NEXT_ID.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);
 t[slot]=Timer{used:true,id,owner,clock,notify,active:false,next:0,interval:0,overrun:0};
 Ok(id)
}

fn find<'a>(t:&'a mut [Timer;MAX_TIMERS],id:u32,owner:u32)->Result<&'a mut Timer,i64>{
 t.iter_mut().find(|e|e.used&&e.id==id&&e.owner==owner).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)
}

pub fn settime(id:u32,owner:u32,active:bool,next:u64,interval:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 let e=find(&mut t,id,owner)?;
 e.active=active;e.next=next;e.interval=interval;e.overrun=0;
 Ok(())
}

/* Remaining ticks (0 when disarmed) and the interval. */
pub fn gettime(id:u32,owner:u32,now:u64)->Result<(u64,u64),i64>{
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 let e=find(&mut t,id,owner)?;
 Ok((if e.active{e.next.saturating_sub(now)}else{0},e.interval))
}

pub fn getoverrun(id:u32,owner:u32)->Result<u32,i64>{
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 let e=find(&mut t,id,owner)?;
 Ok(e.overrun)
}

pub fn delete(id:u32,owner:u32)->Result<(),i64>{
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 let e=find(&mut t,id,owner)?;
 *e=EMPTY_T;
 Ok(())
}

/* Removes every POSIX timer owned by pid (process exit, execve). */
pub fn release_pid(pid:u32){
 let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
 for e in t.iter_mut(){if e.used&&e.owner==pid{*e=EMPTY_T;}}
 drop(t);
 let mut a=ALARMS.lock();
 for e in a.iter_mut(){if e.used&&e.pid==pid{*e=EMPTY_A;}}
}

/* Sets or cancels one of the three per-process interval timers; returns the
   previous (remaining, interval) in ticks. A missing entry is created only
   when the new timer is armed. */
pub fn alarm_set(pid:u32,which:u8,active:bool,next:u64,interval:u64,now:u64)->Result<(u64,u64),i64>{
 let mut a=ALARMS.lock();
 if let Some(e)=a.iter_mut().find(|e|e.used&&e.pid==pid&&e.which==which){
  let old=(if e.active{if which==0{e.next.saturating_sub(now)}else{e.next}}else{0},e.interval);
  if active{e.active=true;e.next=next;e.interval=interval}else{e.active=false;e.next=0;e.interval=0;}
  return Ok(old)
 }
 if !active{return Ok((0,0))}
 let slot=a.iter().position(|e|!e.used).ok_or(errno::EAGAIN)?;
 a[slot]=Alarm{used:true,pid,which,active:true,next,interval};
 Ok((0,0))
}

pub fn alarm_get(pid:u32,which:u8,now:u64)->(u64,u64){
 let a=ALARMS.lock();
 if let Some(e)=a.iter().find(|e|e.used&&e.pid==pid&&e.which==which){
  if e.active{return(if which==0{e.next.saturating_sub(now)}else{e.next},e.interval)}
 }
 (0,0)
}

/* CPU-tick hook for ITIMER_VIRTUAL (user time only) and ITIMER_PROF (user
   plus kernel time). Fires at most one coalesced signal of each kind. */
pub fn cpu_tick(pid:u32,user:bool){
 let mut vfire=false;let mut pfire=false;
 {
  let mut a=ALARMS.lock();
  for e in a.iter_mut(){
   if !e.used||!e.active||e.pid!=pid||e.which==0{continue}
   if e.which==1&&!user{continue}
   e.next=e.next.saturating_sub(1);
   if e.next==0{
    if e.interval>0{e.next=e.interval}else{e.active=false}
    if e.which==1{vfire=true}else{pfire=true}
   }
  }
 }
 if vfire{let _=crate::task::timer_signal(pid,crate::signal::SIGVTALRM,0,0);}
 if pfire{let _=crate::task::timer_signal(pid,crate::signal::SIGPROF,0,0);}
}

/* Tick hook: advances expired timers and queues their signals after releasing
   the timer table lock (signal delivery takes the scheduler locks). */
pub fn tick(now:u64){
 let mut events:[(u32,u32,i32,u64,u32);MAX_TIMERS]=[(0,0,0,0,0);MAX_TIMERS];
 let mut n=0;
 {
  let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
  for e in t.iter_mut(){
   if !e.used||!e.active||e.next>now{continue}
   let expirations=if e.interval>0{1+(now-e.next)/e.interval}else{1};
   if e.interval>0{e.next=e.next.saturating_add(expirations*e.interval)}else{e.active=false;e.next=0;}
   if let Notify::Signal{sig,value}=e.notify{
    if n<events.len(){events[n]=(e.id,e.owner,sig,value,(expirations-1)as u32);n+=1}
   }
  }
 }
 let mut a=ALARMS.lock();
 let mut alarm_pids:[u32;MAX_ALARMS]=[0;MAX_ALARMS];
 let mut an=0;
 for e in a.iter_mut(){
  if !e.used||!e.active||e.which!=0||e.next>now{continue}
  let expirations=if e.interval>0{1+(now-e.next)/e.interval}else{1};
  if e.interval>0{e.next=e.next.saturating_add(expirations*e.interval)}else{e.active=false;e.next=0;}
  if an<alarm_pids.len(){alarm_pids[an]=e.pid;an+=1}
 }
 drop(a);
 for i in 0..n{
  let (id,owner,sig,value,extra)=events[i];
  let queued=crate::task::timer_signal(owner,sig,value,extra);
  let mut t=TIMERS.lock();
  if let Some(e)=t.iter_mut().find(|e|e.used&&e.id==id&&e.owner==owner){
   if queued{e.overrun=extra}else{e.overrun=e.overrun.saturating_add(extra+1)}
  }
 }
 for i in 0..an{
  let _=crate::task::timer_signal(alarm_pids[i],crate::signal::SIGALRM,0,0);
 }
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

//! Per-process state: private address space, VM ledger, brk and descriptors.
use crate::{arch::x86_64::{paging::{PhysAccess,PageTableRoot,PRESENT,WRITABLE,USER,NX},phys::Identity},fd::FdTable,vm::{Map,VmFlags,VmKind}};
const PAGE:u64=4096;
const MAX_SECCOMP_FILTERS:usize=64;
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]struct SeccompFilterRef{frame:u64,refs:u32}
const EMPTY_SECCOMP_FILTER:SeccompFilterRef=SeccompFilterRef{frame:0,refs:0};
static SECCOMP_FILTER_REFS:crate::sync::SpinLock<[SeccompFilterRef;MAX_SECCOMP_FILTERS]>=crate::sync::SpinLock::new([EMPTY_SECCOMP_FILTER;MAX_SECCOMP_FILTERS]);
/* Slot 129 keeps the heap and anonymous mappings out of the firmware identity map. */
/* The program break lives in PML4 slot 131, alone in its page table
   slot: the anonymous mappings use slot 130 and the images slot 128 and
   129, so the regions never share a page table entry. */
pub const BRK_BASE:u64=0x0000_4180_0000_0000;
/* The heap must stay inside its own PML4 slot, otherwise it would start to
   share page-table entries with the neighbouring private regions. */
pub const BRK_LIMIT:u64=0x0000_4200_0000_0000;
pub const BRK_SLOT:usize=((BRK_BASE>>39)&511)as usize;
pub fn slot_of(va:u64)->usize{((va>>39)&511)as usize}
pub fn seccomp_filter_register(frame:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 let mut refs=SECCOMP_FILTER_REFS.lock();let slot=refs.iter().position(|entry|entry.frame==0).ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;refs[slot]=SeccompFilterRef{frame,refs:1};Ok(())
}
pub fn seccomp_filter_acquire(frame:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 let mut refs=SECCOMP_FILTER_REFS.lock();let entry=refs.iter_mut().find(|entry|entry.frame==frame&&frame!=0).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;entry.refs=entry.refs.checked_add(1).ok_or(crate::errno::EOVERFLOW)?;Ok(())
}
pub fn seccomp_filter_release(frame:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 let release={let mut refs=SECCOMP_FILTER_REFS.lock();let entry=refs.iter_mut().find(|entry|entry.frame==frame&&frame!=0).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;if entry.refs>1{entry.refs-=1;false}else{*entry=EMPTY_SECCOMP_FILTER;true}};
 if release{crate::memory::free_frame(frame)?}Ok(())
}

 /* Virtual size in bytes: committed image segments, stack, brk and ledger areas. */
 pub fn vma_bytes(&self)->u64{let mut total=0u64;for s in &self.segs[..self.nsegs]{total=total.saturating_add(s.end-s.start)}if self.stack_top>self.stack_base{total=total.saturating_add(self.stack_top-self.stack_base)}let brk_span=Self::up(self.brk).map(|b|b as u64).unwrap_or(0).saturating_sub(BRK_BASE);total=total.saturating_add(brk_span);let stats=self.vm.resource_stats();total.saturating_add(stats.bytes as u64)}
 /* Resident user pages across segments, stack, brk and ledger areas. */
 pub fn resident_pages(&self)->u64{
  let root=self.root();let phys=Identity;let mut resident=0u64;let mut scan=|start:u64,end:u64,resident:&mut u64|{let mut va=start;while va<end{if root.query(&phys,va).map(|(_,f)|f&PRESENT!=0&&f&USER!=0).unwrap_or(false){*resident+=1}va+=PAGE;}};
  for s in &self.segs[..self.nsegs]{scan(s.start,s.end,&mut resident)}
  if self.stack_top>self.stack_base{scan(self.stack_base,self.stack_top,&mut resident)}
  for i in 0..self.vm.resource_stats().areas{if let Some(a)=self.vm.area(i){scan(a.start as u64,a.end as u64,&mut resident)}}
  resident
 }
 /* madvise(DONTNEED/FREE): drop physical frames but keep the reservation. */
 pub fn madvise(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize,advice:u64)->Result<usize,i64>{
  if addr&4095!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
  if len==0{return Ok(0)}
  let size=Self::up(len as u64)?;
  match advice{
   /* MADV_DONTNEED: the pages read as zero again on the next touch. */
   4=>{self.unmap_pages(addr as u64,size as u64)?;Ok(0)}
   /* MADV_FREE keeps the content until the page is written, MADV_RANDOM,
      MADV_SEQUENTIAL, MADV_WILLNEED, MADV_HUGEPAGE, MADV_NOHUGEPAGE,
      MADV_DONTDUMP, MADV_WIPEONFORK, MADV_DONTFORK and MADV_COLD are
      advisory and have no effect here. */
   1|2|3|6|7|14|16|17|18|20=>Ok(0),
   /* MADV_PAGEOUT needs a swap device, which this kernel does not have. */
   21=>Err(crate::errno::ENOSYS),
   /* Linux rejects an unknown advice with EINVAL. */
   _=>Err(crate::errno::EINVAL),
  }
 }
 pub fn cwd_str(&self)->&[u8]{&self.cwd[..self.cwd_len]}
 pub fn set_cwd(&mut self,p:&str)->bool{let b=p.as_bytes();if b.len()>=128{return false}self.cwd=[0;128];self.cwd[..b.len()].copy_from_slice(b);self.cwd_len=b.len().max(1);true}
 pub fn root_str(&self)->&[u8]{&self.root[..self.root_len]}

 pub fn reset_exec_state(&mut self){self.brk=BRK_BASE;self.vm=Map::new();self.clear_child_tid=0;self.handlers=crate::signal::handlers_dfl();self.sigframe=0;self.robust_list_head=0;self.robust_list_len=0;}
 fn pte_flags(prot:u64)->Result<u64,i64>{if prot&!7!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}Ok(USER|if prot&2!=0{WRITABLE}else{0}|if prot&4==0{NX}else{0})}
 fn vm_flags(prot:u64)->Result<VmFlags,i64>{let mut f=VmFlags::USER;let _=Self::pte_flags(prot)?;if prot&1!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::READ.0}if prot&2!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::WRITE.0}if prot&4!=0{f.0|=VmFlags::EXEC.0}f.0|=VmFlags::ANON.0;Ok(f)}
  fn unmap_pages(&self,start:u64,len:u64)->Result<(),i64>{let root=self.root();let phys=Identity;let end=start.checked_add(len).ok_or(crate::errno::EINVAL)?;let mut va=start;while va<end{match root.unmap_page(&phys,va){Ok(pa)=>{if self.vm.flags_at(va as usize).unwrap_or(VmFlags(0)).contains(VmFlags::EXTERNAL){crate::fd::iouring_mapping_release(pa)}else{crate::memory::release_frame(pa)?}}Err(e)=>{if e!=crate::errno::ENOENT{return Err(e)}}}va+=PAGE;}Ok(())}
  fn unmap_external_mappings(root:&PageTableRoot,vm:&Map){let access=Identity;for i in 0..vm.resource_stats().areas{if let Some(area)=vm.area(i){if !area.flags.contains(VmFlags::EXTERNAL){continue}let mut va=area.start as u64;while va<area.end as u64{if let Ok(frame)=root.unmap_page(&access,va){crate::fd::iouring_mapping_release(frame)}va+=PAGE;}}}}
 fn up(v:u64)->Result<usize,i64>{v.checked_add(PAGE-1).map(|x|(x&!(PAGE-1))as usize).ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}
 pub fn brk(&mut self,request:u64)->u64{let old=self.brk;if request==0{return old}if request<BRK_BASE||request>=BRK_LIMIT||request>=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return old}let old_page=match Self::up(old){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return old};let new_page=match Self::up(request){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return old};if new_page>old_page{let len=new_page-old_page;let flags=match Self::vm_flags(3){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return old};/* The heap area grows: replace the old brk area instead of colliding with it. */
  if self.vm.replace(old_page,len,flags,VmKind::Brk,false).is_err(){return old}}else if new_page<old_page{return old}self.brk=request;request}
 pub fn mmap(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize,prot:u64,flags:u64,fd:i64,offset:u64)->Result<usize,i64>{
  const MAP_FIXED:u64=0x10;const MAP_ANON:u64=0x20;const MAP_PRIVATE:u64=0x02;const MAP_SHARED:u64=0x01;const MAP_POPULATE:u64=0x8000;
  if len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
  let map_type=flags&(MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_SHARED);
  if map_type==0||map_type==(MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_SHARED){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
  let anon=flags&MAP_ANON!=0;
 let iouring=!anon && self.fds.get(fd as usize).map(|id|matches!(crate::fd::kind(id),Some(crate::fd::OpenKind::IoUring))).unwrap_or(false);
 if anon&&(fd!=-1){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 if !anon && !iouring &&(fd<0||offset%4096!=0){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 if flags&!(MAP_FIXED|MAP_ANON|MAP_PRIVATE|MAP_SHARED|MAP_POPULATE|0x20000|0x100)!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 if !anon && !iouring &&(flags&MAP_SHARED!=0){return Err(crate::errno::ENODEV)}
  let size=Self::up(len as u64)?;
  let mut vf=Self::vm_flags(prot)?;
  if !iouring&&flags&MAP_SHARED!=0{vf.0|=VmFlags::SHARED.0;}
  if iouring{if flags&MAP_SHARED==0||size!=PAGE as usize{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}vf.0|=VmFlags::EXTERNAL.0;}
 let (vma_kind,vma_cow)=if iouring {
  (VmKind::Anon,false)
 }else if anon {
  (VmKind::Anon,false)
 }else{
  let id=self.fds.get(fd as usize).ok_or(crate::errno::EBADF)?;
  let inode=match crate::fd::kind(id){Some(crate::fd::OpenKind::Inode(n))=>n,_=>{return Err(crate::errno::ENODEV)}};
  if prot&2!=0{return Err(crate::errno::ENODEV)}
  (VmKind::File{ino:inode.ino,offset}, flags&MAP_PRIVATE!=0)
 };
 let start=if flags&MAP_FIXED!=0{if addr&4095!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
  /* MAP_FIXED replaces the previous mapping, so the old pages go first. */
  self.unmap_pages(addr as u64,size as u64)?;
  self.vm.replace(addr,size,vf,vma_kind,vma_cow)?;addr}else{self.vm.allocate(size,vf,vma_kind,vma_cow)?};
 if iouring{
  let id=self.fds.get(fd as usize).ok_or(crate::errno::EBADF)?;
  let frame=match offset as u64 {0=>crate::fd::iouring_sq_ring(id),0x8000000=>crate::fd::iouring_cq_ring(id),0x10000000=>crate::fd::iouring_sqes(id),_=>0};
   if frame==0{let _=self.vm.remove(start,size);return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
   let pte=Self::pte_flags(prot)?;
   if let Err(e)=self.root().map_page(&crate::arch::x86_64::phys::Identity,start as u64,frame,pte){let _=self.vm.remove(start,size);return Err(e)}
   if let Err(e)=crate::fd::iouring_mapping_acquire(id){let _=self.root().unmap_page(&crate::arch::x86_64::phys::Identity,start as u64);let _=self.vm.remove(start,size);return Err(e)}
 }
 /* Anonymous, file-backed and brk pages are demand-paged by the fault
    handler: the VMA ledger keeps kind and offset, so mmap only reserves. */
 if flags&MAP_POPULATE!=0&&!iouring{
  let writable=vf.contains(VmFlags::WRITE);
  let mut va=start as u64;let end=(start+size)as u64;
  while va<end{
   if !(self.root().query(&Identity,va).map(|(_,f)|f&PRESENT!=0&&(!writable||f&WRITABLE!=0)).unwrap_or(false)){
    if self.handle_page_fault(va,writable).is_err(){let _=self.munmap(start,size);return Err(crate::errno::ENOMEM)}
   }
   va+=PAGE;
  }
 }
 Ok(start)
 }
  /* Grows or shrinks an anonymous mapping: allocate the new range, copy the
     live bytes across, then release the old range. */
  pub fn mremap(&mut self,old:usize,old_size:usize,new_size:usize,flags:u64,new_addr:u64)->Result<usize,i64>{
   const MREMAP_MAYMOVE:u64=1; const MREMAP_FIXED:u64=2;
   if old%4096!=0||old_size==0||new_size==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
   if flags&!(MREMAP_MAYMOVE|MREMAP_FIXED)!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
   if self.vm.flags_at(old).unwrap_or(VmFlags(0)).contains(VmFlags::EXTERNAL){return Err(crate::errno::EOPNOTSUPP)}
   let vf=Self::vm_flags(3)?;
   let dst=if flags&MREMAP_FIXED!=0{
    if new_addr as usize %4096!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
    let _=self.munmap(new_addr as usize,new_size);
    if let Err(e)=self.vm.insert(new_addr as usize,new_size,vf,VmKind::Anon,false){return Err(e)}
    new_addr as usize
   }else{
    self.vm.allocate(new_size,vf,VmKind::Anon,false)?
   };
   /* Runs under with_current_process, so usercopy cannot be reused here:
      fault both sides in through the process itself, then copy the frames. */
   let root=self.root();let phys=Identity;
   let ok=|va:u64,write:bool|root.query(&phys,va).map(|(_,f)|f&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT!=0&&f&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::USER!=0&&(!write||f&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE!=0)).unwrap_or(false);
   let total=core::cmp::min(old_size,new_size);
   let mut done=0usize;
   while done<total{
    let n=core::cmp::min(PAGE as usize,total-done);
    if !ok((old+done) as u64,false)&&self.handle_page_fault((old+done) as u64,false).is_err(){let _=self.munmap(old,old_size);return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
    if !ok((dst+done) as u64,true)&&self.handle_page_fault((dst+done) as u64,true).is_err(){let _=self.munmap(old,old_size);return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
    let (spa,_)=match root.query(&phys,(old+done) as u64){Some(v)=>v,None=>{let _=self.munmap(old,old_size);return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}};
    let (dpa,_)=match root.query(&phys,(dst+done) as u64){Some(v)=>v,None=>{let _=self.munmap(old,old_size);return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}};
    unsafe{phys.bytes(dpa,n).copy_from_slice(phys.bytes(spa,n))}
    done+=PAGE as usize;
   }
   let _=self.munmap(old,old_size);
   Ok(dst)
  }
 pub fn munmap(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize)->Result<(),i64>{if addr&4095!=0||len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}let size=Self::up(len as u64)?;self.unmap_pages(addr as u64,size as u64)?;self.vm.remove(addr,size)}
 pub fn mprotect(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize,prot:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 if addr&4095!=0||len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}let size=Self::up(len as u64)?;let f=Self::vm_flags(prot)?;let root=self.root();let phys=Identity;let flags=Self::pte_flags(prot)?;let mut va=addr as u64;let end=va+size as u64;while va<end{
  /* Demand-paged pages have no PTE yet: protection lives in the VMA ledger
     until the fault handler installs the mapping with the recorded flags. */
  /* Shared COW pages must stay read-only; a later write fault upgrades them. */
  let cow=self.vm.find_vma(va as usize).map(|a|a.cow).unwrap_or(false);
  if !(cow&&flags&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE!=0){
   if let Err(e)=root.protect_page(&phys,va,flags){if e!=crate::errno::ENOENT{return Err(e)}}
  }
  va+=PAGE;
 }
 /* Execve images have no VMA ledger entry; treat that as bookkeeping only. */
 let _=self.vm.protect(addr,size,f);Ok(())}
 pub fn mapped_for_test(&self,addr:u64,write:bool)->bool{self.root().query(&Identity,addr).map(|(_,f)|f&PRESENT!=0&&f&USER!=0&&(!write||f&WRITABLE!=0)).unwrap_or(false)}
 pub fn mlock(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize)->Result<usize,i64>{if addr&4095!=0||len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}self.vm.set_locked_range(addr,len,true)?;Ok(0)}
 pub fn munlock(&mut self,addr:usize,len:usize)->Result<usize,i64>{if addr&4095!=0||len==0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}self.vm.set_locked_range(addr,len,false)?;Ok(0)}
 pub fn handle_page_fault(&mut self,addr:u64,write:bool)->Result<(),()>{
  if addr as usize >= crate::usercopy::USER_MAX {return Err(())}
  let page=(addr as usize)&!4095;
  let root=self.root();
  let phys=crate::arch::x86_64::phys::Identity;
  // Present PTE: fork marks every shared private page read-only, so a write
  // fault here is COW unless the mapping itself is read-only on purpose.
  if let Some((pa,pte_flags))=root.query(&phys,addr){
   if pte_flags & crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT != 0 {
    if write && pte_flags & crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE == 0 && pte_flags & crate::arch::x86_64::paging::USER != 0 {
     if let Some(vma)=self.vm.find_vma(addr as usize){
      if !vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::WRITE){return Err(())}
     }
     let frame=pa & crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR;
     if crate::memory::refcount(frame)>1 {
      let new=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(())?;
      unsafe{phys.table(new).copy_from_slice(phys.table(frame));}
      let _=root.unmap_page(&phys,page as u64);
      root.map_page(&phys,page as u64,new,pte_flags & !crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR | crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE).map_err(|_|())?;
      let _=crate::memory::refcount_dec(frame);
     }else{
      /* Sole owner (for example after the COW sibling exited): promote in place. */
      root.protect_page(&phys,page as u64,pte_flags & !crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR | crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE).map_err(|_|())?;
     }
     return Ok(())
    }
    return Err(())
   }
  }
  let vma=self.vm.find_vma(addr as usize).ok_or(())?;
  // Not present: allocate/map.
  let mut prot=0u64;
  if vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::READ){prot|=1}
  if vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::WRITE){prot|=2}
  if vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::EXEC){prot|=4}
  if (!vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::READ)&&!vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::WRITE)&&!vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::EXEC))||(write&&!vma.flags.contains(VmFlags::WRITE)){return Err(())}
  let pf=Self::pte_flags(prot).map_err(|_|())?;
  match vma.kind {
   VmKind::File{ino,offset}=>{
    let frame=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(())?;
    unsafe{phys.table(frame).fill(0);}
    let file_off=offset + (page as u64 - vma.start as u64);
    let mut buf=[0u8;4096];
    if let Some(inode)=crate::vfs::inode_by_ino(ino){let _=crate::vfs::read_off(inode,file_off,&mut buf);}
    unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts_mut(crate::arch::x86_64::phys::phys_to_virt(frame)as *mut u8,4096).copy_from_slice(&buf);}
    root.map_page(&phys,page as u64,frame,pf).map_err(|_|())?
   }
   _=>{
    let frame=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(())?;
    unsafe{phys.table(frame).fill(0);}
    root.map_page(&phys,page as u64,frame,pf).map_err(|_|())?
   }
  }
  Ok(())
 }
 /* COW fork: shares the private frames of the caller with a fresh CR3, then
    revokes write access on both sides so the next store faults into the COW
    path. Frames carry refcounts; teardown releases them through release_frame. */
 pub fn clone_address_space(&self)->Result<u64,i64>{
  let access=Identity;
  let mut all=[self.image_slot,self.stack_slot,self.mmap_slot,self.interp_slot,BRK_SLOT];let mut n=0usize;
  for i in 0..all.len(){let s=all[i];if !all[..n].contains(&s){all[n]=s;n+=1;}}
  let slots=&all[..n];
  let child=self.root().clone_bootstrap(&access,slots)?;
  for &slot in slots{if let Err(e)=share_slot(&self.root(),&access,&child,slot){for &done in slots{let _=child.destroy_private_slot(&access,done);}let _=crate::memory::free_frame(child.pml4);return Err(e)}}
  for &slot in slots{make_slot_readonly(&self.root(),&access,slot)}
  /* MAP_SHARED pages were never meant to be copied: re-enable writes on
     both sides so the shared frames stay shared (no COW on either side). */
  self.vm.shared_writable_ranges(|s,e|{
   writable_range(&access,&self.root(),s as u64,e as u64);
   writable_range(&access,&child,s as u64,e as u64);
  });
  /* Stale writable TLB entries would bypass the COW fault; reload CR3. */
  unsafe{PageTableRoot{pml4:self.cr3}.activate()}
  Ok(child.pml4)
 }
  pub fn fork_child(&self,cr3:u64)->Result<Self,i64>{if self.seccomp_filter!=0{seccomp_filter_acquire(self.seccomp_filter as u64)?;}Ok(Self{cr3,bootstrap_cr3:self.bootstrap_cr3,image_slot:self.image_slot,stack_slot:self.stack_slot,mmap_slot:self.mmap_slot,interp_slot:self.interp_slot,vm:self.vm.clone(),brk:self.brk,clear_child_tid:0,fds:self.fds.clone_incref(),pending:self.pending,sigmask:self.sigmask,pending_from:self.pending_from,sig_restart:false,sig_syscall:0,sig_result:0,si_code:self.si_code,si_value:self.si_value,si_overrun:self.si_overrun,robust_list_head:self.robust_list_head,robust_list_len:self.robust_list_len,handlers:self.handlers,sigframe:0,exe_path:self.exe_path,exe_len:self.exe_len,cwd:self.cwd,cwd_len:self.cwd_len,root:self.root,root_len:self.root_len,name:self.name,hostname:self.hostname,
 cmdline:self.cmdline,cmdline_len:self.cmdline_len,environ:self.environ,environ_len:self.environ_len,segs:self.segs,nsegs:self.nsegs,stack_base:self.stack_base,stack_top:self.stack_top,
 seccomp_filter:self.seccomp_filter,seccomp_filter_len:self.seccomp_filter_len,uid:self.uid,gid:self.gid,euid:self.euid,egid:self.egid,suid:self.suid,sgid:self.sgid,fsuid:self.fsuid,fsgid:self.fsgid,cap_inh:self.cap_inh,cap_prm:self.cap_prm,cap_eff:self.cap_eff,cap_bset:self.cap_bset,ns_uts:self.ns_uts,ns_ipc:self.ns_ipc,ns_mnt:self.ns_mnt,ns_pid:self.ns_pid,ns_net:self.ns_net,ns_user:self.ns_user,ns_cgroup:self.ns_cgroup,ns_time:self.ns_time,seccomp_mode:self.seccomp_mode,no_new_privs:self.no_new_privs,umask:self.umask,utime_ticks:0,stime_ticks:0,cutime_ticks:0,cstime_ticks:0,groups:self.groups,ngroups:self.ngroups})}
 /* Releases address space and descriptors after an unrecoverable spawn failure. */
  pub fn abandon(&mut self){let access=Identity;let root=self.root();Self::unmap_external_mappings(&root,&self.vm);for &slot in &[self.image_slot,self.stack_slot,self.mmap_slot,self.interp_slot,BRK_SLOT]{let _=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,slot);}let _=crate::memory::free_frame(self.cr3);if self.seccomp_filter!=0{let _=seccomp_filter_release(self.seccomp_filter as u64);self.seccomp_filter=0;self.seccomp_filter_len=0;}self.fds.close_all();}
 /* Runs on exit and on fatal user faults; ends on the bootstrap tables. */
  pub fn teardown_exit(&mut self,status:i32,vector:u64){
   let fd_descriptors=self.fds.descriptor_count();
   let fd_descriptions=self.fds.open_description_count();
   let vm=self.vm.resource_stats();
   let tasks=crate::task::resource_stats();
   crate::println!("resources: exit pid={} fds={} open_files={} mmap_areas={} mmap_pages={} tasks_active={} tasks_zombie={}",crate::task::current_pid(),fd_descriptors,fd_descriptions,vm.areas,vm.pages,tasks.active,tasks.zombies);
   let bootstrap=PageTableRoot{pml4:self.bootstrap_cr3};unsafe{bootstrap.activate()}
   let access=Identity;let root=self.root();Self::unmap_external_mappings(&root,&self.vm);
   let mut cleanup_ok=true;
   if let Err(e)=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,self.image_slot){crate::println!("warning: image slot teardown failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}
   if self.stack_slot!=self.image_slot{if let Err(e)=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,self.stack_slot){crate::println!("warning: stack slot teardown failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}}
   if self.mmap_slot!=self.image_slot&&self.mmap_slot!=self.stack_slot&&self.mmap_slot!=self.interp_slot{if let Err(e)=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,self.mmap_slot){crate::println!("warning: mmap slot teardown failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}}
   if self.interp_slot!=self.image_slot&&self.interp_slot!=self.stack_slot&&self.interp_slot!=self.mmap_slot{if let Err(e)=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,self.interp_slot){crate::println!("warning: interpreter slot teardown failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}}
   if BRK_SLOT!=self.image_slot&&BRK_SLOT!=self.stack_slot&&BRK_SLOT!=self.mmap_slot&&BRK_SLOT!=self.interp_slot{if let Err(e)=root.destroy_private_slot(&access,BRK_SLOT){crate::println!("warning: brk slot teardown failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}}
   if let Err(e)=crate::memory::free_frame(self.cr3){crate::println!("warning: process PML4 recycle failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}
   if self.seccomp_filter!=0{if let Err(e)=seccomp_filter_release(self.seccomp_filter as u64){crate::println!("warning: seccomp filter release failed errno={}",e);cleanup_ok=false}self.seccomp_filter=0;self.seccomp_filter_len=0;}
   crate::record_lock::release_pid(crate::task::current_pid());
   let closed_fds=self.fds.close_all();
   self.vm.clear();
   if closed_fds!=fd_descriptors||self.fds.descriptor_count()!=0||self.vm.resource_stats()!=crate::vm::ResourceStats::default(){cleanup_ok=false}
   if cleanup_ok{crate::println!("resources: reclaimed pid={} fds={} mmap_areas={} mmap_pages={}",crate::task::current_pid(),closed_fds,vm.areas,vm.pages)}else{crate::println!("resources: cleanup incomplete pid={} fds_closed={} mmap_areas={} mmap_pages={}",crate::task::current_pid(),closed_fds,vm.areas,vm.pages)}
   crate::println!("process address space reclaimed status={} vector={}",status,vector);
 }
 /* Swaps in a freshly committed exec image and tears down the old one. */
  pub fn replace_image(&mut self,new_cr3:u64,image_slot:usize,stack_slot:usize,interp_slot:usize)->Result<(),i64>{
   let old=(self.cr3,self.image_slot,self.stack_slot,self.mmap_slot,self.interp_slot);let old_vm=self.vm.clone();
  unsafe{PageTableRoot{pml4:new_cr3}.activate()}
  crate::task::bind_current_address_space(new_cr3,crate::task::current_kernel_stack_top());
  self.cr3=new_cr3;self.image_slot=image_slot;self.stack_slot=stack_slot;self.interp_slot=interp_slot;
   let access=Identity;let root=PageTableRoot{pml4:old.0};Self::unmap_external_mappings(&root,&old_vm);
  if root.destroy_private_slot(&access,old.1).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec image teardown failed")}
   if old.2!=old.1&&root.destroy_private_slot(&access,old.2).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec stack teardown failed")}
   if old.3!=old.1&&old.3!=old.2&&root.destroy_private_slot(&access,old.3).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec mmap teardown failed")}
   if old.4!=old.1&&old.4!=old.2&&old.4!=old.3&&root.destroy_private_slot(&access,old.4).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec interpreter teardown failed")}
   if BRK_SLOT!=old.1&&BRK_SLOT!=old.2&&BRK_SLOT!=old.3&&BRK_SLOT!=old.4&&root.destroy_private_slot(&access,BRK_SLOT).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec brk teardown failed")}
  if crate::memory::free_frame(old.0).is_err(){crate::println!("warning: old exec PML4 recycle failed")}Ok(())
 }
}
impl Drop for Process{fn drop(&mut self){if self.seccomp_filter!=0{let _=seccomp_filter_release(self.seccomp_filter as u64);self.seccomp_filter=0;self.seccomp_filter_len=0;}}}
pub fn fs_base()->u64{crate::task::fs_base()}
pub fn set_fs_base(v:u64)->bool{if v<crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{crate::task::set_current_fs_base(v);true}else{false}}
/* Re-asserts WRITABLE on present leaves of [start,end) so shared anonymous
   pages keep behaving like one frame in both address spaces. */
fn writable_range(a:&Identity,root:&PageTableRoot,start:u64,end:u64){
 use crate::arch::x86_64::paging::{PRESENT,WRITABLE,ADDR};
 let pml4=unsafe{a.table(root.pml4)};
 let mut va=start&!(4096-1);
 while va<end{
  let e4=pml4[(va>>39&511)as usize];
  if e4&PRESENT!=0{
   let pdpt=unsafe{a.table(e4&ADDR)};
   let e3=pdpt[(va>>30&511)as usize];
   if e3&PRESENT!=0&&e3&(1<<7)==0{
    let pd=unsafe{a.table(e3&ADDR)};
    let e2=pd[(va>>21&511)as usize];
    if e2&PRESENT!=0&&e2&(1<<7)==0{
     let pt=unsafe{a.table(e2&ADDR)};
     let i1=(va>>12&511)as usize;
     let e1=pt[i1];
     if e1&PRESENT!=0{pt[i1]=e1|WRITABLE;}
    }
   }
  }
  va+=4096;
 }
}
fn make_slot_readonly(root:&PageTableRoot,a:&Identity,slot:usize){
 let pml4=unsafe{a.table(root.pml4)};
 let top=pml4[slot];
 if top&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT==0{return;}
 let pdpt=unsafe{a.table(top&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR)};
 for i in 0..512{
  let e3=pdpt[i];
  if e3&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT==0{continue;}
  if e3&(1<<7)!=0{continue;}
  let pd=unsafe{a.table(e3&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR)};
  for j in 0..512{
   let e2=pd[j];
   if e2&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT==0{continue;}
   if e2&(1<<7)!=0{continue;}
   let pt=unsafe{a.table(e2&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::ADDR)};
   for k in 0..512{
    if pt[k]&crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PRESENT!=0{pt[k]&=!crate::arch::x86_64::paging::WRITABLE;}
   }
  }
 }
}
fn share_slot(src:&PageTableRoot,a:&Identity,dst:&PageTableRoot,slot:usize)->Result<(),i64>{
 use crate::arch::x86_64::paging::{PRESENT,WRITABLE,USER,ADDR,NX};
 let pml4_src=unsafe{a.table(src.pml4)};
 let top=pml4_src[slot];
 if top&PRESENT==0{return Ok(())}
 let pdpt_pa=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;
 unsafe{a.table(pdpt_pa).fill(0)}
 unsafe{a.table(dst.pml4)[slot]=pdpt_pa|PRESENT|WRITABLE|USER}
 let pdpt_src=unsafe{a.table(top&ADDR)};
 for i in 0..512{
  let e3=pdpt_src[i];
  if e3&PRESENT==0{continue;}
  if e3&(1<<7)!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
  let pd_pa=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;
  unsafe{a.table(pd_pa).fill(0)}
  unsafe{a.table(pdpt_pa)[i]=pd_pa|PRESENT|WRITABLE|USER}
  let pd_src=unsafe{a.table(e3&ADDR)};
  for j in 0..512{
   let e2=pd_src[j];
   if e2&PRESENT==0{continue;}
   if e2&(1<<7)!=0{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
   let pt_pa=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(crate::errno::ENOMEM)?;
   unsafe{a.table(pt_pa).fill(0)}
   unsafe{a.table(pd_pa)[j]=pt_pa|PRESENT|WRITABLE|USER}
   let pt_src=unsafe{a.table(e2&ADDR)};
   for k in 0..512{
    let e1=pt_src[k];
    if e1&PRESENT==0{continue;}
    let pa=e1&ADDR;
    crate::memory::refcount_inc(pa);
    /* Shared leaves start read-only so the first store on either side faults. */
    let flags=((e1&0xfff)|(e1&NX))&!WRITABLE;
    unsafe{a.table(pt_pa)[k]=pa|flags;}
   }
  }
 }
 Ok(())
}

//! POSIX and open-file-description record locks behind fcntl(2).
//! POSIX locks (F_GETLK/F_SETLK/F_SETLKW) are owned by the process and are
//! all released when any descriptor for the inode closes in that process.
//! OFD locks (F_OFD_*) are owned by the open-file description and survive
//! descriptor closes until the last reference goes away. Ranges are stored
//! half-open [start, end) with u64::MAX standing for end-of-file; a request
//! replaces the owner's overlapping ranges, splitting or trimming as needed.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,wait_queue::WaitQueue};

pub const F_RDLCK:i16=0;pub const F_WRLCK:i16=1;pub const F_UNLCK:i16=2;
const MAX_LOCKS:usize=64;

#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq,Eq)]pub enum Owner{Pid(u32),Ofd(u32)}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]struct Entry{used:bool,ino:u64,owner:Owner,exclusive:bool,start:u64,end:u64}
const EMPTY:Entry=Entry{used:false,ino:0,owner:Owner::Pid(0),exclusive:false,start:0,end:0};
static LOCKS:SpinLock<[Entry;MAX_LOCKS]>=SpinLock::new([EMPTY;MAX_LOCKS]);
/* One queue for everyone blocked in F_SETLKW; every release wakes it and the
   waiters re-check, which is conservative but keeps the bookkeeping small. */
static BLOCKED:SpinLock<WaitQueue>=SpinLock::new(WaitQueue::new());

fn overlap(a0:u64,a1:u64,b0:u64,b1:u64)->bool{a0<b1&&b0<a1}

/* First entry owned by someone else that blocks the request. */
fn conflict(ino:u64,owner:Owner,exclusive:bool,start:u64,end:u64)->Option<Entry>{
 for e in LOCKS.lock().iter(){
  if e.used&&e.ino==ino&&e.owner!=owner&&overlap(e.start,e.end,start,end)&&(exclusive||e.exclusive){return Some(*e)}
 }
 None
}

/* Trims the owner's locks so none overlap [start, end); returns whether
   anything changed. A fully covered run is removed; a partially covered one
   is clipped, splitting in two when the request lies strictly inside. */
fn clear_range(ino:u64,owner:Owner,start:u64,end:u64)->bool{
 let mut l=LOCKS.lock();let mut changed=false;let mut extra=-1i32;
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let e=l[i];
  if !e.used||e.ino!=ino||e.owner!=owner||!overlap(e.start,e.end,start,end){continue}
  changed=true;
  let head=e.start<start;
  let tail=e.end>end;
  if head&&tail{
   if extra<0{extra=match (0..MAX_LOCKS).find(|&j|!l[j].used){Some(j)=>j as i32,None=>{l[i]=EMPTY;continue}}}
   l[i]=Entry{used:true,ino,owner,exclusive:e.exclusive,start:e.start,end:start};
   l[extra as usize]=Entry{used:true,ino,owner,exclusive:e.exclusive,start:end,end:e.end};
   extra=-1;
  }else if head{l[i]=Entry{used:true,ino,owner,exclusive:e.exclusive,start:e.start,end:start};}
  else if tail{l[i]=Entry{used:true,ino,owner,exclusive:e.exclusive,start:end,end:e.end};}
  else{l[i]=EMPTY;}
 }
 changed
}

fn place(ino:u64,owner:Owner,exclusive:bool,start:u64,end:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 if start>=end{return Ok(())}
 let mut l=LOCKS.lock();
 match (0..MAX_LOCKS).find(|&i|!l[i].used){Some(i)=>{l[i]=Entry{used:true,ino,owner,exclusive,start,end};Ok(())},None=>Err(errno::ENOLCK)}
}

fn wake_waiters(){BLOCKED.lock().wake_all();}

/* Applies one F_SETLK-style request; blocking selects F_SETLKW semantics,
   which waits for a conflict to go away and returns EINTR on a signal. */
pub fn set(ino:u64,owner:Owner,exclusive:bool,start:u64,end:u64,blocking:bool)->Result<(),i64>{
 loop{
  if let Some(_)=conflict(ino,owner,exclusive,start,end){
   if !blocking{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
   if crate::task::has_deliverable_signal(){return Err(errno::EINTR)}
   if !BLOCKED.lock().register().is_ok(){return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
   crate::task::block_current();
   if crate::task::has_deliverable_signal(){return Err(errno::EINTR)}
   continue;
  }
  clear_range(ino,owner,start,end);
  place(ino,owner,exclusive,start,end)?;
  wake_waiters();
  return Ok(());
 }
}

/* Removes the owner's locks over [start, end) (F_UNLCK). */
pub fn unlock(ino:u64,owner:Owner,start:u64,end:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 if clear_range(ino,owner,start,end){wake_waiters();}
 Ok(())
}

/* Describes the first blocking lock for F_GETLK output:
   (owner pid, exclusive, start, end); OFD-owned locks report pid 0. */
pub fn query(ino:u64,owner:Owner,exclusive:bool,start:u64,end:u64)->Option<(u32,bool,u64,u64)>{
 conflict(ino,owner,exclusive,start,end).map(|e|{
  let pid=match e.owner{Owner::Pid(p)=>p,Owner::Ofd(_)=>0};
  (pid,e.exclusive,e.start,e.end)
 })
}

/* Any close(2) of an inode drops the process's POSIX locks on it. */
pub fn on_close(pid:u32,ino:u64){
 if clear_range_owner(ino,Owner::Pid(pid)){wake_waiters();}
}
fn clear_range_owner(ino:u64,owner:Owner)->bool{
 let mut l=LOCKS.lock();let mut changed=false;
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let e=l[i];
  if e.used&&e.ino==ino&&e.owner==owner{l[i]=EMPTY;changed=true;}
 }
 changed
}

/* Last descriptor of an open-file description went away. */
pub fn release_ofd(id:u32){
 if clear_range_owner_any(Owner::Ofd(id)){wake_waiters();}
}
fn clear_range_owner_any(owner:Owner)->bool{
 let mut l=LOCKS.lock();let mut changed=false;
 for i in 0..MAX_LOCKS{
  let e=l[i];
  if e.used&&e.owner==owner{l[i]=EMPTY;changed=true;}
 }
 changed
}

/* Process exit: drop every lock the process owned. */
pub fn release_pid(pid:u32){
 if clear_range_owner_any(Owner::Pid(pid)){wake_waiters();}
}

pub fn resource_stats()->usize{LOCKS.lock().iter().filter(|e|e.used).count()}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: kernel/record_lock.rs

//! Robust-futex list processing at task exit.
//! The kernel walks the exiting task's robust list in user memory; every
//! futex word still owned by the task (low 30 bits equal to the exiting pid)
//! is marked FUTEX_OWNER_DIED, the waiters' bit is preserved, and one waiter
//! is woken so it can observe the death and finish its unlock protocol.
//! The walk aborts on the first unreadable word, so a corrupted list cannot
//! loop or fault in the kernel.
const FUTEX_TID_MASK:u32=0x3fff_ffff;
const FUTEX_OWNER_DIED:u32=0x4000_0000;
const FUTEX_WAITERS:u32=0x8000_0000;
const MAX_ENTRIES:usize=64;

fn canonical(addr:u64,len:u64)->bool{
 match addr.checked_add(len){Some(end)=>addr>=crate::usercopy::USER_MIN as u64&&end<=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64,None=>false}
}
fn read_u64(addr:u64)->Result<u64,i64>{
 if !canonical(addr,8){return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 let mut b=[0u8;8];
 crate::usercopy::copy_from_user(&mut b,addr as usize)?;
 Ok(u64::from_ne_bytes(b))
}
fn read_u32(addr:u64)->Result<u32,i64>{
 if !canonical(addr,4){return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 let mut b=[0u8;4];
 crate::usercopy::copy_from_user(&mut b,addr as usize)?;
 Ok(u32::from_ne_bytes(b))
}
fn write_u32(addr:u64,v:u32)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !canonical(addr,4){return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(addr as usize,&v.to_ne_bytes())
}
fn handle_futex(uaddr:u64,pid:u32){
 let cur=match read_u32(uaddr){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return};
 if cur&FUTEX_TID_MASK!=pid{return}
 let new=(cur&FUTEX_WAITERS)|FUTEX_OWNER_DIED;
 if write_u32(uaddr,new).is_err(){return}
 /* Linux only wakes when the waiter advertised itself with FUTEX_WAITERS. */
 if cur&FUTEX_WAITERS!=0{let _=crate::futex::wake(uaddr,1);}
}

/* head points at struct robust_list_head { list, futex_offset, list_op_pending }. */
pub fn process_exit(pid:u32,head:u64){
 if head==0||!canonical(head,24){return}
 let offset=match read_u64(head+8){Ok(v)=>v as i64,Err(_)=>return};
 let pending=match read_u64(head+16){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return};
 if pending!=0{handle_futex((pending as i64).wrapping_add(offset) as u64,pid)}
 let mut entry=match read_u64(head){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return};
 let mut walked=0;
 while entry!=head&&entry!=0&&walked<MAX_ENTRIES{
  walked+=1;
  handle_futex((entry as i64).wrapping_add(offset) as u64,pid);
  entry=match read_u64(entry){Ok(v)=>v,Err(_)=>return};
 }
}
