//! Linux signal ABI: kill, rt_sigaction, rt_sigprocmask and rt_sigreturn.
//!
//! Per-process pending masks, blocked masks and handler slots live in
//! Process. Signal delivery builds the published x86-64 `rt_sigframe` on the
//! user stack: the restorer address occupies the lowest slot, the
//! `ucontext_t` follows and the `siginfo_t` closes the frame. The layout
//! facts are recorded in `docs/compat-ledger.md`.
use crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::{self,UserFrame};
use crate::task;
pub const SIGHUP:i32=1;pub const SIGINT:i32=2;pub const SIGQUIT:i32=3;pub const SIGILL:i32=4;pub const SIGTRAP:i32=5;pub const SIGABRT:i32=6;pub const SIGBUS:i32=7;pub const SIGFPE:i32=8;pub const SIGKILL:i32=9;pub const SIGUSR1:i32=10;pub const SIGSEGV:i32=11;pub const SIGUSR2:i32=12;pub const SIGPIPE:i32=13;pub const SIGALRM:i32=14;pub const SIGTERM:i32=15;pub const SIGCHLD:i32=17;pub const SIGCONT:i32=18;pub const SIGSTOP:i32=19;pub const SIGTSTP:i32=20;pub const SIGTTIN:i32=21;pub const SIGTTOU:i32=22;pub const SIGURG:i32=23;pub const SIGVTALRM:i32=26;pub const SIGPROF:i32=27;pub const SIGWINCH:i32=28;
pub const SIG_DFL:u64=0;pub const SIG_IGN:u64=1;
pub const SA_NOCLDSTOP:u64=1;pub const SA_NOCLDWAIT:u64=2;pub const SA_SIGINFO:u64=4;pub const SA_ONSTACK:u64=0x08000000;pub const SA_RESTORER:u64=0x04000000;pub const SA_RESTART:u64=0x10000000;pub const SA_NODEFER:u64=0x40000000;pub const SA_RESETHAND:u64=0x80000000;
pub const BLOCK:u64=0;pub const UNBLOCK:u64=1;pub const SETMASK:u64=2;
const MAX_SIG:usize=64;
/* Linux x86-64 kernel_sigaction layout, used verbatim by rt_sigaction. */
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Debug)]
#[repr(C)]
pub struct Action{pub handler:u64,pub flags:u64,pub restorer:u64,pub mask:u64}
const DFL:Action=Action{handler:SIG_DFL,flags:0,restorer:0,mask:0};
pub fn valid(sig:i32)->bool{sig>=1&&sig<MAX_SIG as i32}
pub fn bit(sig:i32)->u64{1u64<<(sig as u64-1)}
/* Signals that cannot be blocked or ignored. */
pub fn unblockable(sig:i32)->bool{sig==SIGKILL||sig==SIGSTOP}
/* Classifies the disposition installed for a signal number. */
pub enum Disposition{Handler(u64,u64,u64),Ignore,Terminate,Stop,Continue}
fn task_index(pid:u32)->Option<usize>{let s=task::sched_pids();(0..8).find(|&i|s[i]==pid)}
/* Delivers a signal to another task by marking its pending mask. */
pub fn send(pid:u32,sig:i32)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(sig){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 if !unblockable(sig)&&task_index(pid).is_none(){return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)}
 task::signal_pid(pid,sig)
}
/* Delivers a signal to the calling task itself. */
pub fn send_self(sig:i32)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(sig){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 task::with_current_process(|p|{if unblockable(sig){p.sigmask&=!bit(sig)}p.pending|=bit(sig);Ok::<(),i64>(())}).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?;
 Ok(())
}pub fn debug_mask()->u64{task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigmask).unwrap_or(0)}pub fn action_get(sig:i32)->Result<Action,i64>{
 if !valid(sig){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 task::with_current_process(|p|{Ok(p.handlers[sig as usize])}).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?
}
pub fn action_set(sig:i32,a:Action)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(sig){return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 if sig==SIGKILL||sig==SIGSTOP{return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)}
 task::with_current_process(|p|{p.handlers[sig as usize]=a;Ok::<(),i64>(())}).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn mask_op(how:u64,set:u64)->Result<u64,i64>{
 task::with_current_process(|p|{let old=p.sigmask;match how{BLOCK=>p.sigmask|=set,UNBLOCK=>p.sigmask&=!set,SETMASK=>p.sigmask=set,_=>return Err(crate::errno::EINVAL)};Ok(old)}).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?
}
/* Next pending, unblocked signal; unblockable signals ignore the mask. */
pub fn pending()->Option<i64>{
 task::with_current_process(|p|{let blocked=p.sigmask|bit(SIGKILL)|bit(SIGSTOP);let m=p.pending&!blocked;if m==0{None}else{Some(m.trailing_zeros()as i64+1)}}).flatten()
}
/* True when a signal is queued and deliverable, so a blocking syscall can
   report EINTR instead of sleeping through the delivery. */
pub fn has_pending()->bool{pending().is_some()}
pub fn pop(sig:i64){task::with_current_process(|p|{p.pending&=!bit(sig as i32);});}
pub fn clear_all(){task::with_current_process(|p|{p.pending=0;p.sigmask=0;});}
pub fn is_blocked(sig:i64)->bool{task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigmask&bit(sig as i32)!=0).unwrap_or(false)}
pub fn set_sigframe(addr:u64){task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigframe=addr);}
pub fn get_sigframe()->u64{task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigframe).unwrap_or(0)}
pub fn unblockable_mask()->u64{bit(SIGKILL)|bit(SIGSTOP)}
pub fn handlers_dfl()->[Action;64]{[DFL;64]}

/* True when the default disposition of sig kills the process. */
pub fn default_terminates(sig:i32)->bool{
 matches!(sig,
  SIGHUP|SIGINT|SIGQUIT|SIGILL|SIGABRT|SIGBUS|SIGFPE|SIGKILL|SIGSEGV|
  SIGPIPE|SIGALRM|SIGTERM|SIGUSR1|SIGUSR2|SIGVTALRM|SIGPROF)
}

/* The published REG_* index list of the x86-64 machine context. */
pub const REG_R8:usize=0;pub const REG_R9:usize=1;pub const REG_R10:usize=2;pub const REG_R11:usize=3;
pub const REG_R12:usize=4;pub const REG_R13:usize=5;pub const REG_R14:usize=6;pub const REG_R15:usize=7;
pub const REG_RDI:usize=8;pub const REG_RSI:usize=9;pub const REG_RBP:usize=10;pub const REG_RBX:usize=11;
pub const REG_RDX:usize=12;pub const REG_RAX:usize=13;pub const REG_RCX:usize=14;pub const REG_RSP:usize=15;
pub const REG_RIP:usize=16;pub const REG_EFL:usize=17;pub const REG_CSGSFS:usize=18;pub const REG_ERR:usize=19;
pub const REG_TRAPNO:usize=20;pub const REG_OLDMASK:usize=21;pub const REG_CR2:usize=22;

/* Linux x86-64 `mcontext_t`: the register array, the floating-point pointer
   and the reserved words. The register array is the `sigcontext` view. */
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct MachineContext{pub gregs:[u64;23],pub fpregs:u64,pub reserved1:[u64;8]}

/* Linux x86-64 `stack_t`. */
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct StackArea{pub ss_sp:u64,pub ss_flags:u32,pub ss_pad:u32,pub ss_size:u64}

/* Linux x86-64 `ucontext_t`. */
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]
pub struct UContext{pub uc_flags:u64,pub uc_link:u64,pub uc_stack:StackArea,pub uc_mcontext:MachineContext,pub uc_sigmask:u64,pub fpstate:[u8;512]}
impl Default for UContext{fn default()->Self{Self{uc_flags:0,uc_link:0,uc_stack:StackArea::default(),uc_mcontext:MachineContext::default(),uc_sigmask:0,fpstate:[0;512]}}}

/* Linux `siginfo_t`: the header followed by the 128-byte union area. */
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Default)]
pub struct SigInfo{pub si_signo:i32,pub si_errno:i32,pub si_code:i32,pub si_pad:[i32;29]}

/* The sender of a queued signal was a process. */
pub const SI_USER:i32=0;

/* The user context prepared for a handler entry. */
pub struct Delivery{pub rip:u64,pub rsp:u64,pub rflags:u64,pub sig:i32,pub siginfo:u64,pub ucontext:u64}

/* The size of a complete signal frame on the user stack. */
pub fn frame_size()->usize{8+core::mem::size_of::<UContext>()+core::mem::size_of::<SigInfo>()}

/* Prepares the next deliverable signal and returns the handler entry
   context. `None` means that nothing is pending or that the default action
   consumed the signal. */
pub fn prepare_delivery(frame:*mut u64)->Option<Delivery>{
 loop{
  let sig=match pending(){Some(s)=>s,None=>return None};
  let action=match action_get(sig as i32){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>{pop(sig);continue}};
  if action.handler==SIG_IGN{pop(sig);continue}
  if action.handler==SIG_DFL{
   if default_terminates(sig as i32){crate::task::exit_signal(sig as i32);}
   pop(sig);continue;
  }
  match build_frame(sig as i32,action,frame){
   Ok(d)=>return Some(d),
   Err(_)=>{crate::task::exit_signal(SIGSEGV);return None;}
  }
 }
}

/* Builds the published rt_sigframe on the user stack: the restorer address,
   then the ucontext, then the siginfo. The handler is entered with the
   signal number, the siginfo pointer and the ucontext pointer. */
fn build_frame(sig:i32,action:Action,frame:*mut u64)->Result<Delivery,i64>{
 let snap=syscall::capture_user_frame(frame).ok_or(crate::errno::EFAULT)?;
 let size=frame_size() as u64;
 if snap.rsp<0x4000+size{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 if action.handler<0x1000||action.handler>=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 if action.restorer<0x1000||action.restorer>=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 /* The handler entry stack pointer must be 8 modulo 16 so that the handler
    frame keeps the System V alignment. */
 let x=((snap.rsp-size-8)&!15)+8;
 let saved_mask=task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigmask).unwrap_or(0);
 let mut uc=UContext::default();
 let cs=crate::arch::x86_64::descriptors::UCODE as u64;
 let ss=crate::arch::x86_64::descriptors::UDATA as u64;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R8]=snap.r8;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R9]=snap.r9;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R10]=snap.r10;
 /* The SYSCALL instruction clobbers RCX and R11, so the interrupted values
     are not available; Linux reports them as zero as well. */
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R11]=0;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R12]=snap.r12;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R13]=snap.r13;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R14]=snap.r14;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_R15]=snap.r15;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RDI]=snap.rdi;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RSI]=snap.rsi;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RBP]=snap.rbp;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RBX]=snap.rbx;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RDX]=snap.rdx;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RAX]=snap.rax;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RCX]=0;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RSP]=snap.rsp;uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RIP]=snap.rip;
 /* The machine context reports the result of the interrupted syscall, the
    way Linux fills the ucontext of a handler. */
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_RAX]=unsafe{syscall::SYSCALL_RESULT} as i64 as u64;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_EFL]=snap.rflags;
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_CSGSFS]=(cs&0xffff)|((ss&0xffff)<<16);
 uc.uc_mcontext.gregs[REG_OLDMASK]=saved_mask;
 uc.uc_stack.ss_sp=snap.rsp;
 uc.uc_sigmask=saved_mask;
 let mut info=SigInfo::default();
 info.si_signo=sig;info.si_errno=0;info.si_code=SI_USER;
 /* The sender of the signal travels in the _kill member, which starts right
    after the three header words. */
 let sender=task::with_current_process(|p|p.pending_from[sig as usize]).unwrap_or(0);
 info.si_pad[0]=sender as i32;
 info.si_pad[1]=0;
 let uc_addr=x+8;
 let info_addr=uc_addr+core::mem::size_of::<UContext>() as u64;
 crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(x as usize,&action.restorer.to_ne_bytes())?;
 crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(uc_addr as usize,unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts((&uc as*const UContext).cast::<u8>(),core::mem::size_of::<UContext>())})?;
 crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(info_addr as usize,unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts((&info as*const SigInfo).cast::<u8>(),core::mem::size_of::<SigInfo>())})?;
 let mut mask=saved_mask|action.mask;
 if action.flags&SA_NODEFER==0{mask&=!bit(sig)}
 task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigmask=mask);
 /* A handler installed with SA_RESTART restarts the interrupted syscall;
    every other handler leaves the syscall with EINTR. */
 task::with_current_process(|p|{p.sig_restart=action.flags&SA_RESTART!=0;p.sig_syscall=snap.rax;p.sig_result=unsafe{syscall::SYSCALL_RESULT} as i64;});
 /* The signal leaves the pending set when it is delivered: Linux removes it
    before the handler runs, so the handler does not see it again. */
 pop(sig as i64);
 if action.flags&SA_RESETHAND!=0{
  let reset=Action{handler:SIG_DFL,flags:0,restorer:0,mask:0};
  let _=action_set(sig,reset);
 }
 /* A handler without SA_SIGINFO receives no siginfo pointer, the same way a
    Linux handler is called with a single argument. */
 let siginfo=if action.flags&SA_SIGINFO!=0{info_addr}else{0};
 let flags=(snap.rflags&!(1u64<<8))|(1u64<<9);
 Ok(Delivery{rip:action.handler,rsp:x,rflags:flags,sig:sig,siginfo:siginfo,ucontext:uc_addr})
}

/* Restores the interrupted context from the handler frame. The restorer
   enters the kernel with the stack pointer pointing just above the frame, so
   the frame starts one word below. */
pub fn restore_frame()->Result<(),i64>{
 let sp=unsafe{syscall::SYSCALL_USER_RSP};
 if sp<0x2000{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 let base=sp-8;
 let uc_addr=base+8;
 let mut uc=UContext::default();
 crate::usercopy::copy_from_user(unsafe{core::slice::from_raw_parts_mut((&mut uc as*mut UContext).cast::<u8>(),core::mem::size_of::<UContext>())},uc_addr as usize)?;
 let g=uc.uc_mcontext.gregs;
 let rip=g[REG_RIP];let rsp=g[REG_RSP];let rflags=g[REG_EFL];
 if rip<0x1000||rip>=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 if rsp<0x1000||rsp>=crate::usercopy::USER_MAX as u64{return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 /* Without SA_RESTART the interrupted syscall ends with EINTR: the user
    sees the error instead of the syscall running again. With SA_RESTART the
    syscall number stays in RAX and the user repeats the call. */
 let (restart,syscall_no,result)=task::with_current_process(|p|(p.sig_restart,p.sig_syscall,p.sig_result)).unwrap_or((false,0,0));
 /* A handler with SA_RESTART repeats the interrupted syscall: the syscall
    number stays in RAX so the user repeats the call. Every other handler
    leaves the syscall result in RAX, which is EINTR when the syscall was
    interrupted. */
 let rax=if restart{syscall_no}else{result as u64};
 let frame=UserFrame{
  r15:g[REG_R15],r14:g[REG_R14],r13:g[REG_R13],r12:g[REG_R12],rbp:g[REG_RBP],rbx:g[REG_RBX],
  r10:g[REG_R10],r9:g[REG_R9],r8:g[REG_R8],rdx:g[REG_RDX],rsi:g[REG_RSI],rdi:g[REG_RDI],
  rax:rax,rip:rip,rflags:rflags,rsp:rsp};
 task::with_current_process(|p|p.sigmask=uc.uc_sigmask);
 if !syscall::set_return_context(unsafe{syscall::SYSCALL_FRAME as *mut u64},&frame){return Err(crate::errno::EFAULT)}
 /* The return path takes the user-mode result from this cell, so the
    restored value has to be published here. */
 unsafe{syscall::SIGRET_VALUE=rax;syscall::SIGRET_VALID=1;}
 Ok(())
}

//! AF_UNIX stream sockets: bind/listen/connect/accept over a fixed table.
//! Each endpoint owns a ring buffer its peer writes into; read and write
//! block on wait queues exactly like pipes, with EOF when the peer closes.
use crate::{errno,sync::SpinLock,wait_queue::WaitQueue};
pub const MAX_SOCKETS:usize=16;
/* AF_INET endpoints are dispatched by id range: 16..48 hand off to net.rs. */
pub const INET_UDP_BASE:u8=16;pub const INET_TCP_BASE:u8=32;
pub fn inet_kind(id:u8)->Option<bool>{
 if id>=INET_TCP_BASE{Some(true)}else if id>=INET_UDP_BASE{Some(false)}else{None}
}pub const CAPACITY:usize=4096;pub const PATH_MAX:usize=108;
pub const SOCK_STREAM:u32=1;pub const SOCK_DGRAM:u32=2;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq)]pub enum SockState{Unbound,Listening,Connected}
#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq)]pub enum SockKind{Stream,Dgram}
struct Socket{
 used:bool,kind:SockKind,state:SockState,
 path:[u8;PATH_MAX],path_len:usize,
 peer:Option<u8>,
 buf:[u8;CAPACITY],head:usize,len:usize,
 peer_closed:bool,
 readq:WaitQueue,writeq:WaitQueue,
 acceptq:WaitQueue,
}
impl Socket{
 const fn new()->Self{Self{used:false,kind:SockKind::Stream,state:SockState::Unbound,path:[0;PATH_MAX],path_len:0,peer:None,buf:[0;CAPACITY],head:0,len:0,peer_closed:false,readq:WaitQueue::new(),writeq:WaitQueue::new(),acceptq:WaitQueue::new()}}
 fn path_str(&self)->&str{core::str::from_utf8(&self.path[..self.path_len]).unwrap_or("")}
 fn free(&mut self){*self=Socket::new();}
}
static SOCKS:[SpinLock<Socket>;MAX_SOCKETS]=[const{SpinLock::new(Socket::new())};MAX_SOCKETS];
/* Server-side pending-connection requests: (requester socket, requester pid). */
static PENDING:[SpinLock<[(Option<u8>,Option<u8>);16]>;MAX_SOCKETS]=[const{SpinLock::new([(None,None);16])};MAX_SOCKETS];
fn valid(id:u8)->bool{(id as usize)<MAX_SOCKETS}
pub fn socket(kind:u32)->Result<u8,i64>{
 let sk=match kind{SOCK_STREAM=>SockKind::Stream,SOCK_DGRAM=>SockKind::Dgram,_=>return Err(errno::EINVAL)};
 for i in 0..MAX_SOCKETS{
  let mut s=SOCKS[i].lock();
  if !s.used{s.used=true;s.kind=sk;s.state=SockState::Unbound;return Ok(i as u8)}
 }
 Err(errno::EMFILE)
}
pub fn kind_of(id:u8)->Option<SockKind>{if !valid(id){return None}Some(SOCKS[id as usize].lock().kind)}
pub fn bind(id:u8,path:&str)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 if path.is_empty()||path.len()>=PATH_MAX{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 for i in 0..MAX_SOCKETS{
  let s=SOCKS[i].lock();
  if s.used&&s.path_len==path.len()&&s.path_str()==path{return Err(errno::EADDRINUSE)}
 }
 let mut s=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
 let b=path.as_bytes();s.path[..b.len()].copy_from_slice(b);s.path_len=b.len();
 s.state=SockState::Unbound;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn listen(id:u8)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let mut s=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
 if s.path_len==0{return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 s.state=SockState::Listening;
 Ok(())
}
/* Finds the listening socket bound to path. */
fn find_listener(path:&str)->Option<u8>{
 for i in 0..MAX_SOCKETS{
  let s=SOCKS[i].lock();
  if s.used&&s.state==SockState::Listening&&s.path_len==path.len()&&s.path_str()==path{return Some(i as u8)}
 }
 None
}
pub fn connect(id:u8,path:&str)->Result<(),i64>{
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let listener=match find_listener(path){Some(l)=>l,None=>return Err(errno::ECONNREFUSED)};
 /* Establish the pair immediately so the client can send before the server
    accepts; accept hands the already-connected endpoint over. */
 let server=match socket(SOCK_STREAM){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>return Err(e)};
 {
  let mut c=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
  let mut sv=SOCKS[server as usize].lock();
  c.peer=Some(server);sv.peer=Some(id);
  c.state=SockState::Connected;sv.state=SockState::Connected;
 }
 let mut q=PENDING[listener as usize].lock();
 for slot in q.iter_mut(){
  if slot.0.is_none(){*slot=(Some(id),Some(server));drop(q);SOCKS[listener as usize].lock().acceptq.wake_all();return Ok(())}
 }
 drop(q);
 let _=server;
 Err(errno::ECONNREFUSED)
}
/* Takes the first pending request, creating a connected pair for it.
   Blocks while the queue is empty, mirroring a listening socket with no
   connection yet; the caller passes false for non-blocking sockets. */
pub const SOCK_NONBLOCK:u32=0o4000;
pub const SOCK_CLOEXEC:u32=0o2000000;
pub fn accept(listener:u8,nonblocking:bool)->Result<u8,i64>{
 if !valid(listener){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 loop{
  let requester={
   let mut q=PENDING[listener as usize].lock();
   match q.iter_mut().find(|s|s.0.is_some()){
    Some(slot)=>{let c=slot.0.take();let sv=slot.1.take();*slot=(None,None);(c,sv)},
    None=>(None,None),
   }
  };
  if let (Some(client),Some(server))=requester{
   {
    let mut sv=SOCKS[server as usize].lock();
    let c=SOCKS[client as usize].lock();
    sv.path[..c.path_len.min(PATH_MAX)].copy_from_slice(&c.path[..c.path_len.min(PATH_MAX)]);
   }
   return Ok(server);
  }
    if nonblocking{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
  /* Register before re-checking so a connect that lands between the check
     and the sleep cannot be lost. */
  let registered={let s=SOCKS[listener as usize].lock();s.acceptq.register().is_ok()};
  let pending=PENDING[listener as usize].lock().iter().any(|s|s.0.is_some());
  if pending{continue}
  if !registered{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
  crate::task::block_current();
 }
}
/* True when a pending connection request is waiting. */
pub fn has_pending(listener:u8)->bool{
 if !valid(listener){return false}
 PENDING[listener as usize].lock().iter().any(|s|s.0.is_some())
}
/* Creates a pre-connected pair for socketpair. */
pub fn socketpair()->Result<(u8,u8),i64>{
 let a=socket(SOCK_STREAM)?;let b=socket(SOCK_STREAM)?;
 {
  let mut sa=SOCKS[a as usize].lock();let mut sb=SOCKS[b as usize].lock();
  sa.peer=Some(b);sb.peer=Some(a);
  sa.state=SockState::Connected;sb.state=SockState::Connected;
 }
 Ok((a,b))
}
pub fn send(id:u8,data:&[u8],nonblock:bool)->Result<usize,i64>{
 if let Some(tcp)=inet_kind(id){
  return if tcp{crate::net::tcp_send(id-INET_TCP_BASE,data)}else{crate::net::udp_send(id-INET_UDP_BASE,None,data)};
 }
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let peer=match SOCKS[id as usize].lock().peer{Some(p)=>p,None=>return Err(errno::ENOTCONN)};
  let mut done=0usize;
 while done<data.len(){
  let registered={
   let mut p=SOCKS[peer as usize].lock();
   if p.peer_closed{return if done>0{Ok(done)}else{Err(errno::EPIPE)}}
   if p.len<CAPACITY{
    let n=core::cmp::min(CAPACITY-p.len,data.len()-done);
    let base=p.head+p.len;
    for i in 0..n{p.buf[(base+i)%CAPACITY]=data[done+i];}
    p.len+=n;done+=n;p.readq.wake_all();
    false
   }else if nonblock{return if done>0{Ok(done)}else{Err(errno::EAGAIN)}}else{p.writeq.register().is_ok()}
  };
  if done==data.len(){break}
  if !registered{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
  crate::task::block_current();
 }
 Ok(done)
}
pub fn recv(id:u8,dst:&mut[u8],nonblock:bool)->Result<usize,i64>{
 if let Some(tcp)=inet_kind(id){
  return if tcp{crate::net::tcp_recv(id-INET_TCP_BASE,dst)}else{crate::net::udp_recv(id-INET_UDP_BASE,dst).map(|(n,_,_)|n)};
 }
 if dst.is_empty(){return Ok(0)}
 loop{
  let registered={
   let mut s=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
      if s.len>0{
    let n=core::cmp::min(s.len,dst.len());
    for i in 0..n{dst[i]=s.buf[s.head];s.head=(s.head+1)%CAPACITY;}
    s.len-=n;s.writeq.wake_all();
    return Ok(n);
   }
   if s.peer_closed{return Ok(0)}
   if nonblock{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
   s.readq.register().is_ok()
  };
  if !registered{return Err(errno::EAGAIN)}
  crate::task::block_current();
 }
}
/* Half-closes: the peer observes EOF while this end may still read. */
pub fn shutdown(id:u8)->Result<(),i64>{
 if let Some(tcp)=inet_kind(id){
  return if tcp{crate::net::tcp_shutdown(id-INET_TCP_BASE,1)}else{Ok(())};
 }
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let peer=SOCKS[id as usize].lock().peer;
 SOCKS[id as usize].lock().peer_closed=true;
 if let Some(p)=peer{
  let mut ps=SOCKS[p as usize].lock();
  ps.peer_closed=true;
  ps.readq.wake_all();
 }
 Ok(())
}
pub fn close(id:u8){
 if let Some(tcp)=inet_kind(id){
  if tcp{crate::net::tcp_close(id-INET_TCP_BASE)}else{crate::net::udp_close(id-INET_UDP_BASE)};
  return;
 }
 if !valid(id){return}
 let mut s=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
 if !s.used{return}
 let peer=s.peer;
 s.free();
 drop(s);
 if let Some(p)=peer{
  let mut ps=SOCKS[p as usize].lock();
  ps.peer_closed=true;
  ps.readq.wake_all();
 }
}
pub fn path_of(id:u8)->Result<[u8;PATH_MAX],i64>{
 if !valid(id){return Err(errno::EBADF)}
 let s=SOCKS[id as usize].lock();
 Ok(s.path)
}
pub fn path_len_of(id:u8)->usize{SOCKS[id as usize].lock().path_len}
pub fn is_sockaddr_un(family:u16)->bool{family==1}

use core::{cell::UnsafeCell, hint::spin_loop, ops::{Deref, DerefMut}, sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering}};

pub struct SpinLock<T> { held: AtomicBool, value: UnsafeCell<T> }
unsafe impl<T: Send> Sync for SpinLock<T> {}
unsafe impl<T: Send> Send for SpinLock<T> {}
impl<T> SpinLock<T> {
    pub const fn new(value: T) -> Self { Self { held: AtomicBool::new(false), value: UnsafeCell::new(value) } }
    pub fn lock(&self) -> Guard<'_, T> {
        while self.held.compare_exchange_weak(false, true, Ordering::Acquire, Ordering::Relaxed).is_err() {
            while self.held.load(Ordering::Relaxed) { spin_loop(); }
        }
        Guard { lock: self }
    }
}
pub struct Guard<'a, T> { lock: &'a SpinLock<T> }
impl<T> Deref for Guard<'_, T> { type Target=T; fn deref(&self)->&T { unsafe { &*self.lock.value.get() } } }
impl<T> DerefMut for Guard<'_, T> { fn deref_mut(&mut self)->&mut T { unsafe { &mut *self.lock.value.get() } } }
impl<T> Drop for Guard<'_, T> { fn drop(&mut self) { self.lock.held.store(false, Ordering::Release); } }

/// Spin lock that cannot be interrupted on the local CPU while held.
pub struct IrqSpinLock<T>{inner:SpinLock<T>}
impl<T> IrqSpinLock<T>{pub const fn new(value:T)->Self{Self{inner:SpinLock::new(value)}}pub fn lock(&self)->IrqGuard<'_,T>{let flags=irq_save();IrqGuard{guard:core::mem::ManuallyDrop::new(self.inner.lock()),flags}}}
pub struct IrqGuard<'a,T>{guard:core::mem::ManuallyDrop<Guard<'a,T>>,flags:u64}
impl<T>Deref for IrqGuard<'_,T>{type Target=T;fn deref(&self)->&T{&self.guard}}
impl<T>DerefMut for IrqGuard<'_,T>{fn deref_mut(&mut self)->&mut T{&mut self.guard}}
impl<T>Drop for IrqGuard<'_,T>{fn drop(&mut self){unsafe{core::mem::ManuallyDrop::drop(&mut self.guard)}irq_restore(self.flags)}}
#[inline]fn irq_save()->u64{let flags:u64;unsafe{core::arch::asm!("pushfq","pop {}","cli",out(reg)flags,options(nomem));}flags}
#[inline]fn irq_restore(flags:u64){if flags&(1<<9)!=0{unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti",options(nomem,nostack,preserves_flags));}}}

//! Single-CPU round-robin scheduler, task lifecycle and process ownership.
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool,AtomicU32,AtomicU64,Ordering};
use crate::process::Process;
use crate::sync::IrqSpinLock;
const MAX_TASKS:usize=8;const STACK_SIZE:usize=65536;
#[derive(Clone,Copy,Debug,Default,PartialEq,Eq)]
pub struct ResourceStats{pub active:usize,pub zombies:usize,pub runnable:usize,pub sleeping:usize,pub unused:usize}
#[repr(align(16))]struct Stack([u8;STACK_SIZE]);
#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq)]pub enum State{Unused,Running,Runnable,Sleeping,Zombie}
#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub struct Task{pub fpu:u64,pub pid:u32,pub tgid:u32,pub ppid:u32,pub pgid:u32,pub sid:u32,pub state:State,pub rsp:u64,pub kernel_stack_top:u64,pub cr3:u64,pub fs_base:u64,pub wake_tick:u64,pub wake_pending:bool,pub exit_code:i32,pub term_signal:u8,pub is_thread:bool,pub user_rsp:u64}
const EMPTY:Task=Task{fpu:0,pid:0,tgid:0,ppid:0,pgid:0,sid:0,state:State::Unused,rsp:0,kernel_stack_top:0,cr3:0,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:false,user_rsp:0};
struct Scheduler{tasks:[Task;MAX_TASKS],current:usize,next_pid:u32}
static SCHED:IrqSpinLock<Scheduler>=IrqSpinLock::new(Scheduler{tasks:[EMPTY;MAX_TASKS],current:0,next_pid:2});
static NEXT_NS:AtomicU64=AtomicU64::new(2);
static mut UTS_NAMESPACES:[[u8;65];16]=[[0;65];16];
fn uts_init(){unsafe{UTS_NAMESPACES[1][..4].copy_from_slice(b"slop");}}
/* One Process per user task slot; kernel-only tasks leave their entry empty. */
static PROCS:IrqSpinLock<[Option<Process>;MAX_TASKS]>=IrqSpinLock::new([const{None};MAX_TASKS]);
static mut STACKS:[Stack;MAX_TASKS]=[const{Stack([0;STACK_SIZE])};MAX_TASKS];
static NEED_RESCHED:AtomicBool=AtomicBool::new(false);static PID:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(1);
unsafe extern "sysv64"{fn switch_context(old:*mut u64,new:u64,old_fpu:u64,new_fpu:u64);}
/* FXSAVE/FXRSTOR helpers: the FPU context lives in a 512 byte area per task
   so floating point and SSE registers survive a context switch. */
const FPU_AREA:usize=256;
/// Per-slot SSE register file, kept out of the task stacks so saving the
/// registers can never overwrite a live stack.
#[repr(align(16))]
struct FpuArea([u8;FPU_AREA]);
static mut FPU_AREAS:[FpuArea;MAX_TASKS]=[const{FpuArea([0;FPU_AREA])};MAX_TASKS];
/* The FXSAVE area must be 16-byte aligned and must not straddle a page,
   so it sits at the page-aligned bottom of the task stack, which the growing
   stack reaches last. */
fn fpu_area(slot:usize)->u64{unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(FPU_AREAS[slot])as u64}}
unsafe extern "sysv64"{fn fpu_save_regs(area:*mut u8);}
fn fpu_save(addr:u64){let _=addr;}
pub fn init(bootstrap_stack_top:u64){uts_init();fpu_save(fpu_area(0));let mut s=SCHED.lock();let cr3=crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PageTableRoot::current().pml4;s.tasks[0]=Task{fpu:fpu_area(0),pid:1,tgid:1,ppid:0,pgid:1,sid:1,state:State::Running,rsp:0,kernel_stack_top:bootstrap_stack_top,cr3,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:false,user_rsp:0};crate::println!("task: scheduler bootstrap pid=1 capacity={}",MAX_TASKS);}
/* The identifier of the running task. The scheduler is the single source of
   truth: a cached copy could name a task that ran in between, and a signal
   aimed at the wrong thread would be lost. */
pub fn current_pid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].pid}
pub fn current_ppid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].ppid}
pub fn current_pgid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].pgid}
pub fn current_sid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].sid}
pub fn alloc_ns()->u64{NEXT_NS.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed)}
pub fn uts_hostname(ns:u64)->[u8;65]{let idx=(ns as usize)%16;unsafe{if UTS_NAMESPACES[idx][0]==0{let mut d=[0u8;65];d[..4].copy_from_slice(b"slop");d}else{UTS_NAMESPACES[idx]}}}
pub fn set_uts_hostname(ns:u64,name:&[u8]){let idx=(ns as usize)%16;unsafe{UTS_NAMESPACES[idx]=[0;65];let n=name.len().min(64);UTS_NAMESPACES[idx][..n].copy_from_slice(&name[..n]);}}
pub fn alloc_uts_ns(src:u64)->u64{let id=alloc_ns();let idx=(id as usize)%16;let srcidx=(src as usize)%16;unsafe{UTS_NAMESPACES[idx]=UTS_NAMESPACES[srcidx];}id}
pub fn sched_pids()->[u32;8]{let s=SCHED.lock();let mut p=[0;8];for i in 0..8{p[i]=s.tasks[i].pid};p}
pub fn current_kernel_stack_top()->u64{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].kernel_stack_top}
pub fn resource_stats()->ResourceStats{
 let s=SCHED.lock();let mut stats=ResourceStats::default();
 for task in &s.tasks{match task.state{State::Unused=>stats.unused+=1,State::Zombie=>stats.zombies+=1,State::Runnable=>{stats.active+=1;stats.runnable+=1},State::Sleeping=>{stats.active+=1;stats.sleeping+=1},State::Running=>stats.active+=1}}
 stats
}
/* Locks are never nested the other way around: the scheduler guard is released
   before PROCS is taken, so closure bodies may lock anything but SCHED. */
pub fn with_current_process<R>(f:impl FnOnce(&mut Process)->R)->Option<R>{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let mut procs=PROCS.lock();procs[slot].as_mut().map(f)}
pub fn has_current_process()->bool{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;PROCS.lock()[slot].is_some()}
/* Immutable view of another live process, for /proc/<pid> introspection. */
pub fn with_process_pid<R>(pid:u32,f:impl FnOnce(&Process)->R)->Option<R>{let slot={let s=SCHED.lock();(0..MAX_TASKS).find(|&i|s.tasks[i].pid==pid&&s.tasks[i].state!=State::Unused&&!s.tasks[i].is_thread)?};let procs=PROCS.lock();procs[slot].as_ref().map(f)}
pub fn live_pids(mut cb:impl FnMut(u32)){let s=SCHED.lock();let mut seen=[0u32;MAX_TASKS];let mut n=0usize;for t in &s.tasks{if t.state==State::Unused||t.is_thread||seen[..n].contains(&t.pid){continue}seen[n]=t.pid;n+=1;cb(t.pid);}}
pub fn thread_count(pid:u32)->usize{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().filter(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused).count().max(1)}
pub fn ppid_of(pid:u32)->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().find(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused).map(|t|t.ppid).unwrap_or(0)}
pub fn last_pid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.next_pid.saturating_sub(1).max(1)}
fn take_slot(s:&mut Scheduler)->Result<usize,i64>{
 if let Some(i)=(1..MAX_TASKS).find(|&i|s.tasks[i].state==State::Unused){return Ok(i)}
 /* Threads are joined in userspace; the kernel never waits for them, so a
    dead thread's slot is reclaimed here. Its CPU time folds into its
    thread-group leader, matching Linux's per-process accounting. */
 if let Some(i)=(1..MAX_TASKS).find(|&i|s.tasks[i].state==State::Zombie&&s.tasks[i].is_thread){
  let t=s.tasks[i];s.tasks[i]=EMPTY;
  KERNEL_ENTRIES.lock()[i]=None;USER_STARTS.lock()[i]=None;FORK_RESUMES.lock()[i]=None;
  let (ut,st,ct,cst)={let mut procs=PROCS.lock();match procs[i].take(){Some(p)=>(p.utime_ticks,p.stime_ticks,p.cutime_ticks,p.cstime_ticks),_=>(0,0,0,0)}};
  let lead=(1..MAX_TASKS).find(|&j|s.tasks[j].pid==t.tgid&&s.tasks[j].state!=State::Unused&&!s.tasks[j].is_thread);
  if let Some(j)=lead{let mut procs=PROCS.lock();if let Some(p)=procs[j].as_mut(){
   p.utime_ticks=p.utime_ticks.saturating_add(ut);p.stime_ticks=p.stime_ticks.saturating_add(st);
   p.cutime_ticks=p.cutime_ticks.saturating_add(ct);p.cstime_ticks=p.cstime_ticks.saturating_add(cst);}}
  return Ok(i)
 }
 Err(crate::errno::EAGAIN)
}
fn alloc_pid(s:&mut Scheduler)->u32{let pid=s.next_pid;s.next_pid=s.next_pid.wrapping_add(1).max(2);pid}
fn build_kernel_stack(slot:usize,entry:u64)->u64{let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let mut sp=(top&!15)-8;unsafe{sp-=8;*(sp as*mut u64)=entry;for _ in 0..6{sp-=8;*(sp as*mut u64)=0;}}sp}
pub fn spawn_kernel(entry:fn())->Result<u32,i64>{let mut s=SCHED.lock();let slot=take_slot(&mut s)?;let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,kernel_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);fpu_save(fpu_area(slot));KERNEL_ENTRIES.lock()[slot]=Some(entry);let cr3=crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PageTableRoot::current().pml4;let ppid=s.tasks[s.current].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[s.current].pgid;let sid=s.tasks[s.current].sid;s.tasks[slot]=Task{fpu:fpu_area(slot),pid,tgid:pid,ppid,pgid,sid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:false,user_rsp:0};Ok(pid)}
static KERNEL_ENTRIES:IrqSpinLock<[Option<fn()>;MAX_TASKS]>=IrqSpinLock::new([None;MAX_TASKS]);
static USER_STARTS:IrqSpinLock<[Option<crate::exec_runtime::Loaded>;MAX_TASKS]>=IrqSpinLock::new([None;MAX_TASKS]);
static FORK_RESUMES:IrqSpinLock<[Option<crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame>;MAX_TASKS]>=IrqSpinLock::new([const{None};MAX_TASKS]);
pub fn spawn_user(loaded:crate::exec_runtime::Loaded)->Result<u32,i64>{let mut proc=Process::new(loaded.cr3,loaded.bootstrap_cr3,loaded.image_slot,loaded.stack_slot,loaded.interp_slot)?;proc.set_exec_meta(&loaded);let mut s=SCHED.lock();let slot=take_slot(&mut s)?;let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let ppid=s.tasks[s.current].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[s.current].pgid;let sid=s.tasks[s.current].sid;let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,user_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);fpu_save(fpu_area(slot));PROCS.lock()[slot]=Some(proc);USER_STARTS.lock()[slot]=Some(loaded);s.tasks[slot]=Task{fpu:fpu_area(slot),pid,tgid:pid,ppid,pgid,sid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3:loaded.cr3,fs_base:0,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:false,user_rsp:0};Ok(pid)}
pub fn spawn_fork(mut child:Process,frame:crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame)->Result<u32,i64>{let child_cr3=child.cr3;let mut s=SCHED.lock();let slot=match take_slot(&mut s){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>{drop(s);child.abandon();return Err(e)}};let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let parent=s.current;let ppid=s.tasks[parent].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[parent].pgid;let sid=s.tasks[parent].sid;let fs_base=s.tasks[parent].fs_base;let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,fork_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);fpu_save(fpu_area(slot));FORK_RESUMES.lock()[slot]=Some(frame);PROCS.lock()[slot]=Some(child);s.tasks[slot]=Task{fpu:fpu_area(slot),pid,tgid:pid,ppid,pgid,sid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3:child_cr3,fs_base,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:false,user_rsp:0};Ok(pid)}
pub fn fork_process(frame:crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame,flags:u64)->Result<u32,i64>{let child_cr3=match with_current_process(|p|p.clone_address_space()){Some(Ok(v))=>v,Some(Err(e))=>return Err(e),None=>return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)};let mut child=match with_current_process(|p|p.fork_child(child_cr3)){Some(Ok(v))=>v,Some(Err(e))=>return Err(e),None=>return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)};if let Err(e)=apply_ns_flags(&mut child,flags){child.abandon();return Err(e)}spawn_fork(child,frame)}
extern "sysv64" fn user_thread_trampoline()->!{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let loaded=USER_STARTS.lock()[slot].take().unwrap();crate::println!("task: entering user pid={} rip={:#x} rsp={:#x}",current_pid(),loaded.entry,loaded.rsp);unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::user::enter(loaded.entry,loaded.rsp)}}
/* Spawns a CLONE_VM thread: shares the caller's address space and descriptor
   table, runs on the caller-provided stack with the given TLS base. */
pub fn spawn_thread(frame:crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::UserFrame,child_stack:u64,tls:u64,ctid:u64,ptid:u64,ns_flags:u64)->Result<u32,i64>{
 const CLONE_PARENT_SETTID:u64=0x100000;const CLONE_CHILD_SETTID:u64=0x1000000;
  let mut child=match with_current_process(|p|p.fork_child(p.cr3)){
   Some(Ok(child))=>child,Some(Err(e))=>return Err(e),None=>return Err(crate::errno::ESRCH)};
  child.pending=0;child.sigframe=0;child.clear_child_tid=ctid;
  let cr3=child.cr3;
  if let Err(e)=apply_ns_flags(&mut child,ns_flags){return Err(e)}
 let mut frame=frame;frame.rsp=child_stack;
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();
 let slot=match take_slot(&mut s){Ok(v)=>v,Err(e)=>return Err(e)};
 let pid=alloc_pid(&mut s);let parent=s.current;let tgid=s.tasks[parent].tgid;let ppid=s.tasks[parent].pid;let pgid=s.tasks[parent].pgid;let sid=s.tasks[parent].sid;
 let top=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS[slot].0)as u64+STACK_SIZE as u64};let sp=build_kernel_stack(slot,fork_thread_trampoline as *const () as u64);fpu_save(fpu_area(slot));
 FORK_RESUMES.lock()[slot]=Some(frame);PROCS.lock()[slot]=Some(child);
 s.tasks[slot]=Task{fpu:fpu_area(slot),pid,tgid,ppid,pgid,sid,state:State::Runnable,rsp:sp,kernel_stack_top:top,cr3,fs_base:tls,wake_tick:0,wake_pending:false,exit_code:0,term_signal:0,is_thread:true,user_rsp:child_stack};
 drop(s);
 /* CLONE_PARENT_SETTID: store the child TID in the parent's memory (same
    address space here); CLONE_CHILD_SETTID: store it in the child's copy. */
 let tidb=pid.to_ne_bytes();
 if ns_flags&CLONE_PARENT_SETTID!=0&&ptid!=0{let _=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(ptid as usize,&tidb);}
 if ns_flags&CLONE_CHILD_SETTID!=0&&ctid!=0{let _=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(ctid as usize,&tidb);}
 Ok(pid)
}
extern "sysv64" fn fork_thread_trampoline()->!{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let frame=FORK_RESUMES.lock()[slot].take().unwrap();crate::println!("task: fork child resumed pid={}",current_pid());unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::user::resume(&frame)}}
extern "sysv64" fn kernel_thread_trampoline()->!{let slot=SCHED.lock().current;let entry=KERNEL_ENTRIES.lock()[slot].take().unwrap();unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti",options(nomem,nostack,preserves_flags));}entry();finish_current(0)}
pub fn yield_now(){schedule(false)}
pub fn on_timer_tick(user:bool){NEED_RESCHED.store(true,Ordering::Release);let now=crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks();account_tick(user);wake_sleepers(now);crate::posix_timer::tick(now);}
/* Attributes one 1ms tick to the running process: CS&3 told us whether the
   interrupted context was user or kernel code. */
fn account_tick(user:bool){
 let(slot,pid)={let s=SCHED.lock();(s.current,s.tasks[s.current].pid)};{let mut procs=PROCS.lock();if let Some(p)=procs[slot].as_mut(){if user{if p.utime_ticks<u64::MAX{p.utime_ticks+=1}}else if p.stime_ticks<u64::MAX{p.stime_ticks+=1}}}crate::posix_timer::cpu_tick(pid,user);}
pub fn take_need_resched()->bool{NEED_RESCHED.swap(false,Ordering::AcqRel)}
/* A wake that lands before the target blocks is latched in wake_pending, so a
   register-then-block waiter can never lose it. */
pub fn sleep_until(tick:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;if s.tasks[current].wake_pending{s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;return}s.tasks[current].wake_tick=tick;s.tasks[current].state=State::Sleeping;drop(s);schedule(true)}
fn wake_sleepers(now:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();for t in &mut s.tasks{if t.state==State::Sleeping&&t.wake_pending{t.wake_pending=false;t.state=State::Runnable}else if t.state==State::Sleeping&&t.wake_tick<=now{t.state=State::Runnable;}}}
pub fn block_current(){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;if s.tasks[current].wake_pending{s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;return}s.tasks[current].state=State::Sleeping;s.tasks[current].wake_tick=u64::MAX;drop(s);schedule(true)}
/* Sleep until a signal is deliverable. Returns true when a signal is
   pending after the sleep, false when the task woke for another reason. */
pub fn block_current_until_signal()->bool{
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;
 if s.tasks[current].wake_pending{s.tasks[current].wake_pending=false;return crate::signal::pending().is_some()}
 s.tasks[current].state=State::Sleeping;s.tasks[current].wake_tick=u64::MAX;drop(s);schedule(true);
 crate::signal::pending().is_some()
}
pub fn wake_pid(pid:u32)->bool{let mut s=SCHED.lock();if let Some(t)=s.tasks.iter_mut().find(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused&&t.state!=State::Zombie){if t.state==State::Sleeping{t.state=State::Runnable}else{t.wake_pending=true}true}else{false}}
pub fn current_tgid()->u32{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].tgid}
/* Marks sig pending on the thread-group member (tgid,pid) and wakes it. */
pub fn signal_task(tgid:u32,pid:u32,sig:i32)->Result<(),i64>{

 let slot={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().position(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.tgid==tgid&&t.state!=State::Unused).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?};
 {let mut procs=PROCS.lock();let p=procs[slot].as_mut().ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?;
 p.pending|=crate::signal::bit(sig);
 p.si_code[sig as usize]=0;p.si_value[sig as usize]=0;p.si_overrun[sig as usize]=0;}
 wake_pid(pid);
 Ok(())
}
/* Marks sig pending on the task owning pid and wakes it if blocked. */
pub fn signal_pid(pid:u32,sig:i32)->Result<(),i64>{
 let slot={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().position(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused).ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?};
 {let mut procs=PROCS.lock();let p=procs[slot].as_mut().ok_or(crate::errno::ESRCH)?;
 p.pending|=crate::signal::bit(sig);
 /* Remember the sender so the delivered siginfo can report it. */
 p.pending_from[sig as usize]=current_pid();
 p.si_code[sig as usize]=0;p.si_value[sig as usize]=0;p.si_overrun[sig as usize]=0;}
 wake_pid(pid);
 Ok(())
}
/* Queues a timer-originated signal; returns false when it coalesced with an
   already-pending signal (the caller then accounts the overrun). */
pub fn timer_signal(pid:u32,sig:i32,value:u64,overrun:u32)->bool{
 let slot=match {let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks.iter().position(|t|t.pid==pid&&t.state!=State::Unused&&t.state!=State::Zombie)}{Some(v)=>v,None=>return false};
 let queued={let mut procs=PROCS.lock();match procs[slot].as_mut(){
  Some(p)=>{let b=crate::signal::bit(sig);if p.pending&b!=0{false}else{p.pending|=b;p.si_code[sig as usize]=-2;p.si_value[sig as usize]=value;p.si_overrun[sig as usize]=overrun;true}}
  None=>return false}};
 if queued{wake_pid(pid);}
 queued
}
fn finish(code:i32,signal:u8)->!{{let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;s.tasks[current].state=State::Zombie;s.tasks[current].exit_code=code;s.tasks[current].term_signal=signal;let parent=s.tasks[current].ppid;let is_thread=s.tasks[current].is_thread;drop(s);if parent!=0&&!is_thread{let _=signal_pid(parent,crate::signal::SIGCHLD);}}loop{schedule(true);unsafe{core::arch::asm!("hlt",options(nomem,nostack));}}}
pub fn setpgid(pid:u32,pgid:u32)->Result<(),i64>{
 let me=current_pid();
 let target=if pid==0{me}else{pid};
 if target!=me{return Err(crate::errno::EPERM)}
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();
 let idx=s.current;
 let newpg=if pgid==0{me}else{pgid};
 if newpg!=me{return Err(crate::errno::EPERM)}
 s.tasks[idx].pgid=newpg;
 Ok(())
}
pub fn setsid()->Result<u32,i64>{
 let me=current_pid();
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();
 let idx=s.current;
 if s.tasks[idx].pgid==me{return Err(crate::errno::EPERM)}
 s.tasks[idx].pgid=me;s.tasks[idx].sid=me;Ok(me)
}
fn apply_ns_flags(p:&mut Process,flags:u64)->Result<(),i64>{
 const CLONE_NEWUTS:u64=0x04000000; const CLONE_NEWIPC:u64=0x08000000; const CLONE_NEWUSER:u64=0x10000000; const CLONE_NEWPID:u64=0x20000000; const CLONE_NEWNET:u64=0x40000000; const CLONE_NEWCGROUP:u64=0x02000000; const CLONE_NEWTIME:u64=0x00000080; const CLONE_NEWNS:u64=0x00020000;
 if flags&CLONE_NEWUTS!=0{p.ns_uts=alloc_uts_ns(p.ns_uts);}
 if flags&CLONE_NEWIPC!=0{p.ns_ipc=alloc_ns();}
  if flags&CLONE_NEWNS!=0{let ns=alloc_ns();crate::vfs::clone_mount_namespace(p.ns_mnt,ns)?;p.ns_mnt=ns;}
 if flags&CLONE_NEWPID!=0{p.ns_pid=alloc_ns();}
 if flags&CLONE_NEWNET!=0{p.ns_net=alloc_ns();}
 if flags&CLONE_NEWUSER!=0{p.ns_user=alloc_ns();p.uid=0;p.gid=0;p.euid=0;p.egid=0;p.suid=0;p.sgid=0;p.cap_eff=u64::MAX;p.cap_prm=u64::MAX;}
 if flags&CLONE_NEWCGROUP!=0{p.ns_cgroup=alloc_ns();}
  if flags&CLONE_NEWTIME!=0{p.ns_time=alloc_ns();}
  Ok(())
}
pub fn finish_current(code:i32)->!{finish(code,0)}
/* True when a pending signal is deliverable: it has a handler, or it is a
   default action that terminates. Used by pause() and blocking fcntl locks. */
pub fn has_deliverable_signal()->bool{
 with_current_process(|p|{
  let mut m=p.pending&!(p.sigmask&!crate::signal::unblockable_mask());
  while m!=0{
   let sig=m.trailing_zeros()as i32+1;
   let h=p.handlers[sig as usize].handler;
   if h!=crate::signal::SIG_IGN&&(h!=crate::signal::SIG_DFL||crate::signal::default_terminates(sig)){return true}
   m&=!crate::signal::bit(sig);
  }
  false
 }).unwrap_or(false)
}
/* Robust-futex list handling must run while the task's address space is
   still active. */
fn robust_exit(){
 let head=with_current_process(|p|{let h=p.robust_list_head;p.robust_list_head=0;h}).unwrap_or(0);
 if head!=0{crate::robust::process_exit(current_pid(),head);}
}
pub fn exit(code:i32)->!{
 let is_thread={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].is_thread};
 if is_thread{
  /* Threads share the address space: clear the child TID and wake any
     joiner, then leave the image and descriptors intact. */
  with_current_process(|proc|{
   let ctid=proc.clear_child_tid;
   if ctid!=0{
    let zero=0u64;
    let _=crate::usercopy::copy_to_user(ctid as usize,&zero.to_ne_bytes());
    let _=crate::futex::wake(ctid,1);
   }
  });
  robust_exit();
  crate::println!("thread {} exited {}",current_pid(),code);
  finish(code,0);
 }
 robust_exit();crate::println!("task {} exited {}",current_pid(),code);with_current_process(|proc|{crate::println!("task exited status={}",code);proc.teardown_exit(code,0)});finish(code,0)}
/* Kills the current task as if terminated by sig; wait status reports the signal. */
pub fn exit_signal(sig:i32)->!{robust_exit();crate::println!("task {} terminated by signal {}",current_pid(),sig);with_current_process(|proc|proc.teardown_exit(128+sig,sig as u64));finish(128+sig,sig as u8)}
pub fn stack_guard(){
 let r:u64;unsafe{core::arch::asm!("mov {}, rsp",out(reg)r,options(nostack,preserves_flags));}
 let top={let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].kernel_stack_top};
 let base=unsafe{core::ptr::addr_of!(STACKS)as u64};
 if top>=base+STACK_SIZE as u64&&top<=base+(MAX_TASKS*(STACK_SIZE+64))as u64&&r+1024<top.saturating_sub(STACK_SIZE as u64){
  crate::println!("fatal: kernel stack overflow pid={} rsp={:#x} top={:#x}",current_pid(),r,top);
  crate::arch::halt();
 }
}
pub fn exit_fault(vector:u64,error:u64,rip:u64,cr2:u64)->!{robust_exit();crate::println!("user fault vector={} error={:#x} rip={:#x} cr2={:#x}",vector,error,rip,cr2);with_current_process(|proc|proc.teardown_exit(128+11,vector));finish(128+11,11)}
fn schedule(blocked:bool){let(old_ptr,next,old_fpu,next_fpu)= {let mut s=SCHED.lock();let old=s.current;if !blocked&&s.tasks[old].state==State::Running{s.tasks[old].state=State::Runnable;}
 /* The user RSP is process-wide state in the syscall path; park the outgoing
    task's copy so a resuming thread restores its own. */
 if s.tasks[old].state!=State::Unused{s.tasks[old].user_rsp=unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::read_user_rsp()};}
 let mut next=old;for step in 1..=MAX_TASKS{let i=(old+step)%MAX_TASKS;if s.tasks[i].state==State::Runnable{next=i;break}}if next==old{s.tasks[old].state=State::Running;return}s.tasks[next].state=State::Running;s.current=next;(&mut s.tasks[old].rsp as*mut u64,s.tasks[next],s.tasks[old].fpu,s.tasks[next].fpu)};PID.store(next.pid,Ordering::Relaxed);unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::write_user_rsp(next.user_rsp);apply_arch_state(next);switch_context(old_ptr,next.rsp,old_fpu,next_fpu)}}

pub fn set_current_fs_base(value:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;s.tasks[current].fs_base=value;drop(s);unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::msr::wrmsr(crate::arch::x86_64::msr::IA32_FS_BASE,value)}}
pub fn fs_base()->u64{let s=SCHED.lock();s.tasks[s.current].fs_base}
pub fn bind_current_address_space(cr3:u64,kernel_stack_top:u64){let mut s=SCHED.lock();let current=s.current;s.tasks[current].cr3=cr3;s.tasks[current].kernel_stack_top=kernel_stack_top;drop(s);unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::descriptors::set_rsp0(kernel_stack_top);crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::set_kernel_stack(kernel_stack_top)}}
unsafe fn apply_arch_state(task:Task){unsafe{let current=crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PageTableRoot::current().pml4;if current!=task.cr3{crate::arch::x86_64::paging::PageTableRoot{pml4:task.cr3}.activate();}crate::arch::x86_64::msr::wrmsr(crate::arch::x86_64::msr::IA32_FS_BASE,task.fs_base);crate::arch::x86_64::descriptors::set_rsp0(task.kernel_stack_top);crate::arch::x86_64::syscall::set_kernel_stack(task.kernel_stack_top);}}
pub enum WaitOutcome{Reaped(u32,i32,u64,u64),NoHang,Blocked}
fn encode_status(t:Task)->i32{if t.term_signal!=0{t.term_signal as i32}else{(t.exit_code&0xff)<<8}}
pub fn reap_child(pid_sel:i64,nohang:bool)->Result<WaitOutcome,i64>{
 let me=current_pid();let(pslot,my_pgid)={let s=SCHED.lock();(s.current,s.tasks[s.current].pgid)};
 let matches=|t:&Task|t.ppid==me&&(pid_sel==-1||(pid_sel==0&&t.pgid==my_pgid)||(pid_sel>0&&t.pid==pid_sel as u32)||(pid_sel< -1&&t.pgid==(-pid_sel) as u32));
 let mut s=SCHED.lock();
 if let Some(slot)=s.tasks.iter().position(|t|t.state==State::Zombie&&matches(t)){let t=s.tasks[slot];s.tasks[slot]=EMPTY;drop(s);KERNEL_ENTRIES.lock()[slot]=None;USER_STARTS.lock()[slot]=None;FORK_RESUMES.lock()[slot]=None;let(utime,stime)={let mut procs=PROCS.lock();let times=procs[slot].take().map(|p|(p.utime_ticks.saturating_add(p.cutime_ticks),p.stime_ticks.saturating_add(p.cstime_ticks))).unwrap_or((0,0));if !t.is_thread{if let Some(pp)=procs[pslot].as_mut(){pp.cutime_ticks=pp.cutime_ticks.saturating_add(times.0);pp.cstime_ticks=pp.cstime_ticks.saturating_add(times.1);}}times};return Ok(WaitOutcome::Reaped(t.pid,encode_status(t),utime,stime))}
 let has_child=s.tasks.iter().any(|t|t.state!=State::Unused&&matches(t));drop(s);
 if !has_child{return Err(crate::errno::ECHILD)}
 if nohang{Ok(WaitOutcome::NoHang)}else{Ok(WaitOutcome::Blocked)}
}
pub fn wait_child(child_pid:u32)->Result<i32,i64>{loop{match reap_child(child_pid as i64,false)?{WaitOutcome::Reaped(_,code,_,_)=>return Ok(code),_=>{yield_now();/* Idle until the next interrupt so sleeping children can be woken by ticks. */unsafe{core::arch::asm!("sti","hlt","cli",options(nomem,nostack));}}}}}
pub fn scheduler_self_test()->Result<(),&'static str>{static A:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(0);static B:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(0);static TLS_OK:AtomicU32=AtomicU32::new(0);fn a(){set_current_fs_base(0x1000);A.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);yield_now();if unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::msr::rdmsr(crate::arch::x86_64::msr::IA32_FS_BASE)}==0x1000{TLS_OK.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);}A.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);}fn b(){set_current_fs_base(0x2000);B.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);yield_now();if unsafe{crate::arch::x86_64::msr::rdmsr(crate::arch::x86_64::msr::IA32_FS_BASE)}==0x2000{TLS_OK.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);}B.fetch_add(1,Ordering::Relaxed);}spawn_kernel(a).map_err(|_|"spawn A failed")?;spawn_kernel(b).map_err(|_|"spawn B failed")?;for _ in 0..8{yield_now();if A.load(Ordering::Relaxed)==2&&B.load(Ordering::Relaxed)==2&&TLS_OK.load(Ordering::Relaxed)==2{let ca=wait_child(2).map_err(|_|"reap A failed")?;let cb=wait_child(3).map_err(|_|"reap B failed")?;if ca!=0||cb!=0{return Err("unexpected child status")}let stats=resource_stats();if stats.active!=1||stats.zombies!=0||stats.unused!=MAX_TASKS-1{return Err("task slots not reclaimed after wait")}crate::println!("scheduler: context, FS base and zombie reap self-test passed");crate::println!("resources: tasks active={} zombies={} unused={}",stats.active,stats.zombies,stats.unused);return Ok(())}}Err("scheduler self-test stalled")}

//! Monotonic bootstrap time and xorshift64 random source.
use core::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64,Ordering};
const NS_PER_TICK:u64=1_000_000;
static RNG:AtomicU64=AtomicU64::new(0x6e6f_7274_6873_7461);
pub fn monotonic_ns()->u64{crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks().saturating_mul(NS_PER_TICK)}
static REALTIME_OFFSET_NS:AtomicU64=AtomicU64::new(0);
pub fn realtime_ns()->u64{monotonic_ns().wrapping_add(REALTIME_OFFSET_NS.load(Ordering::Relaxed))}
pub fn set_realtime_ns(ns:u64){REALTIME_OFFSET_NS.store(ns.wrapping_sub(monotonic_ns()),Ordering::Relaxed)}
pub fn ticks_to_ns(t:u64)->u64{t.saturating_mul(NS_PER_TICK)}
pub fn ns_to_ticks_ceil(ns:u64)->u64{ns.saturating_add(NS_PER_TICK-1)/NS_PER_TICK}
pub fn ticks_after_ns(ns:u64)->u64{crate::arch::x86_64::apic::ticks().saturating_add(ns.saturating_add(NS_PER_TICK-1)/NS_PER_TICK)}
pub fn fill_random(out:&mut[u8]){let mut x=RNG.load(Ordering::Relaxed)^monotonic_ns().rotate_left(17);for b in out{x^=x<<13;x^=x>>7;x^=x<<17;*b=x as u8;}RNG.store(x,Ordering::Relaxed)}
pub fn self_test()->bool{let a=monotonic_ns();let mut b=[0u8;16];fill_random(&mut b);monotonic_ns()>=a&&b.iter().any(|x|*x!=0)}

//! Fault-avoiding user copies for the bootstrap address space.
use crate::{arch::x86_64::{paging::{PageTableRoot,PRESENT,USER,WRITABLE},phys::Identity},errno};
pub const USER_MIN:usize=0x1000;pub const USER_MAX:usize=0x0000_8000_0000_0000;const PAGE:usize=4096;
fn checked(ptr:usize,len:usize)->Result<usize,i64>{if len==0{return Ok(ptr)}let end=ptr.checked_add(len).ok_or(errno::EFAULT)?;if ptr<USER_MIN||end>USER_MAX||end<ptr{return Err(errno::EFAULT)}Ok(end)}
/* Resolves one user address, materialising demand-paged and COW pages via the
   current process fault handler so syscall buffers work without pre-touching. */
fn resolve(va:usize,write:bool)->Result<u64,i64>{
 let root=PageTableRoot::current();let phys=Identity;
 let ok=|flags:u64|if write{flags&(PRESENT|USER|WRITABLE)==(PRESENT|USER|WRITABLE)}else{flags&(PRESENT|USER)==(PRESENT|USER)};
 if let Some((pa,flags))=root.query(&phys,va as u64){if ok(flags){return Ok(pa)}}
 if crate::task::has_current_process()&&crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.handle_page_fault(va as u64,write)).map(|r|r.is_ok()).unwrap_or(false){
  if let Some((pa,flags))=root.query(&phys,va as u64){if ok(flags){return Ok(pa)}}
 }
 Err(errno::EFAULT)
}
pub fn copy_from_user(dst:&mut[u8],src:usize)->Result<(),i64>{checked(src,dst.len())?;let phys=Identity;let mut done=0;while done<dst.len(){let va=src+done;let pa=resolve(va,false)?;let n=core::cmp::min(PAGE-(va&(PAGE-1)),dst.len()-done);let from=unsafe{phys.bytes(pa,n)};dst[done..done+n].copy_from_slice(from);done+=n;}Ok(())}
pub fn copy_to_user(dst:usize,src:&[u8])->Result<(),i64>{checked(dst,src.len())?;let phys=Identity;let mut done=0;while done<src.len(){let va=dst+done;let pa=resolve(va,true)?;let n=core::cmp::min(PAGE-(va&(PAGE-1)),src.len()-done);let to=unsafe{phys.bytes(pa,n)};to.copy_from_slice(&src[done..done+n]);done+=n;}Ok(())}

use crate::{arch::x86_64::{paging::PhysAccess,phys::{Identity,phys_to_virt}},errno,sync::SpinLock,vm::VmFlags};
pub type Result<T>=core::result::Result<T,i64>;

#[derive(Clone,Copy)]pub struct Inode{pub ino:u64,pub mode:u32,pub size:u64,pub capacity:u64,pub data:*mut u8,pub rdev:u64,pub pages:[u64;8],pub npages:u8,pub name:[u8;NAME_MAX+1],pub name_len:usize}

const EMPTY:Inode=Inode{ino:0,mode:0,size:0,capacity:0,data:core::ptr::null_mut(),rdev:0,pages:[0;8],npages:0,name:[0;NAME_MAX+1],name_len:0};

   it are read-only, like a real initramfs root. */
static INITRAMFS_SPAN:SpinLock<(u64,u64)>=SpinLock::new((0,0));

const MAX_MOUNTS:usize=16;

#[derive(Clone,Copy,PartialEq,Eq)]enum MountKind{Tmpfs,Ext2}

const EMPTY_MOUNT:MountEntry=MountEntry{used:false,ns:0,kind:MountKind::Tmpfs,fs_id:0,target:[0;128],tlen:0};

fn current_mount_ns()->u64{crate::task::with_current_process(|p|p.ns_mnt).unwrap_or(1)}
fn path_has_prefix(path:&str,prefix:&str)->bool{path==prefix||(path.starts_with(prefix)&&(prefix=="/"||path.as_bytes().get(prefix.len())==Some(&b'/')))}
fn route_path(path:&str,ns:u64)->PathRoute{
 let mut route=PathRoute{kind:None,fs_id:0,target:[0;128],tlen:0,relative:[0;128],rlen:0};
 let mut best:Option<MountEntry>=None;
 {
  let registry=MOUNT_REGISTRY.lock();
  for entry in registry.entries.iter().copied(){
   if !entry.used||entry.ns!=ns{continue}
   let target=core::str::from_utf8(&entry.target[..entry.tlen]).unwrap_or("");
   if path_has_prefix(path,target)&&best.map(|old|old.tlen<entry.tlen).unwrap_or(true){best=Some(entry);}
  }
 }
 if let Some(entry)=best{
  route.kind=Some(entry.kind);route.fs_id=entry.fs_id;route.tlen=entry.tlen;route.target=entry.target;
  let suffix=&path[entry.tlen..];let relative=if suffix.is_empty(){"/"}else{suffix};
  route.rlen=relative.len().min(127);route.relative[..route.rlen].copy_from_slice(&relative.as_bytes()[..route.rlen]);
  return route
 }
 /* Keep the legacy virtio-ext2 mount visible unless an explicit mount shadows it. */
 if crate::ext2::ready()&&path_has_prefix(path,"/mnt"){
  route.kind=Some(MountKind::Ext2);route.target[..4].copy_from_slice(b"/mnt");route.tlen=4;
  let suffix=&path[4..];let relative=if suffix.is_empty(){"/"}else{suffix};
  route.rlen=relative.len().min(127);route.relative[..route.rlen].copy_from_slice(&relative.as_bytes()[..route.rlen]);
  return route
 }
 route.rlen=path.len().min(127);route.relative[..route.rlen].copy_from_slice(&path.as_bytes()[..route.rlen]);
 route
}

pub fn mount_filesystem(source:&str,target:&str,fstype:&str,flags:u64,ns:u64)->Result<()>{
 let _=source;let _=flags;
 if !target.starts_with('/')||target=="/"||target.as_bytes().contains(&0){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 if target.len()>127{return Err(errno::ENAMETOOLONG)}
 let kind=match fstype{"tmpfs"=>MountKind::Tmpfs,"ext2"=>MountKind::Ext2,_=>return Err(errno::ENODEV)};
 let point=lookup_in_namespace(target,ns)?;
 if point.mode&0o170000!=S_IFDIR{return Err(errno::ENOTDIR)}
 if kind==MountKind::Ext2&&!crate::ext2::ready(){return Err(errno::ENODEV)}
 let mut registry=MOUNT_REGISTRY.lock();
 if registry.entries.iter().any(|e|e.used&&e.ns==ns&&core::str::from_utf8(&e.target[..e.tlen]).ok()==Some(target)){return Err(errno::EBUSY)}
 let entry_slot=registry.entries.iter().position(|e|!e.used).ok_or(errno::ENOMEM)?;
 let fs_id=if kind==MountKind::Tmpfs{
  let id=registry.tmpfs.iter().position(|s|!s.used).ok_or(errno::ENOSPC)?;
  registry.tmpfs[id]=TmpfsStore{used:true,..EMPTY_TMPFS};id
 }else{0};
 let mut name=[0u8;128];name[..target.len()].copy_from_slice(target.as_bytes());
 registry.entries[entry_slot]=MountEntry{used:true,ns,kind,fs_id,target:name,tlen:target.len()};
 Ok(())
}
pub fn unmount_filesystem(target:&str,flags:u64,ns:u64)->Result<()>{
  let _=flags;let mut registry=MOUNT_REGISTRY.lock();
  let index=registry.entries.iter().position(|e|e.used&&e.ns==ns&&core::str::from_utf8(&e.target[..e.tlen]).ok()==Some(target)).ok_or(errno::EINVAL)?;

  registry.entries[index].used=false;Ok(())
}

pub fn clone_mount_namespace(source_ns:u64,destination_ns:u64)->Result<()>{
 let mut registry=MOUNT_REGISTRY.lock();
 let mut source=[EMPTY_MOUNT;MAX_MOUNTS];let mut count=0usize;
 for entry in registry.entries.iter().copied(){if entry.used&&entry.ns==source_ns{source[count]=entry;count+=1;}}
 let free=registry.entries.iter().filter(|e|!e.used).count();
 if free<count{return Err(errno::ENOMEM)}

 for i in 0..MAX_MOUNTS{if registry.entries[i].used{continue}while next<count{let mut entry=source[next];next+=1;entry.ns=destination_ns;registry.entries[i]=entry;break}if next>=count{break}}
 Ok(())
}

}
fn tmpfs_lookup(fs_id:usize,relative:&str,visible:&str)->Result<Inode>{

}
fn tmpfs_parent_exists(store:&TmpfsStore,path:&str)->bool{

}
fn tmpfs_add_node(fs_id:usize,relative:&str,visible:&str,mode:u32,rdev:u64,data:Option<&[u8]>)->Result<Inode>{
 if !relative.starts_with('/')||relative=="/"||relative.as_bytes().contains(&0){return Err(errno::EINVAL)}
 if relative.len()>NAME_MAX{return Err(errno::ENAMETOOLONG)}

 if store.len==MAX_NODES{return Err(errno::ENOSPC)}

  let frame=crate::memory::alloc_frame().ok_or(errno::ENOMEM)?;
  unsafe{Identity.table(frame).fill(0)}

}
fn tmpfs_readdir(fs_id:usize,relative:&str,mut cb:impl FnMut(u64,u32,&str))->Result<usize>{
 let mut entries:[(u64,u32,[u8;NAME_MAX+1],usize);MAX_NODES]=[(0,0,[0;NAME_MAX+1],0);MAX_NODES];let mut count=0usize;
 {
  let registry=MOUNT_REGISTRY.lock();let store=registry.tmpfs.get(fs_id).filter(|s|s.used).ok_or(errno::ENOENT)?;
  let dir=if relative.is_empty(){"/"}else{relative.trim_end_matches('/')};
  for i in 0..store.len{let node=store.nodes[i];if !store.linked[i]||node.ino==0{continue}
   let name=core::str::from_utf8(&node.name[..node.name_len]).map_err(|_|errno::EINVAL)?;
   let child=if dir=="/"{name.strip_prefix('/').unwrap_or("")}else{name.strip_prefix(dir).and_then(|r|r.strip_prefix('/')).unwrap_or("")};
   if child.is_empty()||child.contains('/')||count==MAX_NODES{continue}
   let mut b=[0u8;NAME_MAX+1];b[..child.len()].copy_from_slice(child.as_bytes());entries[count]=(node.ino,node.mode,b,child.len());count+=1;
  }
 }
 for(ino,mode,name,len)in entries[..count].iter(){let n=core::str::from_utf8(&name[..*len]).map_err(|_|errno::EINVAL)?;cb(*ino,*mode,n)}
 Ok(count)
}
fn tmpfs_unlink(fs_id:usize,relative:&str,visible:&str)->Result<()>{
