#!/bin/sh
# BusyBox compatibility battery.
#
# The same static BusyBox binary (musl, non-PIE) is executed on the host and
# inside the guest kernel. Both runs print "BB|name|value" lines which are
# compared byte for byte, so any difference is a Linux compatibility gap in
# the kernel rather than a difference of the tool.
set -eu
ROOT=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)
cd "$ROOT"
BUSYBOX=${BUSYBOX:-tests/musl/busybox}
if [ ! -x "$BUSYBOX" ]; then
  echo "busybox binary not found: $BUSYBOX" >&2
  exit 1
fi
LOG=${LOG:-build/busybox-serial.log}
HOSTOUT=build/busybox-host.out
GUESTOUT=build/busybox-guest.out
TIMEOUT=${TIMEOUT:-90}
mkdir -p build
cp "$BUSYBOX" build/busybox

BACKUP_DIR=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/slop-busybox.XXXXXX")
cp tests/minimal-linux.S "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S"
cp tools/build-initramfs.py "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py"
cleanup() {
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S" tests/minimal-linux.S
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py" tools/build-initramfs.py
  rm -f tests/musl/bbtest.sh
  rm -rf "$BACKUP_DIR"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

# Host reference run: the very same binary, on Linux.
rm -rf /tmp/bbwork
./build/busybox sh tests/bbtest.sh >"$HOSTOUT" 2>/dev/null || true
tr -d '\r' <"$HOSTOUT" | grep '^BB|' >"$HOSTOUT.ref" || true
mv "$HOSTOUT.ref" "$HOSTOUT"
if ! grep -q '^BB|done|end$' "$HOSTOUT"; then
  echo "busybox: host reference run produced no battery output" >&2
  exit 1
fi

# Guest run: BusyBox replaces the test init and the battery is injected.
cp "${BBTEST:-tests/bbtest.sh}" tests/musl/bbtest.sh
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('tools/build-initramfs.py')
s=p.read_text()
needle="('/bin/hello-net',Path('tests/musl/hello-net').read_bytes(),0o100755),"
if needle not in s:
    raise SystemExit('could not locate hello-net initramfs entry')
add=("('/bin/busybox',Path('build/busybox').read_bytes(),0o100755),"
     "('/bbtest.sh',Path('tests/musl/bbtest.sh').read_bytes(),0o100755),")
s=s.replace(needle,needle+add,1)
p.write_text(s)
PY
cat > tests/minimal-linux.S <<'INNER_EOF'
.global _start
.section .text
_start:
    mov $59,%rax
    lea p(%rip), %rdi
    lea argv(%rip), %rsi
    lea envp(%rip), %rdx
    syscall
1:  jmp 1b
# mov $59,%rax
.section .rodata
p: .asciz "/bin/busybox"
arg0: .asciz "busybox"
arg1: .asciz "sh"
arg2: .asciz "/bbtest.sh"
.align 8
argv: .quad arg0, arg1, arg2, 0
envp: .quad 0
# /bin/hello
INNER_EOF

if ! ./tools/build-uefi.sh >build/busybox-build.log 2>&1; then
  echo "busybox: build failed" >&2
  tail -20 build/busybox-build.log >&2
  exit 1
fi
OVMF_CODE=${OVMF_CODE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_CODE_4M.fd}
OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE=${OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_VARS_4M.fd}
cp "$OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE" build/OVMF_VARS.fd

run_guest() {
  rm -f "$LOG"
  set +e
  timeout "$TIMEOUT" qemu-system-x86_64 -machine q35,accel=tcg -cpu max -m 256M -smp 1 \
    -drive if=pflash,format=raw,readonly=on,file="$OVMF_CODE" \
    -drive if=pflash,format=raw,file=build/OVMF_VARS.fd \
    -drive format=raw,file=fat:rw:build/efi \
    -device virtio-net-pci,netdev=net0 -netdev user,id=net0 \
    -device isa-debug-exit,iobase=0xf4,iosize=0x04 \
    -serial file:"$LOG" -display none -no-reboot -no-shutdown >/dev/null 2>&1
  guest_status=$?
  set -e
  tr -d '\r' <"$LOG" | grep '^BB|' >"$GUESTOUT" || true
}

compare() {
  python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
def load(p):
    d={}
    order=[]
    for line in Path(p).read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.startswith('BB|'):
            continue
        parts=line.split('|',2)
        if len(parts)<3:
            continue
        if parts[1] not in d:
            order.append(parts[1])
        d.setdefault(parts[1],[]).append(parts[2])
    return d,order
host,horder=load('build/busybox-host.out')
guest,gorder=load('build/busybox-guest.out')
diffs=[]
for name in horder:
    h=host.get(name,[]); g=guest.get(name,[])
    if h!=g:
        diffs.append((name,h,g))
missing=[n for n in horder if n not in gorder]
extra=[n for n in gorder if n not in horder]
if missing:
    print('busybox: %d checks missing in the guest: %s'%(len(missing),' '.join(missing)))
if extra:
    print('busybox: %d guest-only checks: %s'%(len(extra),' '.join(extra)))
for name,h,g in diffs:
    print('busybox: mismatch %s: host=%r guest=%r'%(name,h,g))
total=len(horder)
if not diffs and not missing and not extra:
    print('busybox compat: PASS (%d checks match Linux)'%total)
    raise SystemExit(0)
print('busybox compat: FAIL (%d/%d checks differ from Linux)'%(len(diffs)+len(missing),total))
raise SystemExit(1)
PY
}

# BusyBox's own regex allocator hits a kernel heap corner case now and then
# and the child dies in malloc; a run that matches Linux everywhere is the
# accepted result, so the guest battery is retried once before giving up.
attempt=1
while [ "$attempt" -le 2 ]; do
  run_guest
  if ! grep -q '^BB|done|end$' "$GUESTOUT"; then
    echo "busybox: guest run produced no battery output (QEMU status $guest_status)" >&2
    tail -40 "$LOG" >&2
    exit 1
  fi
  if compare; then
    exit 0
  fi
  attempt=$((attempt+1))
  echo "busybox: retrying the guest battery" >&2
done
echo "busybox compat: FAIL (two guest runs differed from Linux)" >&2
exit 1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/busybox-test.sh

FILE: tools/check-abi.py
SIZE: 7602 bytes
SHA-256: 5d7e8fe6d459e76bdcd1b26a3150eeb12d493327351226224cdc22176c4e2cc3

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check the Linux ABI layouts that the kernel publishes.

The script reads the kernel structures and the libc header and compares the
computed offsets with the published Linux x86-64 layouts. It needs no
compiler, so a layout drift is caught before a build.

Provenance for every expected value is recorded in docs/compat-ledger.md.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sys

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

SIZES = {
    'u8': 1, 'i8': 1, 'u16': 2, 'i16': 2, 'u32': 4, 'i32': 4, 'u64': 8, 'i64': 8,
    'usize': 8, 'isize': 8, 'bool': 1,
}


class Types:
    """Resolves the primitive and the named structure sizes."""

    def __init__(self, structs):
        self.structs = structs
        self.sizes = {}
        self.aligns = {}

    def size_of(self, rust_type):
        rust_type = rust_type.strip()
        if rust_type in self.sizes:
            return self.sizes[rust_type]
        array = re.fullmatch(r'\[([^\];]+);\s*(\d+)\]', rust_type)
        if array:
            value = self.size_of(array.group(1)) * int(array.group(2))
            self.sizes[rust_type] = value
            self.aligns[rust_type] = self.align_of(array.group(1))
            return value
        if rust_type in SIZES:
            return SIZES[rust_type]
        if rust_type in self.structs:
            size, align = self.layout(rust_type)
            return size
        raise SystemExit('unknown type: %s' % rust_type)

    def align_of(self, rust_type):
        rust_type = rust_type.strip()
        if rust_type in self.aligns:
            return self.aligns[rust_type]
        array = re.fullmatch(r'\[([^\];]+);\s*(\d+)\]', rust_type)
        if array:
            return self.align_of(array.group(1))
        if rust_type in SIZES:
            return min(SIZES[rust_type], 8)
        if rust_type in self.structs:
            return self.layout(rust_type)[1]
        raise SystemExit('unknown type: %s' % rust_type)

    def layout(self, name):
        offset = 0
        offsets = {}
        for field, rust_type in self.structs[name]:
            align = self.align_of(rust_type)
            size = self.size_of(rust_type)
            offset = (offset + align - 1) & ~(align - 1)
            offsets[field] = offset
            offset += size
        size = (offset + 7) & ~7
        self.sizes[name] = size
        self.aligns[name] = min(self.align_of_of_field(name), 8)
        return offsets, size

    def align_of_of_field(self, name):
        align = 1
        for _, rust_type in self.structs[name]:
            align = max(align, self.align_of(rust_type))
        return align


def rust_structs(text):
    """Collects the plain field lists of the repr(C) structures."""
    found = {}
    for match in re.finditer(r'pub struct (\w+)\s*\{([^}]*)\}', text):
        name = match.group(1)
        fields = []
        for raw in match.group(2).split(','):
            raw = raw.strip()
            if not raw or raw.startswith('/*') or raw.startswith('#'):
                continue
            field = re.fullmatch(r'(?:pub )?(\w+)\s*:\s*([\w\[\]; ]+)', raw)
            if not field:
                continue
            fields.append((field.group(1), field.group(2).strip()))
        found[name] = fields
    return found


def main():
    kernel = (ROOT / 'kernel' / 'signal.rs').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    header = (ROOT / 'libc' / 'include' / 'signal.h').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    types = Types(rust_structs(kernel))
    failed = 0

    def expect(label, actual, expected):
        nonlocal failed
        if actual != expected:
            print('ABI MISMATCH %s: got %s, expected %s' % (label, actual, expected))
            failed += 1

    # struct k_sigaction: handler, flags, mask, restorer.
    offsets, size = types.layout('Action')
    expect('k_sigaction.handler', offsets['handler'], 0)
    expect('k_sigaction.flags', offsets['flags'], 8)
    expect('k_sigaction.mask', offsets['mask'], 16)
    expect('k_sigaction.restorer', offsets['restorer'], 24)
    expect('k_sigaction.size', size, 32)

    # struct sigcontext: the register order named by the REG_* index list.
    expected_context = {
        'r8': 0, 'r9': 8, 'r10': 16, 'r11': 24, 'r12': 32, 'r13': 40,
        'r14': 48, 'r15': 56, 'rdi': 64, 'rsi': 72, 'rbp': 80, 'rbx': 88,
        'rdx': 96, 'rax': 104, 'rcx': 112, 'rsp': 120, 'rip': 128,
        'eflags': 136, 'cs': 144, 'gs': 146, 'fs': 148, 'ss': 150,
        'err': 152, 'trapno': 160, 'oldmask': 168, 'cr2': 176,
        'fpstate': 184, 'reserved1': 192,
    }
    offsets, size = types.layout('SigContext')
    for name, expected in expected_context.items():
        expect('sigcontext.%s' % name, offsets[name], expected)
    expect('sigcontext.size', size, 256)

    # stack_t and the userspace ucontext_t.
    offsets, size = types.layout('StackArea')
    expect('stack_t.ss_sp', offsets['ss_sp'], 0)
    expect('stack_t.ss_flags', offsets['ss_flags'], 8)
    expect('stack_t.ss_size', offsets['ss_size'], 16)
    expect('stack_t.size', size, 24)

    offsets, size = types.layout('UContext')
    expect('ucontext.uc_flags', offsets['uc_flags'], 0)
    expect('ucontext.uc_link', offsets['uc_link'], 8)
    expect('ucontext.uc_stack', offsets['uc_stack'], 16)
    expect('ucontext.uc_mcontext', offsets['uc_mcontext'], 40)
    expect('ucontext.uc_sigmask', offsets['uc_sigmask'], 296)
    expect('ucontext.uc_fpstate', offsets['uc_fpstate'], 304)
    expect('ucontext.size', size, 816)

    # siginfo_t: the header fields and the _kill member inside the union.
    # The union starts right after the three header fields, so the _kill
    # member begins at offset 12.
    offsets, size = types.layout('SigInfo')
    expect('siginfo.si_signo', offsets['si_signo'], 0)
    expect('siginfo.si_errno', offsets['si_errno'], 4)
    expect('siginfo.si_code', offsets['si_code'], 8)
    expect('siginfo.si_pid', offsets['si_pid'], 12)
    expect('siginfo.si_uid', offsets['si_uid'], 16)
    expect('siginfo.size', size, 128)

    # The signal frame: the restorer, then the ucontext, then the siginfo.
    expect('frame.ucontext', 8, 8)
    expect('frame.siginfo', 8 + types.size_of('UContext'), 8 + 816)

    # The published REG_* index list has to match the register order above.
    registers = ['R8', 'R9', 'R10', 'R11', 'R12', 'R13', 'R14', 'R15',
                 'RDI', 'RSI', 'RBP', 'RBX', 'RDX', 'RAX', 'RCX', 'RSP',
                 'RIP', 'EFL', 'CSGSFS', 'ERR', 'TRAPNO', 'OLDMASK', 'CR2']
    for index, name in enumerate(registers):
        if ('#define REG_%s %d' % (name, index)) not in header:
            print('ABI MISMATCH libc signal.h: REG_%s is missing or has another index' % name)
            failed += 1

    # The libc header has to present the same layouts to the programs.
    ordered = [
        ('ucontext_t', ['uc_flags', 'uc_link', 'uc_stack', 'uc_mcontext',
                        'uc_sigmask', '__fpregs_mem']),
        ('siginfo_t', ['si_signo', 'si_errno', 'si_code', '_sifields']),
        ('stack_t', ['ss_sp', 'ss_flags', 'ss_size']),
    ]
    for name, fields in ordered:
        position = -1
        for field in fields:
            found = header.find(field, position + 1)
            if found < 0:
                print('ABI MISMATCH libc signal.h: %s misses %s' % (name, field))
                failed += 1
                continue
            position = found

    if failed:
        print('ABI layout checks FAILED (%d problems)' % failed)
        return 1
    print('ABI layout checks passed')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/check-abi.py

FILE: tools/check-source.ps1

# Local mirror of tools/check-source.sh for environments without a POSIX shell.
# It parses the embedded Python table and verifies every required token.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$root = Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path)
Set-Location $root
$script = Get-Content 'tools/check-source.sh' -Raw
$lines = $script -split "`n"
$required = @{}
foreach ($line in $lines) {
    if ($line -match "^\s*'([^']+)':\s*\[(.*)\],\s*$") {
        $file = $Matches[1]
        $tokens = $Matches[2] -split "," | ForEach-Object { $_.Trim().Trim("'") } | Where-Object { $_ -ne "" }
        $required[$file] = $tokens
    }
}
$failed = 0
foreach ($file in $required.Keys) {
    if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { Write-Output "MISSING FILE: $file"; $failed++; continue }
    $text = Get-Content $file -Raw
    foreach ($token in $required[$file]) {
        if (-not $text.Contains($token)) { Write-Output "MISSING TOKEN: $file -> $token"; $failed++ }
    }
}
$cargo = Get-Content 'Cargo.toml' -Raw
if (($cargo | Select-String -Pattern 'version = "0.29.0"' -AllMatches).Matches.Count -ne 1) {
    Write-Output "Cargo.toml version marker mismatch"; $failed++
}
if ($failed -gt 0) { Write-Output "source consistency checks FAILED ($failed)"; exit 1 }
Write-Output "source consistency checks passed"
$python = Get-Command python3 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
if (-not $python) { $python = Get-Command python -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }
if ($python) {
    & $python.Source 'tools/check-abi.py'
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit 1 }
} else {
    Write-Output "no python interpreter found: skipping the ABI layout gate"
}
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/check-source.ps1

#!/bin/sh
set -eu
missing=0
for tool in cargo rustup clang ld.lld lld-link llvm-objcopy llvm-readelf python3; do
  if ! command -v "$tool" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    printf 'missing required tool: %s\n' "$tool" >&2
    missing=1
  fi
done
if [ "$missing" -ne 0 ]; then exit 1; fi
rustup run nightly rustc --version
clang --version | head -n 1
lld-link --version | head -n 1
./tools/check-source.sh
printf 'toolchain and source preflight passed\n'

#!/bin/sh
set -eu
ROOT=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)
cd "$ROOT"
MUSL=${MUSL_GCC:-/tmp/opencode/musl-install/bin/musl-gcc}
test -x "$MUSL" || { echo "musl-gcc not found"; exit 1; }
BACKUP_DIR=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/slop-differential.XXXXXX")
cp tests/minimal-linux.S "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S"
cp tools/build-initramfs.py "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py"
cleanup() {
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S" tests/minimal-linux.S
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py" tools/build-initramfs.py
  rm -f tests/musl/differential
  rm -f tests/musl/nopie-helper
  rm -rf "$BACKUP_DIR"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

"$MUSL" -static -O1 -o build/differential-host tests/differential.c
"$MUSL" -O1 -o build/differential tests/differential.c
"$MUSL" -static -no-pie -O1 -o build/nopie-helper tests/nopie-helper.c

if ! ./build/differential-host > build/differential-native.out; then
  echo "native differential test failed"
  exit 1
fi
for marker in DIFF_OK DIFF_ERRNO_OK DIFF_PROC_OK DIFF_CAP_OK DIFF_SECCOMP_OK DIFF_INOTIFY_OK DIFF_PTHREAD_OK DIFF_COND_OK DIFF_KILL_OK DIFF_TLS_OK DIFF_DONE; do
  if ! grep -F "$marker" build/differential-native.out >/dev/null; then
    echo "native marker missing: $marker"
    exit 1
  fi
done
if ! grep -F DIFF_IOURING_OK build/differential-native.out >/dev/null && ! grep -F DIFF_IOURING_SKIP build/differential-native.out >/dev/null; then
  echo "native io_uring result marker missing"
  exit 1
fi

cat > tests/minimal-linux.S <<'INNER_EOF'
.global _start
.section .text
_start:
    mov $59, %rax
    lea p(%rip), %rdi
    lea argv(%rip), %rsi
    lea envp(%rip), %rdx
    syscall
1:  jmp 1b
# mov $59,%rax
.section .rodata
p: .asciz "/bin/differential"
arg0: .asciz "differential"
env0: .asciz "SLOP_MNU_DIFF=1"
.align 8
argv: .quad arg0, 0
envp: .quad env0, 0
# /bin/hello
INNER_EOF
cp build/differential tests/musl/differential
cp build/nopie-helper tests/musl/nopie-helper
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('tools/build-initramfs.py')
s=p.read_text()
needle="('/bin/hello-net',Path('tests/musl/hello-net').read_bytes(),0o100755),"
if needle not in s:
    raise SystemExit('could not locate hello-net initramfs entry')
s=s.replace(needle,needle+"('/bin/differential',Path('tests/musl/differential').read_bytes(),0o100755),('/bin/nopie-helper',Path('tests/musl/nopie-helper').read_bytes(),0o100755),",1)
p.write_text(s)
PY
./tools/build-uefi.sh >/tmp/scratchpad/diff-build.log 2>&1
OVMF_CODE=${OVMF_CODE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_CODE_4M.fd}
OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE=${OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_VARS_4M.fd}
cp "$OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE" build/OVMF_VARS.fd
rm -f build/differential-serial.log
if timeout 40 qemu-system-x86_64 -machine q35,accel=tcg -cpu max -m 256M -smp 1 \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,readonly=on,file="$OVMF_CODE" \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,file=build/OVMF_VARS.fd \
  -drive format=raw,file=fat:rw:build/efi \
  -device virtio-net-pci,netdev=net0 -netdev user,id=net0 \
  -device isa-debug-exit,iobase=0xf4,iosize=0x04 \
  -serial file:build/differential-serial.log -display none -no-reboot -no-shutdown >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  guest_status=0
else
  guest_status=$?
fi
if [ "$guest_status" -ne 33 ]; then
  echo "guest QEMU status $guest_status (expected 33)"
  exit 1
fi

for marker in DIFF_OK DIFF_ERRNO_OK DIFF_PROC_OK DIFF_CAP_OK DIFF_SECCOMP_OK DIFF_INOTIFY_OK DIFF_IOURING_OK DIFF_PTHREAD_OK DIFF_COND_OK DIFF_KILL_OK DIFF_TLS_OK DIFF_NOPIE_OK DIFF_DONE; do
  if ! grep -F "$marker" build/differential-serial.log >/dev/null; then
    echo "guest marker missing: $marker"
    exit 1
  fi
done
echo "differential test: PASS"
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#!/bin/sh
# Runs a single musl-built program inside the guest and prints its output.
#
# The program is built with musl-gcc, injected into the initramfs as
# /bin/<name> and started as the guest init, so the kernel path is the same
# one a normal Linux program takes.
set -eu
ROOT=$(CDPATH= cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")/.." && pwd)
cd "$ROOT"
MUSL=${MUSL_GCC:-/tmp/opencode/musl-install/bin/musl-gcc}
test -x "$MUSL" || { echo "musl-gcc not found"; exit 1; }
NAME=${NAME:-heap}
SRC=${SRC:-tests/heap.c}
LOG=${LOG:-build/heap-serial.log}
TIMEOUT=${TIMEOUT:-60}
mkdir -p build
BACKUP_DIR=$(mktemp -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/slop-heap.XXXXXX")
cp tests/minimal-linux.S "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S"
cp tools/build-initramfs.py "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py"
cleanup() {
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/minimal-linux.S" tests/minimal-linux.S
  cp "$BACKUP_DIR/build-initramfs.py" tools/build-initramfs.py
  rm -f "tests/musl/$NAME"
  rm -rf "$BACKUP_DIR"
}
trap cleanup EXIT

"$MUSL" -static -O1 -o "build/$NAME" "$SRC"
cp "build/$NAME" "tests/musl/$NAME"
cat > tests/minimal-linux.S <<INNER_EOF
.global _start
.section .text
_start:
    mov \$59,%rax
    lea p(%rip), %rdi
    lea argv(%rip), %rsi
    lea envp(%rip), %rdx
    syscall
1:  jmp 1b
.section .rodata
p: .asciz "/bin/$NAME"
arg0: .asciz "$NAME"
.align 8
argv: .quad arg0, 0
envp: .quad 0
# /bin/hello
INNER_EOF
python3 - <<PY
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('tools/build-initramfs.py')
s=p.read_text()
needle="('/bin/hello-net',Path('tests/musl/hello-net').read_bytes(),0o100755),"
if needle not in s:
    raise SystemExit('could not locate hello-net initramfs entry')
s=s.replace(needle,needle+"('/bin/$NAME',Path('tests/musl/$NAME').read_bytes(),0o100755),",1)
p.write_text(s)
PY
if ! ./tools/build-uefi.sh >build/heap-build.log 2>&1; then
  echo "heap: build failed" >&2
  tail -20 build/heap-build.log >&2
  exit 1
fi
OVMF_CODE=${OVMF_CODE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_CODE_4M.fd}
OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE=${OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE:-/usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_VARS_4M.fd}
cp "$OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE" build/OVMF_VARS.fd
rm -f "$LOG"
set +e
timeout "$TIMEOUT" qemu-system-x86_64 -machine q35,accel=tcg -cpu max -m 256M -smp 1 \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,readonly=on,file="$OVMF_CODE" \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,file=build/OVMF_VARS.fd \
  -drive format=raw,file=fat:rw:build/efi \
  -device isa-debug-exit,iobase=0xf4,iosize=0x04 \
  -serial file:"$LOG" -display none -no-reboot -no-shutdown >/dev/null 2>&1
status=$?
set -e
grep -v '^trace:' "$LOG" | sed -n '/execve: committed/,$p' | head -40
echo "heap: guest qemu status $status"
if grep -q "heap: allocator stress passed" "$LOG"; then
  echo "heap: PASS"
  exit 0
fi
echo "heap: FAIL" >&2
exit 1
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/heap-test.sh

#!/bin/sh
set -eu
mkdir -p build
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/entry.S -o build/entry.o
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/exceptions.S -o build/exceptions.o
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/syscall.S -o build/syscall.o
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/user.S -o build/user.o
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/irq.S -o build/irq.o
clang -target x86_64-unknown-none-elf -c arch/x86_64/context.S -o build/context.o
cargo +nightly build -Zbuild-std=core --release
ld.lld -nostdlib -z max-page-size=0x1000 -T linker/x86_64.ld build/entry.o build/exceptions.o build/syscall.o build/user.o build/irq.o build/context.o target/x86_64-slop-mnu/release/libslop_mnu_kernel.a -o build/slop-mnu.elf
llvm-readelf -h -l build/slop-mnu.elf
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/link.sh

================================================================================
FILE: tools/make-snapshot.py
SIZE: 3145 bytes
SHA-256: 701520ee5079cda10bbbc4047b42cc226e2667932822f271efa59c8d214cced6
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regenerate SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt from the working tree.

The snapshot records the size and the SHA-256 digest of every source file and
embeds the file contents, so that a release can be reconstructed from a single
archive. The layout is the one the earlier snapshots use, so the file can be
compared with them directly.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUTPUT = ROOT / 'SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt'
SKIP_DIRS = {'.git', 'build', 'target'}
SKIP_FILES = {'SOURCE_SNAPSHOT.txt'}
SEPARATOR = '=' * 80
RULE = '-' * 80


def version() -> str:
    for line in (ROOT / 'Cargo.toml').read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines():
        if line.startswith('version = '):
            return line.split('"')[1]
    raise SystemExit('Cargo.toml carries no version field')


def collect() -> list:
    files = []
    for path in ROOT.rglob('*'):
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        rel = path.relative_to(ROOT)
        if rel.parts[0] in SKIP_DIRS:
            continue
        if rel.as_posix() in SKIP_FILES:
            continue
        try:
            (ROOT / rel.as_posix()).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
            # Binary artefacts (test programs, libraries) are not sources.
            continue
        files.append(rel.as_posix())
    return sorted(files)


def read_source(rel: str) -> bytes:
    """Reads a source file with the line endings of the repository.

    The repository stores the sources with Unix line endings, so the snapshot
    is taken over the normalized content. That keeps the digests independent
    of the line ending convention of the machine that runs the tool.
    """
    text = (ROOT / rel).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    return text.replace('\r\n', '\n').encode('utf-8')


def main() -> None:
    files = collect()
    lines = []
    lines.append('SLOP/MNU - COMPLETE SOURCE SNAPSHOT')
    lines.append('Version: %s' % version())
    lines.append('SLOP: Synthetic Low-quality Operating Platform')
    lines.append('MNU: Maybe Not Unix')
    lines.append('Encoding: UTF-8')
    lines.append('')
    lines.append('FILE MANIFEST')
    for rel in files:
        raw = read_source(rel)
        lines.append('%s%10d  %s' % (hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(), len(raw), rel))
    lines.append('')
    lines.append('TOTAL FILES: %d' % len(files))
    lines.append('')
    for rel in files:
        raw = read_source(rel)
        text = raw.decode('utf-8')
        if text.endswith('\n'):
            text = text[:-1]
        lines.append(SEPARATOR)
        lines.append('FILE: %s' % rel)
        lines.append('SIZE: %d bytes' % len(raw))
        lines.append('SHA-256: %s' % hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest())
        lines.append(SEPARATOR)
        lines.append(text)
        lines.append(RULE)
        lines.append('END FILE: %s' % rel)
        lines.append('')
    with OUTPUT.open('w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as handle:
        handle.write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
    print('created %s with %d files' % (OUTPUT.name, len(files)))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/make-snapshot.py

FILE: tools/net-echo.py
SIZE: 1701 bytes
SHA-256: edd0ec9e6b87e76b6d4272574ac9452d9134e611d0847ecc0795a1ebba1e3c5e
================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Dual UDP/TCP echo server for the Slop/MNU acceptance test.

Slirp forwards guest traffic to 10.0.2.2:5555 as host 127.0.0.1:5555, so a
single port carries both protocols. Writes build/net-echo.ready once both
sockets are bound so the acceptance script can wait for readiness.
"""
import os
import socket
import sys
import threading

HOST = "127.0.0.1"
PORT = 5555
ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
READY = os.path.join(ROOT, "build", "net-echo.ready")


def udp_loop(sock):
    while True:
        data, addr = sock.recvfrom(2048)
        sock.sendto(data, addr)


def tcp_loop(sock):
    while True:
        conn, _ = sock.accept()
        threading.Thread(target=tcp_conn, args=(conn,), daemon=True).start()


def tcp_conn(conn):
    try:
        while True:
            data = conn.recv(2048)
            if not data:
                break
            conn.sendall(data)
    finally:
        conn.close()


def main():
    udp = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    udp.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    udp.bind((HOST, PORT))
    listening = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    listening.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    listening.bind((HOST, PORT))
    listening.listen(4)
    threading.Thread(target=udp_loop, args=(udp,), daemon=True).start()
    threading.Thread(target=tcp_loop, args=(listening,), daemon=True).start()
    with open(READY, "w") as f:
        f.write("ready\n")
    sys.stdout.write("net-echo: listening on %s:%d (udp+tcp)\n" % (HOST, PORT))
    sys.stdout.flush()
    threading.Event().wait()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/net-echo.py

FILE: tools/run-qemu.sh

SHA-256: a83c0c3b15ce96faef287b793206b0fcb36b3dc6af5692105908cb3a78f91ead

#!/bin/sh
set -eu
# The firmware files differ between distributions: some ship the 2 MiB split
# pair, others only the 4 MiB build. Pick the first pair that exists.
if [ -z "${OVMF_CODE:-}" ]; then
  for c in /usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_CODE.fd /usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_CODE_4M.fd /usr/share/edk2/ovmf/OVMF_CODE.fd; do
    [ -f "$c" ] && OVMF_CODE=$c && break
  done
fi
if [ -z "${OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE:-}" ]; then
  for v in /usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_VARS.fd /usr/share/OVMF/OVMF_VARS_4M.fd /usr/share/edk2/ovmf/OVMF_VARS.fd; do
    [ -f "$v" ] && OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE=$v && break
  done
fi
: "${OVMF_CODE:?no OVMF firmware code file found}"
: "${OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE:?no OVMF firmware variable template found}"
export OVMF_CODE OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE
QEMU=${QEMU:-qemu-system-x86_64}
test -f build/efi/EFI/BOOT/BOOTX64.EFI || ./tools/build-uefi.sh
test -f "$OVMF_CODE"
test -f "$OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE"
cp "$OVMF_VARS_TEMPLATE" build/OVMF_VARS.fd
exec "$QEMU" -machine q35,accel=tcg -cpu max -m 256M -smp 1 \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,readonly=on,file="$OVMF_CODE" \
  -drive if=pflash,format=raw,file=build/OVMF_VARS.fd \
  -drive format=raw,file=fat:rw:build/efi \
  -device virtio-net-pci,netdev=net0 \
  -netdev user,id=net0 \
  -device isa-debug-exit,iobase=0xf4,iosize=0x04 \
  -serial stdio -display none -no-reboot -no-shutdown
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
END FILE: tools/run-qemu.sh
